fn main() -> iced::Result {
    iced::application(OpenTerm::load, OpenTerm::update, OpenTerm::view)
        .title("OpenTerm")
        .theme(app_theme)
        .subscription(OpenTerm::subscription)
        .window_size([1180.0, 760.0])
        .centered()
        .run()
}

fn app_theme(app: &OpenTerm) -> Theme {
    app.theme_mode.theme()
}

const SURFACE_0: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.032, 0.037, 0.043);
const SURFACE_1: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.047, 0.054, 0.062);
const SURFACE_2: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.066, 0.076, 0.087);
const TEXT_HIGH: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.905, 0.920, 0.918);
const TEXT_MUTED: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.590, 0.615, 0.620);
const TEXT_DIM: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.390, 0.415, 0.420);
const ACCENT: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.330, 0.655, 0.595);
const ACCENT_STRONG: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.475, 0.820, 0.745);
const DANGER: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.890, 0.310, 0.345);
const WARNING: Color = Color::from_rgb(0.875, 0.660, 0.260);
const BORDER_SUBTLE: Color = Color::from_rgba(0.780, 0.850, 0.840, 0.070);
const BORDER_STRONG: Color = Color::from_rgba(0.780, 0.900, 0.870, 0.160);
const TERMINAL_SEARCH_INPUT_ID: &str = "terminal-search-input";
const TERMINAL_SCROLL_ID: &str = "terminal-scroll";

const FILE_SIZE_COLUMN_WIDTH: f32 = 82.0;
const FILE_NAME_DISPLAY_CHARS: usize = 46;

    &["Upload", "Download", "Open", "Mkdir", "Rename", "Remove"];

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum ButtonTone {
    Primary,
    Secondary,
    Ghost,
    Danger,
    Active,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum TabTone {
    Active,
    Inactive,
    Add,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum PanelTone {
    App,
    Sidebar,
    Toolbar,
    Panel,
    Section,
    Inset,
    Terminal,
    TerminalCanvas,
    Alert,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum ListRowTone {
    Normal,
    Selected,
}

fn button_tone<'a>(
    content: impl Into<Element<'a, Message>>,
    tone: ButtonTone,
) -> iced::widget::Button<'a, Message> {
    button(content)
        .padding([7, 10])
        .style(move |_theme: &Theme, status| button_style(tone, status))
}

fn tab_button<'a>(
    content: impl Into<Element<'a, Message>>,
    tone: TabTone,
) -> iced::widget::Button<'a, Message> {
    button(content)
        .padding([6, 11])
        .style(move |_theme: &Theme, status| tab_button_style(tone, status))
}

fn text_input_tone<'a>(
    placeholder: &'a str,
    value: &'a str,
    on_input: impl Fn(String) -> Message + 'a,
) -> TextInput<'a, Message> {
    text_input(placeholder, value)
        .on_input(on_input)
        .padding([8, 10])
        .size(14)
        .style(input_style)
}

fn panel<'a>(
    content: impl Into<Element<'a, Message>>,
    tone: PanelTone,
) -> iced::widget::Container<'a, Message> {
    container(content).style(move |_theme: &Theme| panel_style(tone))
}

fn app_scrollable<'a>(
    content: impl Into<Element<'a, Message>>,
) -> iced::widget::Scrollable<'a, Message> {
    scrollable(content).style(scrollable_style)
}

fn terminal_scrollable<'a>(
    content: impl Into<Element<'a, Message>>,
) -> iced::widget::Scrollable<'a, Message> {
    scrollable(content)
        .id(TERMINAL_SCROLL_ID)
        .width(Fill)
        .height(Fill)
        .style(terminal_scrollable_style)
}

fn button_style(tone: ButtonTone, status: button::Status) -> button::Style {
    let (background, text_color, border_color) = match tone {
        ButtonTone::Primary => (ACCENT, SURFACE_0, ACCENT),
        ButtonTone::Secondary => (
            Color::from_rgba(0.820, 0.870, 0.850, 0.060),
            TEXT_HIGH,
            BORDER_SUBTLE,
        ),
        ButtonTone::Ghost => (Color::TRANSPARENT, TEXT_MUTED, BORDER_SUBTLE),
        ButtonTone::Danger => (Color::from_rgba(0.890, 0.310, 0.345, 0.130), DANGER, DANGER),
        ButtonTone::Active => (
            Color::from_rgba(0.360, 0.705, 0.665, 0.150),
            ACCENT_STRONG,
            ACCENT,
        ),
    };
    let hovered = matches!(status, button::Status::Hovered | button::Status::Pressed);
    let disabled = matches!(status, button::Status::Disabled);
    let pressed = matches!(status, button::Status::Pressed);
    let adjusted_background = if disabled {
        SURFACE_1
    } else if pressed {
        darken(background, 0.080)
    } else if hovered {
        lighten(background, 0.055)
    } else {
        background
    };

    button::Style {
        background: Some(Background::Color(adjusted_background)),
        text_color: if disabled { TEXT_DIM } else { text_color },
        border: Border {
            radius: 4.0.into(),
            width: if matches!(
                tone,
                ButtonTone::Primary | ButtonTone::Danger | ButtonTone::Active
            ) {
                1.0
            } else {
                0.0
            },
            color: if matches!(tone, ButtonTone::Ghost) && !hovered {
                Color::TRANSPARENT
            } else if hovered && !disabled {
                BORDER_STRONG
            } else {
                border_color
            },
        },
        shadow: if hovered && !disabled && matches!(tone, ButtonTone::Primary) {
            Shadow::default()
        } else {
            Shadow::default()
        },
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn tab_button_style(tone: TabTone, status: button::Status) -> button::Style {
    let hovered = matches!(status, button::Status::Hovered | button::Status::Pressed);
    let disabled = matches!(status, button::Status::Disabled);
    let pressed = matches!(status, button::Status::Pressed);
    let base = match tone {
        TabTone::Active => Color::from_rgb(0.017, 0.020, 0.024),
        TabTone::Inactive | TabTone::Add => Color::TRANSPARENT,
    };
    let background = if disabled {
        SURFACE_1
    } else if pressed {
        darken(base, 0.060)
    } else if hovered {
        match tone {
            TabTone::Active => lighten(base, 0.030),
            TabTone::Inactive | TabTone::Add => Color::from_rgba(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.036),
        }
    } else {
        base
    };
    let text_color = match tone {
        TabTone::Active => TEXT_HIGH,
        TabTone::Inactive => TEXT_MUTED,
        TabTone::Add => TEXT_DIM,
    };

    button::Style {
        background: Some(Background::Color(background)),
        text_color: if disabled { TEXT_DIM } else { text_color },
        border: Border {
            radius: 4.0.into(),
            width: 0.0,
            color: if matches!(tone, TabTone::Active) {
                BORDER_SUBTLE
            } else if hovered {
                BORDER_STRONG
            } else {
                Color::TRANSPARENT
            },
        },
        shadow: Shadow::default(),
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn input_style(_theme: &Theme, status: text_input::Status) -> text_input::Style {
    let focused = matches!(status, text_input::Status::Focused { .. });
    let hovered = matches!(
        status,
        text_input::Status::Hovered | text_input::Status::Focused { is_hovered: true }
    );

    text_input::Style {
        background: Background::Color(if focused {
            Color::from_rgb(0.055, 0.064, 0.074)
        } else {
            Color::from_rgb(0.039, 0.046, 0.053)
        }),
        border: Border {
            radius: 4.0.into(),
            width: 1.0,
            color: if focused {
                ACCENT
            } else if hovered {
                BORDER_STRONG
            } else {
                BORDER_SUBTLE
            },
        },
        icon: TEXT_MUTED,
        placeholder: TEXT_DIM,
        value: TEXT_HIGH,
        selection: ACCENT,
    }
}

fn panel_style(tone: PanelTone) -> container::Style {
    let (background, border, text_color) = match tone {
        PanelTone::App => (SURFACE_0, Color::TRANSPARENT, TEXT_HIGH),
        PanelTone::Sidebar => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.039, 0.045, 0.052),
            BORDER_SUBTLE,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::Toolbar => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.042, 0.048, 0.056),
            BORDER_SUBTLE,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::Panel => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.047, 0.053, 0.061),
            BORDER_SUBTLE,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::Section => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.055, 0.063, 0.073),
            BORDER_SUBTLE,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::Inset => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.030, 0.035, 0.040),
            BORDER_SUBTLE,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::Terminal => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.018, 0.021, 0.025),
            BORDER_STRONG,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::TerminalCanvas => (
            Color::from_rgb(0.006, 0.008, 0.010),
            Color::TRANSPARENT,
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
        PanelTone::Alert => (
            Color::from_rgba(0.875, 0.660, 0.260, 0.100),
            Color::from_rgba(0.875, 0.660, 0.260, 0.420),
            TEXT_HIGH,
        ),
    };

    container::Style {
        text_color: Some(text_color),
        background: Some(Background::Color(background)),
        border: Border {
            radius: if matches!(
                tone,
                PanelTone::App | PanelTone::TerminalCanvas | PanelTone::Toolbar
            ) {
                0.0.into()
            } else {
                4.0.into()
            },
            width: if matches!(
                tone,
                PanelTone::App | PanelTone::TerminalCanvas | PanelTone::Panel
            ) {
                0.0
            } else if matches!(tone, PanelTone::Toolbar) {
                1.0
            } else {
                1.0
            },
            color: border,
        },
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn scrollable_style(_theme: &Theme, status: scrollable::Status) -> scrollable::Style {
    let hovered = matches!(
        status,
        scrollable::Status::Hovered { .. } | scrollable::Status::Dragged { .. }
    );
    let rail = scrollable::Rail {
        background: Some(Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
        border: Border {
            radius: 4.0.into(),
            width: 0.0,
            color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
        },
        scroller: scrollable::Scroller {
            background: Background::Color(if hovered {
                Color::from_rgba(0.800, 0.870, 0.860, 0.220)
            } else {
                Color::from_rgba(0.800, 0.870, 0.860, 0.130)
            }),
            border: Border {
                radius: 4.0.into(),
                width: 0.0,
                color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
            },
        },
    };

    scrollable::Style {
        container: container::Style::default(),
        vertical_rail: rail,
        horizontal_rail: rail,
        gap: Some(Background::Color(Color::TRANSPARENT)),
        auto_scroll: scrollable::AutoScroll {
            background: Background::Color(SURFACE_2),
            border: Border {
                radius: 4.0.into(),
                width: 1.0,
                color: BORDER_SUBTLE,
            },
            shadow: Shadow::default(),
            icon: TEXT_MUTED,
        },
    }
}

fn terminal_scrollable_style(_theme: &Theme, status: scrollable::Status) -> scrollable::Style {
    let hovered = matches!(
        status,
        scrollable::Status::Hovered { .. } | scrollable::Status::Dragged { .. }
    );
    let rail = scrollable::Rail {
        background: Some(Background::Color(Color::from_rgba(
            0.890, 0.925, 0.930, 0.120,
        ))),
        border: Border {
            radius: 4.0.into(),
            width: 0.0,
            color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
        },
        scroller: scrollable::Scroller {
            background: Background::Color(if hovered { ACCENT_STRONG } else { TEXT_MUTED }),
            border: Border {
                radius: 4.0.into(),
                width: 0.0,
                color: Color::TRANSPARENT,
            },
        },
    };

    scrollable::Style {
        container: container::Style::default(),
        vertical_rail: rail,
        horizontal_rail: rail,
        gap: Some(Background::Color(SURFACE_1)),
        auto_scroll: scrollable::AutoScroll {
            background: Background::Color(SURFACE_2),
            border: Border {
                radius: 4.0.into(),
                width: 1.0,
                color: BORDER_STRONG,
            },
            shadow: Shadow::default(),
            icon: TEXT_HIGH,
        },
    }
}

fn lighten(color: Color, amount: f32) -> Color {
    Color {
        r: (color.r + amount).min(1.0),
        g: (color.g + amount).min(1.0),
        b: (color.b + amount).min(1.0),
        a: color.a,
    }
}

fn darken(color: Color, amount: f32) -> Color {
    Color {
        r: (color.r - amount).max(0.0),
        g: (color.g - amount).max(0.0),
        b: (color.b - amount).max(0.0),
        a: color.a,
    }
}

fn status_chip<'a>(
    label: &'static str,
    value: impl Into<String>,
    color: Color,
) -> Element<'a, Message> {
    panel(
        row![
            text(label).size(12).color(TEXT_DIM),
            text(value.into()).size(12).color(color)
        ]
        .spacing(5)
        .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
        PanelTone::Section,
    )
    .padding([6, 8])
    .into()
}

fn quiet_status_text<'a>(value: impl Into<String>) -> Element<'a, Message> {
    text(value.into()).size(12).color(TEXT_DIM).into()
}

fn terminal_session_status_text(status: &str) -> String {
    let trimmed = status.trim();
    if trimmed.starts_with("Terminal window size set")
        || trimmed.starts_with("Terminal auto-resized")
        || trimmed.starts_with("Terminal focused")
        || trimmed.starts_with("Terminal row ")
    {
        String::new()
    } else {
        trimmed.to_string()
    }
}

fn list_row_style(tone: ListRowTone, status: button::Status) -> button::Style {
    let hovered = matches!(status, button::Status::Hovered | button::Status::Pressed);
    let selected = matches!(tone, ListRowTone::Selected);
    let background = if selected {
        Color::from_rgba(0.330, 0.655, 0.595, 0.125)
    } else if hovered {
        Color::from_rgba(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.032)
    } else {
        Color::TRANSPARENT
    };

    button::Style {
        background: Some(Background::Color(background)),
        text_color: if selected { TEXT_HIGH } else { TEXT_MUTED },
        border: Border {
            radius: 4.0.into(),
            width: if selected { 1.0 } else { 0.0 },
            color: if selected {
                Color::from_rgba(0.475, 0.820, 0.745, 0.420)
            } else {
                Color::TRANSPARENT
            },
        },
        shadow: Shadow::default(),
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn combined_activity_log(session: &str, transfers: &str, forwards: &str) -> String {
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    if !session.trim().is_empty() {
        lines.push("[session]".to_string());
        lines.push(session.to_string());
    }
    if !transfers.trim().is_empty() {
        if !lines.is_empty() {
            lines.push(String::new());
        }
        lines.push("[transfers]".to_string());
        lines.push(transfers.to_string());
    }
    if !forwards.trim().is_empty() {
        if !lines.is_empty() {
            lines.push(String::new());
        }
        lines.push("[forwards]".to_string());
        lines.push(forwards.to_string());
    }

    if lines.is_empty() {
        "No events yet".to_string()
    } else {
        lines.join("\n")
    }
}

fn shell_status_color(shell: &ShellState) -> Color {
    match shell {
        ShellState::Disconnected => TEXT_MUTED,
        ShellState::Connecting { .. } => WARNING,
        ShellState::Connected { .. } => ACCENT_STRONG,
    }
}

fn forward_status_color(forward: &ForwardState) -> Color {
    match forward {
        ForwardState::Stopped => TEXT_MUTED,
        ForwardState::Starting { .. } => WARNING,
        ForwardState::Running { .. } => ACCENT_STRONG,
    }
}

    RefreshFiles {
        route: ConnectRoute,
        remote_path: String,

        route: ConnectRoute,

        local_path: PathBuf,

        route: ConnectRoute,

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
enum SshUiError {
    HostKeyVerificationRequired(Box<HostKeyChallenge>),
    Message(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct SshIssue {
    title: String,
    detail: String,
    guidance: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct LocalFileEntry {
    name: String,
    path: PathBuf,
    kind: LocalFileKind,
    size: Option<u64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum LocalFileKind {
    Directory,
    File,
    Symlink,
    Other,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for SshUiError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            SshUiError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(challenge) => write!(
                f,
                "Host key verification required for {}:{}",
                challenge.host, challenge.port
            ),
            SshUiError::Message(message) => write!(f, "{message}"),
        }
    }
}

impl From<SshError> for SshUiError {
    fn from(error: SshError) -> Self {
        match error {
            SshError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(challenge) => {
                SshUiError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(challenge)
            }
            error => SshUiError::Message(error.to_string()),
        }
    }
}

fn is_closed_channel_send_error(error: &SshUiError) -> bool {
    match error {
        SshUiError::Message(message) => message.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("channel send error"),
        SshUiError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(_) => false,
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum TerminalViewMode {
    Screen,
    History,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum AppThemeMode {
    Dark,
    Light,
}

impl AppThemeMode {
    const ALL: [AppThemeMode; 2] = [AppThemeMode::Dark, AppThemeMode::Light];

    fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            AppThemeMode::Dark => "Dark",
            AppThemeMode::Light => "Light",
        }
    }

    fn theme(self) -> Theme {
        match self {
            AppThemeMode::Dark => Theme::CatppuccinMocha,
            AppThemeMode::Light => Theme::CatppuccinLatte,
        }
    }

    fn storage_value(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            AppThemeMode::Dark => "dark",
            AppThemeMode::Light => "light",
        }
    }

    fn from_storage(value: &str) -> Self {
        match value {
            "light" => AppThemeMode::Light,
            _ => AppThemeMode::Dark,
        }
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Display for AppThemeMode {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.label())
    }
}

    History,

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum ForwardMode {
    Local,
    Dynamic,
    Remote,
}

impl ForwardMode {
    fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            ForwardMode::Local => "local",
            ForwardMode::Dynamic => "dynamic SOCKS",
            ForwardMode::Remote => "remote",
        }
    }
}

impl ForwardState {
    fn label(&self, mode: ForwardMode) -> String {
        match self {
            ForwardState::Stopped => format!("{} stopped", mode.label()),
            ForwardState::Starting { forward, .. } => {
                format!("{} starting", forward.mode_label())
            }
            ForwardState::Running { .. } => format!("{} running", mode.label()),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
enum ForwardState {
    Stopped,
    Starting {
        route: ConnectRoute,
        forward: ForwardConfig,
    },
    Running {
        stop: iced_mpsc::Sender<ForwardInput>,
    },
}

        match self {
            ForwardConfig::Local(_) => "local",
            ForwardConfig::Dynamic(_) => "dynamic SOCKS",
            ForwardConfig::Remote(_) => "remote",
        }
    }
}

    Stopped,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum JumpHostChoice {
    None,
    Host { id: HostId, label: String },
}

impl JumpHostChoice {
    fn id(&self) -> Option<HostId> {
        match self {
            JumpHostChoice::None => None,
            JumpHostChoice::Host { id, .. } => Some(*id),
        }
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Display for JumpHostChoice {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            JumpHostChoice::None => write!(f, "No jump host"),
            JumpHostChoice::Host { label, .. } => write!(f, "{label}"),
        }
    }
}

            ShellState::Connected { .. } => "connected",

    }

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct DiagnosticReport {
    target: String,
    steps: Vec<DiagnosticStep>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct DiagnosticStep {
    name: &'static str,
    ok: bool,
    detail: String,
}

impl DiagnosticReport {
    fn status_line(&self) -> String {
        let passed = self.steps.iter().filter(|step| step.ok).count();
        format!(
            "Diagnostics finished for {}: {passed}/{} checks passed.",
            self.target,
            self.steps.len()
        )
    }

    fn render(&self) -> String {
        let mut output = format!("Diagnostics for {}\n", self.target);
        for step in &self.steps {
            output.push_str(if step.ok { "[ok] " } else { "[fail] " });
            output.push_str(step.name);
            output.push_str(" - ");
            output.push_str(&step.detail);
            output.push('\n');
        }
        output
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct TerminalTab {
    title: String,
    selected_host_id: Option<HostId>,
    host_name: String,
    host: String,
    user: String,
    port: String,
    jump_host: JumpHostChoice,
    auth_mode: AuthMode,
    password: String,
    key_path: String,
    passphrase: String,
    terminal_search: String,
    terminal_view_mode: TerminalViewMode,
    terminal_context_menu_open: bool,
    auto_reconnect: bool,
    follow_output: bool,
    capture_keys: bool,
    manual_disconnect_requested: bool,
    hovered_terminal_row: Option<usize>,
    terminal_selection_anchor: Option<usize>,
    selected_terminal_rows: Option<(usize, usize)>,
    hovered_terminal_cell: Option<TerminalCellPoint>,
    terminal_cell_selection_anchor: Option<TerminalCellPoint>,
    selected_terminal_cells: Option<TerminalSelection>,
    shell_output: String,
    shell_log: String,
    terminal_size: EngineTerminalSize,
    shell: ShellState,
}

impl TerminalTab {
    fn new(index: usize, user: String, size: EngineTerminalSize) -> Self {
        Self {
            title: format!("Session {index}"),
            selected_host_id: None,
            host_name: String::new(),
            host: String::new(),
            user,
            port: "22".to_string(),
            jump_host: JumpHostChoice::None,
            auth_mode: AuthMode::AgentOrDefault,
            password: String::new(),
            key_path: default_key_path(),
            passphrase: String::new(),
            terminal_search: String::new(),
            terminal_view_mode: TerminalViewMode::Screen,
            terminal_context_menu_open: false,
            auto_reconnect: false,
            follow_output: true,
            capture_keys: false,
            manual_disconnect_requested: false,
            hovered_terminal_row: None,
            terminal_selection_anchor: None,
            selected_terminal_rows: None,
            hovered_terminal_cell: None,
            terminal_cell_selection_anchor: None,
            selected_terminal_cells: None,
            shell_output: String::new(),
            shell_log: String::new(),
            terminal_size: size,
            shell: ShellState::Disconnected,
        }
    }

    fn clone_for_duplicate(&self, index: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            title: terminal_tab_title(&self.host_name, &self.host, index),
            selected_host_id: self.selected_host_id,
            host_name: self.host_name.clone(),
            host: self.host.clone(),
            user: self.user.clone(),
            port: self.port.clone(),
            jump_host: self.jump_host.clone(),
            auth_mode: self.auth_mode,
            password: self.password.clone(),
            key_path: self.key_path.clone(),
            passphrase: self.passphrase.clone(),
            terminal_search: self.terminal_search.clone(),
            terminal_view_mode: self.terminal_view_mode,
            terminal_context_menu_open: false,
            auto_reconnect: self.auto_reconnect,
            follow_output: self.follow_output,
            capture_keys: self.capture_keys,
            manual_disconnect_requested: false,
            hovered_terminal_row: None,
            terminal_selection_anchor: None,
            selected_terminal_rows: None,
            hovered_terminal_cell: None,
            terminal_cell_selection_anchor: None,
            selected_terminal_cells: None,
            shell_output: String::new(),
            shell_log: String::new(),
            terminal_size: self.terminal_size,
            shell: ShellState::Disconnected,
        }
    }
}

impl OpenTerm {
    fn load() -> (Self, Task<Message>) {
        let db_path = openterm_ui::default_db_path();
        let (hosts, ui_settings) = WorkspaceStore::open(&db_path)
            .map(|store| {
                let hosts = store.list_hosts().unwrap_or_default();
                let settings = store.get_ui_settings().ok().flatten().unwrap_or_default();
                (hosts, settings)
            })
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let default_user = std::env::var("USER").unwrap_or_default();
        let default_size = EngineTerminalSize {
            cols: 100,
            rows: 24,
        };
        let terminal_tabs = vec![TerminalTab::new(1, default_user.clone(), default_size)];
        let smoke_status_path = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_STATUS").map(PathBuf::from);
        let smoke_set_theme = std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_SET_THEME").ok();
        let smoke_set_font_size = std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_SET_FONT_SIZE").ok();
        let smoke_save_host = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_SAVE_HOST").is_some();
        let smoke_auto_connect = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_AUTO_CONNECT").is_some();
        let smoke_duplicate_connect =
            std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_DUPLICATE_CONNECT").is_some();
        let smoke_auto_sftp = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_AUTO_SFTP").is_some();
        let smoke_sftp_roundtrip = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_SFTP_ROUNDTRIP").is_some();
        let smoke_auto_forward = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_AUTO_FORWARD").is_some();
        let smoke_auto_trust_host_key =
            std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_AUTO_TRUST_HOST_KEY").is_some();
        let smoke_input_marker = std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_INPUT_MARKER").ok();
        let smoke_disconnect_reconnect =
            std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_DISCONNECT_RECONNECT").is_some();
        let smoke_overflow_probe = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_OVERFLOW_PROBE").is_some();

        let mut app = Self {
            db_path,
            hosts,
            selected: None,
            host_search: String::new(),
            command_palette_open: false,
            command_palette_query: String::new(),
            pending_delete_host: false,
            sidebar_tools_open: false,
            advanced_connection_open: false,
            vault_details_open: false,
            routing_details_open: false,
            pty_details_open: false,
            panel: Panel::Terminal,
            host_name: String::new(),
            host: String::new(),
            user: default_user,
            port: "22".to_string(),
            jump_host: JumpHostChoice::None,
            import_path: default_ssh_config_path(),
            export_path: default_export_path(),
            auth_mode: AuthMode::AgentOrDefault,
            password: String::new(),
            vault_password: String::new(),
            vault_unlocked: false,
            session_passwords: HashMap::new(),
            key_path: default_key_path(),
            passphrase: String::new(),
            terminal_cols: "100".to_string(),
            terminal_rows: "30".to_string(),
            command: "hostname && whoami && uname -s".to_string(),
            remote_path: ".".to_string(),
            local_path: default_local_sftp_path(),
            new_name: String::new(),
            local_files: Vec::new(),
            selected_local_file: None,
            remote_files: Vec::new(),
            selected_remote_file: None,
            pending_delete_remote: None,
            active_transfer: None,
            transfer_log: String::new(),
            bind_host: "127.0.0.1".to_string(),
            bind_port: "8080".to_string(),
            forward_mode: ForwardMode::Local,
            forward_remote_host: "127.0.0.1".to_string(),
            forward_remote_port: "80".to_string(),
            forward_log: String::new(),
            forward_tools_open: false,
            forward: ForwardState::Stopped,
            terminal_search: String::new(),
            terminal_view_mode: TerminalViewMode::Screen,
            theme_mode: AppThemeMode::from_storage(&ui_settings.theme_mode),
            terminal_font_size: sanitize_terminal_font_size(
                &ui_settings.terminal_font_size.to_string(),
            ),
            terminal_context_menu_open: false,
            auto_reconnect: false,
            follow_output: true,
            capture_keys: false,
            manual_disconnect_requested: false,
            hovered_terminal_row: None,
            terminal_selection_anchor: None,
            selected_terminal_rows: None,
            hovered_terminal_cell: None,
            terminal_cell_selection_anchor: None,
            selected_terminal_cells: None,
            workspace_tools_open: false,
            shell_output: String::new(),
            shell_log: String::new(),
            terminal: AlacrittyTerminalBuffer::new(default_size),
            output: String::new(),
            status: "Offline ready. Add a host or select one from the local workspace.".to_string(),
            activity_log: String::new(),
            issue: None,
            last_ok: None,
            running: false,
            testing_connection: false,
            running_diagnostics: false,
            diagnostics_output: String::new(),
            shell: ShellState::Disconnected,
            terminal_tabs,
            active_terminal_tab: 0,
            pending_host_key: None,
            pending_ssh_action: None,
            smoke_status_path,
            smoke_save_host,
            smoke_auto_connect,
            smoke_duplicate_connect,
            smoke_auto_sftp,
            smoke_sftp_roundtrip,
            smoke_auto_forward,
            smoke_auto_trust_host_key,
            smoke_input_marker,
            smoke_input_probe_sent: false,
            smoke_disconnect_reconnect,
            smoke_disconnect_requested: false,
            smoke_reconnect_requested: false,
            smoke_overflow_probe,
            smoke_overflow_probe_sent: false,
            smoke_resize_probe_sent: false,
            smoke_resize_probe_done: false,
        };
        if app.hosts.len() == 1 && app.host.trim().is_empty() {
            app.select_host(0);
            app.record_smoke_status("single_host_selected");
        }
        app.refresh_local_files();
        if std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_PREFILL_TEST_SERVER").is_some() {
            app.fill_test_server();
        }
        if let Some(theme) = smoke_set_theme {
            app.theme_mode = AppThemeMode::from_storage(&theme);
            app.persist_ui_settings();
            app.record_smoke_status("smoke_theme_set");
        }
        if let Some(font_size) = smoke_set_font_size {
            app.terminal_font_size = sanitize_terminal_font_size(&font_size);
            app.persist_ui_settings();
            app.record_smoke_status("smoke_font_size_set");
        }
        if std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_OPEN_SFTP").is_some_and(|value| !value.is_empty()) {
            app.panel = Panel::Sftp;
            app.record_smoke_status("smoke_open_sftp");
        }
        if let Some(name) = std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_EDIT_HOST_NAME")
            .ok()
            .filter(|name| !name.trim().is_empty())
        {
            app.host_name = name;
            app.record_smoke_status("smoke_edit_host_name");
        }
        app.record_smoke_status("loaded");
        let task = if app.smoke_save_host {
            app.record_smoke_status("save_host_requested");
            app.update(Message::SaveHost)
        } else if app.smoke_duplicate_connect {
            app.record_smoke_status("duplicate_connect_requested");
            let duplicate = app.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);
            let connect = app.update(Message::ConnectShell);
            Task::batch([duplicate, connect])
        } else if app.smoke_sftp_roundtrip {
            app.panel = Panel::Sftp;
            app.record_smoke_status("sftp_roundtrip_requested");
            app.start_smoke_sftp_roundtrip()
        } else if app.smoke_auto_sftp {
            app.panel = Panel::Sftp;
            app.record_smoke_status("auto_sftp_requested");
            app.update(Message::RefreshFiles)
        } else if app.smoke_auto_forward {
            app.configure_forward_smoke_from_values(
                std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_FORWARD_PORT").ok(),
                std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_FORWARD_REMOTE_HOST").ok(),
                std::env::var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_FORWARD_REMOTE_PORT").ok(),
            );
            app.record_smoke_status("auto_forward_requested");
            app.update(Message::StartForward)
        } else if app.smoke_auto_connect {
            app.record_smoke_status("auto_connect_requested");
            app.update(Message::ConnectShell)
        } else {
            Task::none()
        };

        (app, task)
    }

    fn configure_forward_smoke_from_values(
        &mut self,
        bind_port: Option<String>,
        remote_host: Option<String>,
        remote_port: Option<String>,
    ) {
        self.panel = Panel::Forward;
        self.workspace_tools_open = true;
        if let Some(bind_port) = bind_port {
            self.bind_port = bind_port;
        }
        if let Some(remote_host) = remote_host {
            self.forward_remote_host = remote_host;
        }
        self.forward_remote_port = remote_port.unwrap_or_else(|| "22".to_string());
    }

    fn run_palette_action(&mut self, action: PaletteAction) -> Task<Message> {
        self.command_palette_open = false;
        self.command_palette_query.clear();

        match action {
            PaletteAction::Connect => self.update(Message::ConnectShell),
            PaletteAction::NewConnection => self.update(Message::NewConnection),
            PaletteAction::DuplicateSession => self.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab),
            PaletteAction::SaveHost => self.update(Message::SaveHost),
            PaletteAction::TestConnection => self.update(Message::TestConnection),
            PaletteAction::OpenTerminal => self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Terminal)),
            PaletteAction::OpenCommand => self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Command)),
            PaletteAction::OpenSftp => self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp)),
            PaletteAction::OpenForward => self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Forward)),
            PaletteAction::OpenDiagnostics => {
                self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Diagnostics))
            }
            PaletteAction::OpenSettings => self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Settings)),
            PaletteAction::RunCommand => self.update(Message::RunCommand),
            PaletteAction::RefreshSftp => self.update(Message::RefreshFiles),
            PaletteAction::StartForward => self.update(Message::StartForward),
            PaletteAction::StopForward => self.update(Message::StopForward),
            PaletteAction::ToggleDetails => self.update(Message::ToggleAdvancedConnection),
            PaletteAction::OpenActivity => self.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Activity)),
            PaletteAction::ToggleImportExport => {
                self.sidebar_tools_open = !self.sidebar_tools_open;
                Task::none()
            }
        }
    }

    fn open_workspace_panel(&mut self, panel: Panel) -> Task<Message> {
        self.panel = panel;
        self.workspace_tools_open = !matches!(panel, Panel::Terminal | Panel::Sftp);
        if panel == Panel::Sftp && self.local_files.is_empty() {
            self.refresh_local_files();
        }
        if panel == Panel::Terminal {
            return Task::none();
        }
        self.capture_keys = false;
        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
        Task::none()
    }

    fn open_connection_editor(&mut self) {
        self.advanced_connection_open = true;
        self.capture_keys = false;
        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
    }

    fn update(&mut self, message: Message) -> Task<Message> {
        match message {
            Message::ToggleCommandPalette => {
                self.command_palette_open = !self.command_palette_open;
                if self.command_palette_open {
                    self.command_palette_query.clear();
                    self.capture_keys = false;
                    self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::CommandPaletteQueryChanged(value) => {
                self.command_palette_query = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::CommandPaletteRun(action) => self.run_palette_action(action),
            Message::PanelSelected(panel) => self.open_workspace_panel(panel),
            Message::TerminalTabSelected(index) => self.select_terminal_tab(index),
            Message::NewTerminalTab => {
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                let index = self.terminal_tabs.len() + 1;
                let size = self.terminal_size().unwrap_or(EngineTerminalSize {
                    cols: 100,
                    rows: 30,
                });
                let mut tab = TerminalTab::new(index, self.user.clone(), size);
                if !self.host.trim().is_empty() {
                    tab.title = terminal_tab_title(&self.host_name, &self.host, index - 1);
                    tab.selected_host_id = self
                        .selected
                        .and_then(|host_index| self.hosts.get(host_index).map(|host| host.id));
                    tab.host_name = self.host_name.clone();
                    tab.host = self.host.clone();
                    tab.user = self.user.clone();
                    tab.port = self.port.clone();
                    tab.jump_host = self.jump_host.clone();
                    tab.auth_mode = self.auth_mode;
                    tab.password = self.password.clone();
                    tab.key_path = self.key_path.clone();
                    tab.passphrase = self.passphrase.clone();
                    tab.auto_reconnect = self.auto_reconnect;
                }
                self.terminal_tabs.push(tab);
                self.restore_terminal_tab(self.terminal_tabs.len() - 1);
                self.status = format!("Created terminal tab {index}.");
                self.append_activity("terminal", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::DuplicateTerminalTab => self.duplicate_terminal_tab(),
            Message::CloseTerminalTab(index) => {
                let index = if index == usize::MAX {
                    self.active_terminal_tab
                } else {
                    index
                };
                self.close_terminal_tab(index)
            }
            Message::NewConnection => {
                self.start_new_connection();
                self.advanced_connection_open = true;
                self.capture_keys = false;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::HostSelected(index) => {
                self.select_host(index);
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::HostSearchChanged(value) => {
                self.host_search = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::HostNameChanged(value) => {
                self.host_name = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::HostChanged(value) => {
                self.host = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::UserChanged(value) => {
                self.user = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::PortChanged(value) => {
                self.port = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::OpenConnectionEditor => {
                self.open_connection_editor();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleAdvancedConnection => {
                self.advanced_connection_open = !self.advanced_connection_open;
                if self.advanced_connection_open {
                    self.capture_keys = false;
                    self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleVaultDetails => {
                self.vault_details_open = !self.vault_details_open;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleRoutingDetails => {
                self.routing_details_open = !self.routing_details_open;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::JumpHostSelected(choice) => {
                self.jump_host = choice;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ImportPathChanged(value) => {
                self.import_path = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ExportPathChanged(value) => {
                self.export_path = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ImportSshConfig => {
                let path = self.import_path.trim().to_string();
                if path.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "OpenSSH config path is required.".to_string();
                    return Task::none();
                }
                let db_path = self.db_path.clone();
                self.status = format!("Importing OpenSSH config from {path}");
                self.append_activity("config", &self.status.clone());
                Task::perform(
                    async move { import_ssh_config_file(db_path, PathBuf::from(path)).await },
                    Message::ImportFinished,
                )
            }
            Message::ImportFinished(result) => {
                match result {
                    Ok(summary) => {
                        self.hosts = summary.hosts;
                        self.selected = None;
                        self.pending_delete_host = false;
                        self.jump_host = JumpHostChoice::None;
                        self.status = format!(
                            "Imported {} hosts. Skipped {} unsupported entries.",
                            summary.imported, summary.skipped
                        );
                        self.append_activity("config", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                    Err(error) => {
                        self.status = format!("Import failed: {error}");
                        self.append_activity("config", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ExportHosts => {
                let path = self.export_path.trim().to_string();
                if path.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "Export path is required.".to_string();
                    return Task::none();
                }
                let hosts = self.hosts.clone();
                self.status = format!("Exporting {} hosts to {path}", hosts.len());
                self.append_activity("config", &self.status.clone());
                Task::perform(
                    async move { export_hosts_file(hosts, PathBuf::from(path)).await },
                    Message::ExportFinished,
                )
            }
            Message::ExportFinished(result) => {
                match result {
                    Ok(message) => {
                        self.status = message;
                        self.append_activity("config", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                    Err(error) => {
                        self.status = format!("Export failed: {error}");
                        self.append_activity("config", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::AuthModeChanged(mode) => {
                self.auth_mode = mode;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::PasswordChanged(value) => {
                self.password = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::VaultPasswordChanged(value) => {
                self.vault_password = value;
                self.vault_unlocked = false;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::UnlockVault => self.unlock_vault(),
            Message::LockVault => {
                self.vault_unlocked = false;
                self.vault_password.clear();
                self.password.clear();
                self.passphrase.clear();
                self.status = "Vault locked and in-memory secrets cleared.".to_string();
                self.append_activity("vault", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::KeyPathChanged(value) => {
                self.key_path = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::PassphraseChanged(value) => {
                self.passphrase = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::CommandChanged(value) => {
                self.command = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::RemotePathChanged(value) => {
                self.remote_path = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::LocalPathChanged(value) => {
                self.local_path = value;
                self.selected_local_file = None;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::NewNameChanged(value) => {
                self.new_name = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::LocalFileSelected(index) => {
                self.selected_local_file = Some(index);
                if let Some(entry) = self.local_files.get(index) {
                    self.local_path = entry.path.display().to_string();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::RefreshLocalFiles => {
                self.refresh_local_files();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::OpenSelectedLocalDirectory => {
                self.open_selected_local_directory();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::GoLocalParentDirectory => {
                self.go_local_parent_directory();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::RemoteFileSelected(index) => {
                self.selected_remote_file = Some(index);
                if let Some(entry) = self.remote_files.get(index) {
                    self.new_name = entry.name.clone();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::GoHomeDirectory => {
                self.pending_delete_remote = None;
                self.remote_path = ".".to_string();
                self.refresh_files()
            }
            Message::GoRootDirectory => {
                self.pending_delete_remote = None;
                self.remote_path = "/".to_string();
                self.refresh_files()
            }
            Message::SaveHost => {
                match self.host_profile_for_primary_save() {
                    Ok(profile) => self.save_host_profile(profile, "Saved host settings."),
                    Err(error) => {
                        return self.handle_local_validation_error("Save host", error);
                    }
                };
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::SaveHostSecretAndConnect => match self.host_profile_for_primary_save() {
                Ok(profile) => {
                    if self.save_host_profile(profile, "Saved host settings.") {
                        self.update(Message::ConnectShell)
                    } else {
                        Task::none()
                    }
                }
                Err(error) => self.handle_local_validation_error("Save secret", error),
            },
            Message::RequestDeleteHost => {
                if self.selected.is_none() {
                    self.status = "Select a saved host first.".to_string();
                } else {
                    self.pending_delete_host = true;
                    self.status = "Confirm saved host delete.".to_string();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::CancelDeleteHost => {
                self.pending_delete_host = false;
                self.status = "Saved host delete cancelled.".to_string();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ConfirmDeleteHost => {
                let Some(index) = self.selected else {
                    self.pending_delete_host = false;
                    self.status = "Select a saved host first.".to_string();
                    return Task::none();
                };
                let Some(host) = self.hosts.get(index).cloned() else {
                    self.pending_delete_host = false;
                    self.selected = None;
                    self.status = "Selected host is no longer available.".to_string();
                    return Task::none();
                };

                match WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path).and_then(|store| {
                    store.delete_host(host.id)?;
                    store.list_hosts()
                }) {
                    Ok(hosts) => {
                        self.hosts = hosts;
                        self.selected = None;
                        self.pending_delete_host = false;
                        self.status = format!("Deleted saved host {}", host.name);
                        self.append_activity("host", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                    Err(error) => {
                        self.status = format!("Delete host failed: {error}");
                        self.append_activity("host", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::TestConnection => {
                if self.testing_connection {
                    return Task::none();
                }
                let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
                    Ok(parts) => parts,
                    Err(error) => {
                        return self.handle_local_validation_error("Test connection", error);
                    }
                };
                let target = route.target.display_target();

                self.testing_connection = true;
                self.status = format!("Testing SSH connection to {target}");
                self.append_activity("ssh", &self.status.clone());
                self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::TestConnection(route))
            }
            Message::ConnectionTestFinished(result) => {
                self.testing_connection = false;
                let task = match result {
                    Ok(message) => {
                        self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                        self.issue = None;
                        self.mark_connection_ok("test connection");
                        self.status = message;
                        self.append_activity("ssh", &self.status.clone());
                        Task::none()
                    }
                    Err(error) => self.handle_ssh_error(error, "Connection test failed"),
                };
                task
            }
            Message::RunDiagnostics => {
                if self.running_diagnostics {
                    return Task::none();
                }
                let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
                    Ok(parts) => parts,
                    Err(error) => {
                        return self.handle_local_validation_error("Diagnostics", error);
                    }
                };
                let target = route.target.display_target();
                self.running_diagnostics = true;
                self.diagnostics_output = format!("diagnostics started for {target}");
                self.status = format!("Running diagnostics for {target}");
                self.append_activity("diagnostics", &self.status.clone());
                self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::RunDiagnostics(route))
            }
            Message::DiagnosticsFinished(result) => {
                self.running_diagnostics = false;
                let task = match result {
                    Ok(report) => {
                        self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                        self.issue = None;
                        self.mark_connection_ok("diagnostics");
                        self.status = report.status_line();
                        self.diagnostics_output = report.render();
                        self.append_activity("diagnostics", &self.status.clone());
                        Task::none()
                    }
                    Err(error) => self.handle_ssh_error(error, "Diagnostics failed"),
                };
                task
            }
            Message::RunCommand => {
                if self.running {
                    return Task::none();
                }

                if self.command.trim().is_empty() {
                    return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                        "Command",
                        "Command is required.".to_string(),
                    );
                }
                let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
                    Ok(parts) => parts,
                    Err(error) => {
                        return self.handle_local_validation_error("Command", error);
                    }
                };
                let target = route.target.display_target();

                self.running = true;
                self.status = format!("Running command on {target}");
                self.append_activity("command", &self.status.clone());
                self.output.clear();

                let command = self.command.clone();
                self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::RunCommand { route, command })
            }
            Message::CommandFinished(result) => {
                self.running = false;
                let task = match result {
                    Ok(output) => {
                        self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                        self.issue = None;
                        self.mark_ok(format!("command exit {}", output.exit_status));
                        self.status = format!("Command finished with exit {}", output.exit_status);
                        self.append_activity("command", &self.status.clone());
                        self.output = format!(
                            "$ {}\n\n{}{}",
                            self.command,
                            output.stdout,
                            if output.stderr.is_empty() {
                                String::new()
                            } else {
                                format!("\n[stderr]\n{}", output.stderr)
                            }
                        );
                        Task::none()
                    }
                    Err(error) => self.handle_ssh_error(error, "Command failed"),
                };
                task
            }
            Message::RefreshFiles => self.refresh_files(),
            Message::FilesLoaded(result) => {
                let task = match result {
                    Ok(files) => {
                        self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                        self.issue = None;
                        self.mark_ok(format!("sftp list {}", self.remote_path));
                        self.remote_files = sorted_remote_files(files);
                        self.selected_remote_file = None;
                        self.pending_delete_remote = None;
                        self.advanced_connection_open = false;
                        self.status = format!(
                            "Loaded {} remote entries from {}",
                            self.remote_files.len(),
                            self.remote_path
                        );
                        self.append_activity("sftp", &self.status.clone());
                        self.record_smoke_status("sftp_loaded");
                        Task::none()
                    }
                    Err(error) => self.handle_ssh_error(error, "SFTP list failed"),
                };
                task
            }
            Message::DownloadSelected => self.download_selected_file(),
            Message::UploadLocalFile => self.upload_local_file(),
            Message::OpenSelectedDirectory => self.open_selected_directory(),
            Message::GoParentDirectory => self.go_parent_directory(),
            Message::CreateRemoteDirectory => self.create_remote_directory(),
            Message::RequestDeleteSelectedRemote => self.request_delete_selected_remote(),
            Message::CancelDeleteRemote => {
                self.pending_delete_remote = None;
                self.status = "Remote delete cancelled.".to_string();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ConfirmDeleteRemote => self.confirm_delete_remote(),
            Message::RenameSelectedRemote => self.rename_selected_remote(),
            Message::FileTransferFinished(result) => match result {
                Ok(message) => {
                    self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                    self.issue = None;
                    self.mark_ok("sftp transfer");
                    self.active_transfer = None;
                    self.append_transfer_log("done", &message);
                    self.status = message;
                    self.append_activity("sftp", &self.status.clone());
                    if self.smoke_sftp_roundtrip && self.status.contains("SFTP roundtrip complete")
                    {
                        self.record_smoke_status("sftp_roundtrip_done");
                        return Task::none();
                    }
                    self.refresh_files()
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    let detail = error.to_string();
                    self.active_transfer = None;
                    self.append_transfer_log("failed", &detail);
                    self.append_activity("sftp", &format!("SFTP transfer failed: {detail}"));
                    self.handle_ssh_error(error, "SFTP transfer failed")
                }
            },
            Message::AcceptHostKey => self.accept_host_key(),
            Message::CancelHostKey => {
                self.pending_host_key = None;
                self.testing_connection = false;
                self.running = false;
                self.status = "Host key verification cancelled.".to_string();
                self.append_activity("host-key", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::BindHostChanged(value) => {
                self.bind_host = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::BindPortChanged(value) => {
                self.bind_port = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ForwardModeChanged(mode) => {
                if matches!(self.forward, ForwardState::Stopped) {
                    self.forward_mode = mode;
                    self.status = format!("Forward mode set to {}.", mode.label());
                    self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                } else {
                    self.status = "Stop the active forward before changing mode.".to_string();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ForwardRemoteHostChanged(value) => {
                self.forward_remote_host = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ForwardRemotePortChanged(value) => {
                self.forward_remote_port = value;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleForwardTools => {
                self.forward_tools_open = !self.forward_tools_open;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::StartForward => self.start_forward(),
            Message::StopForward => {
                if let ForwardState::Running { stop } = &self.forward {
                    let mut stop = stop.clone();
                    self.status = "Stopping forward.".to_string();
                    self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                    return Task::perform(
                        async move {
                            stop.send(ForwardInput::Stop)
                                .await
                                .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
                        },
                        |result| match result {
                            Ok(()) => Message::ForwardEvent(ForwardUiEvent::Stopped),
                            Err(error) => Message::ForwardEvent(ForwardUiEvent::Failed(
                                SshUiError::Message(error),
                            )),
                        },
                    );
                }
                self.forward = ForwardState::Stopped;
                self.status = "Forward is not running.".to_string();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ForwardEvent(event) => self.handle_forward_event(event),
            Message::ConnectShell => {
                if !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Disconnected) {
                    return Task::none();
                }
                self.manual_disconnect_requested = false;
                let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
                    Ok(parts) => parts,
                    Err(error) => {
                        return self.handle_local_validation_error("Terminal", error);
                    }
                };
                let target = route.target.display_target();
                let size = match self.terminal_size() {
                    Ok(size) => size,
                    Err(error) => {
                        return self.handle_local_validation_error("Terminal", error);
                    }
                };

                self.status = format!("Opening shell on {target}");
                self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                self.record_smoke_status("connect_shell_requested");
                self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::ConnectShell { route, size })
            }
            Message::TerminalSearchChanged(value) => {
                self.terminal_search = value;
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::TerminalViewModeChanged(mode) => {
                self.terminal_view_mode = mode;
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ThemeModeChanged(mode) => {
                self.theme_mode = mode;
                self.status = format!("Theme set to {}.", mode.label());
                self.persist_ui_settings();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::TerminalFontSizeChanged(value) => {
                self.terminal_font_size = sanitize_terminal_font_size(&value);
                self.status = format!("Terminal font size set to {}.", self.terminal_font_size);
                self.persist_ui_settings();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleAutoReconnect => {
                self.auto_reconnect = !self.auto_reconnect;
                self.status = if self.auto_reconnect {
                    "Auto reconnect enabled.".to_string()
                } else {
                    "Auto reconnect disabled.".to_string()
                };
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleFollowOutput => {
                self.follow_output = !self.follow_output;
                self.status = if self.follow_output {
                    "Terminal will follow new output.".to_string()
                } else {
                    "Terminal follow output paused.".to_string()
                };
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                if self.follow_output {
                    scroll_terminal_to_bottom()
                } else {
                    Task::none()
                }
            }
            Message::ToggleCaptureKeys => {
                self.capture_keys = !self.capture_keys;
                self.status = if self.capture_keys {
                    "Terminal key capture enabled.".to_string()
                } else {
                    "Terminal key capture disabled.".to_string()
                };
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ToggleTerminalContextMenu => {
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = !self.terminal_context_menu_open;
                if self.terminal_context_menu_open {
                    self.capture_keys = false;
                }
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::FocusTerminalSearch => {
                self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                self.capture_keys = false;
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                self.status = "Terminal search focused.".to_string();
                operation::focus(TERMINAL_SEARCH_INPUT_ID)
            }
            Message::TerminalPointerMoved(position) => {
                let snapshot = self.terminal.snapshot();
                let row = terminal_row_for_pointer(
                    position,
                    snapshot.cells.len(),
                    self.terminal_font_size_value(),
                );
                let cell = terminal_cell_for_pointer(
                    position,
                    snapshot.size,
                    self.terminal_font_size_value(),
                );
                self.hovered_terminal_row = row;
                self.hovered_terminal_cell = cell;
                if let (Some(anchor), Some(row)) = (self.terminal_selection_anchor, row) {
                    self.selected_terminal_rows = Some(normalize_row_range(anchor, row));
                }
                if let (Some(anchor), Some(cell)) = (self.terminal_cell_selection_anchor, cell) {
                    self.selected_terminal_cells = Some(TerminalSelection {
                        start: anchor,
                        end: cell,
                    });
                }
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::StartTerminalSelection => {
                self.capture_keys = matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. });
                self.terminal_selection_anchor = self.hovered_terminal_row;
                self.terminal_cell_selection_anchor = self.hovered_terminal_cell;
                self.selected_terminal_rows = None;
                self.selected_terminal_cells = None;
                if let Some(row) = self.hovered_terminal_row {
                    self.status = if self.capture_keys {
                        format!("Terminal focused at row {}.", row + 1)
                    } else {
                        format!("Terminal row {}.", row + 1)
                    };
                } else {
                    self.status = if self.capture_keys {
                        "Terminal focused.".to_string()
                    } else {
                        "No terminal row under pointer.".to_string()
                    };
                }
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::FinishTerminalSelection => {
                self.terminal_selection_anchor = None;
                self.terminal_cell_selection_anchor = None;
                if let Some(selection) = self.selected_terminal_cells {
                    self.status = selection_status_label(normalize_row_range(
                        selection.start.row,
                        selection.end.row,
                    ));
                } else if let Some(rows) = self.selected_terminal_rows {
                    self.status = selection_status_label(rows);
                } else if self.capture_keys {
                    self.status = "Terminal focused.".to_string();
                } else {
                    self.status = "No terminal rows selected.".to_string();
                }
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::SendTerminalBytes(bytes) => self.send_terminal_bytes(bytes),
            Message::ClearTerminal => {
                let size = self.terminal_size().unwrap_or(EngineTerminalSize {
                    cols: 100,
                    rows: 30,
                });
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                self.terminal = AlacrittyTerminalBuffer::new(size);
                self.shell_output.clear();
                self.shell_log.clear();
                self.selected_terminal_rows = None;
                self.selected_terminal_cells = None;
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::CopyCommandOutput => {
                if self.output.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No command output to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Command output copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.output.clone())
                }
            }
            Message::CopyDiagnostics => {
                if self.diagnostics_output.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No diagnostics output to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Diagnostics copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.diagnostics_output.clone())
                }
            }
            Message::CopyForwardLog => {
                if self.forward_log.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No forward log to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Forward log copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.forward_log.clone())
                }
            }
            Message::CopyTransferLog => {
                if self.transfer_log.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No SFTP transfer log to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "SFTP transfer log copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.transfer_log.clone())
                }
            }
            Message::CopyActivityLog => {
                if self.activity_log.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No activity log to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Activity log copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.activity_log.clone())
                }
            }
            Message::ClearActivityLog => {
                self.activity_log.clear();
                self.status = "Activity log cleared.".to_string();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::CopyTerminalOutput => {
                if self.shell_log.is_empty() && self.shell_output.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No terminal output to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                    self.status = "Terminal output copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.terminal_copy_text())
                }
            }
            Message::CopyTerminalScreen => {
                if self.shell_output.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No visible terminal screen to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                    self.status = "Visible terminal screen copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.shell_output.clone())
                }
            }
            Message::CopyTerminalSmart => {
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                if let Some(selection) = self.selected_terminal_text() {
                    self.status = "Terminal selection copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(selection)
                } else if self.shell_log.is_empty() && self.shell_output.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No terminal output to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Terminal output copied.".to_string();
                    clipboard::write(self.terminal_copy_text())
                }
            }
            Message::CopySelectedTerminalLine => {
                let Some(selection) = self.selected_terminal_text() else {
                    self.status = "No terminal selection to copy.".to_string();
                    return Task::none();
                };
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                self.status = "Terminal selection copied.".to_string();
                clipboard::write(selection)
            }
            Message::CopyTerminalSearchMatches => {
                let matches =
                    terminal_search_matches(&self.terminal_copy_text(), &self.terminal_search);
                if matches.is_empty() {
                    self.status = "No terminal search matches to copy.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = format!("Copied {} terminal search matches.", matches.len());
                    clipboard::write(matches.join("\n"))
                }
            }
            Message::DismissIssue => {
                self.issue = None;
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::OpenIssueDetails => {
                if let Some(issue) = &self.issue {
                    let detail = format!("{} {} {}", issue.title, issue.detail, issue.guidance);
                    self.open_recovery_controls("Issue", &detail);
                }
                self.advanced_connection_open = true;
                self.status = "Opened recovery details.".to_string();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::PasteClipboardToTerminal => {
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                if !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. }) {
                    self.status = "Shell is not connected.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Reading clipboard.".to_string();
                    clipboard::read().map(Message::PasteClipboardRead)
                }
            }
            Message::PasteClipboardRead(contents) => match contents {
                Some(contents) if !contents.is_empty() => {
                    self.send_terminal_bytes(contents.into_bytes())
                }
                _ => {
                    self.status = "Clipboard is empty.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                }
            },
            Message::PasteClipboardBracketed => {
                self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                if !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. }) {
                    self.status = "Shell is not connected.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                } else {
                    self.status = "Reading clipboard.".to_string();
                    clipboard::read().map(Message::PasteClipboardBracketedRead)
                }
            }
            Message::PasteClipboardBracketedRead(contents) => match contents {
                Some(contents) if !contents.is_empty() => {
                    self.send_terminal_bytes(bracketed_paste_bytes(&contents))
                }
                _ => {
                    self.status = "Clipboard is empty.".to_string();
                    Task::none()
                }
            },
            Message::WindowResized(size) => self.apply_window_terminal_size(size),
            Message::DisconnectShell => {
                if let ShellState::Connected { input } = &self.shell {
                    let mut input = input.clone();
                    self.manual_disconnect_requested = true;
                    self.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                    self.status = "Shell disconnect requested.".to_string();
                    self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                    return Task::perform(
                        async move {
                            input
                                .send(ShellInput::Disconnect)
                                .await
                                .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
                        },
                        |result| match result {
                            Ok(()) => {
                                Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed { tab_id: usize::MAX })
                            }
                            Err(error) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Failed {
                                tab_id: usize::MAX,
                                error: SshUiError::Message(error),
                            }),
                        },
                    );
                }
                self.manual_disconnect_requested = true;
                self.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                Task::none()
            }
            Message::ShellEvent(event) => {
                let event_tab_id = shell_event_tab_id(&event);
                let tab_id = if event_tab_id == usize::MAX {
                    self.active_terminal_tab
                } else {
                    event_tab_id
                };
                if tab_id != self.active_terminal_tab {
                    self.apply_shell_event_to_tab(tab_id, event);
                    return Task::none();
                }
                match event {
                    ShellEvent::StartValidated {
                        route,
                        size,
                        target,
                    } => {
                        self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                        self.shell = ShellState::Connecting { route, size };
                        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
