                        self.shell_output.clear();
                        self.shell_log.clear();
                        self.terminal.resize(size);
                        self.status = format!("Opening shell on {target}");
                        self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                        self.record_smoke_status("shell_start_validated");
                    }
                    ShellEvent::Ready {
                        tab_id: _,
                        mut input,
                    } => {
                        if let ShellState::Connecting { route, size } = &self.shell {
                            let route = route.clone();
                            let size = *size;
                            let term = std::env::var("TERM")
                                .unwrap_or_else(|_| "xterm-256color".to_string());
                            self.shell = ShellState::Connected {
                                input: input.clone(),
                            };
                            self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
                            self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                            self.advanced_connection_open = false;
                            self.capture_keys = true;
                            self.issue = None;
                            self.mark_connection_ok("shell connected");
                            self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                            self.status = "Shell connected.".to_string();
                            self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                            self.record_smoke_status("shell_connected");
                            return Task::perform(
                                async move {
                                    input
                                        .send(ShellInput::Start {
                                            route,
                                            term,
                                            cols: size.cols,
                                            rows: size.rows,
                                        })
                                        .await
                                        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
                                },
                                |result| match result {
                                    Ok(()) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Output {
                                        tab_id: usize::MAX,
                                        bytes: Vec::new(),
                                    }),
                                    Err(error) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Failed {
                                        tab_id: usize::MAX,
                                        error: SshUiError::Message(error),
                                    }),
                                },
                            );
                        }
                    }
                    ShellEvent::Output { bytes, .. } => {
                        if !bytes.is_empty() {
                            append_shell_log(&mut self.shell_log, &bytes, 200_000);
                            let _ = self.terminal.write_remote_output(&bytes);
                            self.shell_output = self.terminal.snapshot().render_plain_text();
                            self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                            self.record_smoke_status("shell_output");
                            if let Some(task) = self.maybe_run_disconnect_reconnect_smoke() {
                                return task;
                            }
                            if let Some(task) = self.maybe_run_input_smoke_probe() {
                                return task;
                            }
                            if let Some(task) = self.maybe_run_overflow_smoke_probe() {
                                return task;
                            }
                            if std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_AUTO_RESIZE_PROBE").is_some() {
                                if let Some(task) = self.maybe_run_resize_smoke_probe() {
                                    return task;
                                }
                            }
                            if self.follow_output {
                                return scroll_terminal_to_bottom();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    ShellEvent::ExitStatus { code, .. } => {
                        self.status = format!("Shell exited with status {code}");
                        self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                        self.record_smoke_status("shell_exit_status");
                    }
                    ShellEvent::Closed { .. } => {
                        let should_reconnect = self.should_auto_reconnect();
                        self.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                        self.capture_keys = false;
                        self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                        if should_reconnect {
                            self.status = "Shell closed unexpectedly. Reconnecting.".to_string();
                        } else {
                            self.status = "Shell closed.".to_string();
                        }
                        self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                        self.record_smoke_status("shell_closed");
                        if let Some(task) = self.maybe_continue_disconnect_reconnect_smoke() {
                            return task;
                        }
                        if should_reconnect {
                            return self.reconnect_shell();
                        }
                    }
                    ShellEvent::Failed { error, .. } => {
                        if is_closed_channel_send_error(&error) {
                            self.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                            self.capture_keys = false;
                            self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                            self.issue = None;
                            self.status = "Shell closed.".to_string();
                            self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                            self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                            self.record_smoke_status("shell_closed");
                            return Task::none();
                        }
                        let should_reconnect = self.should_auto_reconnect();
                        let is_host_key_challenge =
                            matches!(error, SshUiError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(_));
                        if should_reconnect {
                            self.status = format!("Shell failed: {error}. Reconnecting.");
                            self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                            self.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                            self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                            self.record_smoke_status("shell_failed_reconnect");
                            return self.reconnect_shell();
                        }
                        self.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                        self.capture_keys = false;
                        self.terminal_context_menu_open = false;
                        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
                        if !is_host_key_challenge {
                            self.record_smoke_status("shell_failed");
                        }
                        return self.handle_ssh_error(error, "Shell failed");
                    }
                }
                Task::none()
            }
        }
    }

    fn fill_test_server(&mut self) {
        self.selected = None;
        self.pending_delete_host = false;
        self.host_name = "OpenTerm test server".to_string();
        self.host = TEST_SERVER_HOST.to_string();
        self.user = TEST_SERVER_USER.to_string();
        self.port = TEST_SERVER_PORT.to_string();
        self.jump_host = JumpHostChoice::None;
        self.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        if let Some(password_env) = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_PASSWORD_ENV") {
            if let Some(password) = std::env::var_os(password_env) {
                self.password = password.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
            }
        }
        self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
        self.workspace_tools_open = false;
        self.capture_keys = false;
        self.advanced_connection_open = true;
        self.issue = None;
        self.status =
            "Test server filled. Enter the password in the sidebar, then Connect.".to_string();
    }

    fn subscription(&self) -> Subscription<Message> {
        let shell = Subscription::batch(self.terminal_tabs.iter().enumerate().filter_map(
            |(tab_id, tab)| match tab.shell {
                ShellState::Disconnected => None,
                ShellState::Connecting { .. } | ShellState::Connected { .. } => {
                    Some(Subscription::run_with(tab_id, shell_worker).map(Message::ShellEvent))
                }
            },
        ));
        let forward = match self.forward {
            ForwardState::Stopped => Subscription::none(),
            ForwardState::Starting { .. } | ForwardState::Running { .. } => {
                Subscription::run(forward_worker).map(Message::ForwardEvent)
            }
        };

        let keyboard = match self.shell {
            ShellState::Connected { .. } if self.capture_keys => {
                keyboard::listen().filter_map(|event| {
                    let keyboard::Event::KeyPressed {
                        key,
                        text,
                        modifiers,
                        repeat: false,
                        ..
                    } = event
                    else {
                        return None;
                    };

                    if terminal_shortcut_message(&key, modifiers).is_some() {
                        return None;
                    }

                    terminal_key_bytes(key, modifiers, text.as_deref())
                        .map(Message::SendTerminalBytes)
                })
            }
            _ => Subscription::none(),
        };

        let terminal_shortcuts = keyboard::listen().filter_map(|event| {
            let keyboard::Event::KeyPressed {
                key,
                modifiers,
                repeat: false,
                ..
            } = event
            else {
                return None;
            };

            terminal_shortcut_message(&key, modifiers)
        });

        let app_shortcuts = keyboard::listen().filter_map(|event| {
            let keyboard::Event::KeyPressed {
                key,
                modifiers,
                repeat: false,
                ..
            } = event
            else {
                return None;
            };

            app_shortcut_message(&key, modifiers)
        });

        let palette = keyboard::listen().filter_map(|event| {
            let keyboard::Event::KeyPressed {
                key,
                modifiers,
                repeat: false,
                ..
            } = event
            else {
                return None;
            };

            is_command_palette_shortcut(&key, modifiers).then_some(Message::ToggleCommandPalette)
        });

        let resize = window::resize_events().map(|(_id, size)| Message::WindowResized(size));

        Subscription::batch([
            shell,
            forward,
            keyboard,
            terminal_shortcuts,
            app_shortcuts,
            palette,
            resize,
        ])
    }

    fn view(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let sidebar = self.sidebar();
        let workspace = self.workspace();

        container(
            column![
                self.command_palette(),
                row![sidebar, workspace].height(Fill)
            ]
            .spacing(0)
            .padding(0),
        )
        .style(|_theme| panel_style(PanelTone::App))
        .width(Fill)
        .height(Fill)
        .into()
    }

    fn command_palette(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.command_palette_open {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        let mut actions = Column::new().spacing(6);
        let matches = palette_matches(&self.command_palette_query);
        if matches.is_empty() {
            actions = actions.push(text("No matching command").size(13).color(TEXT_DIM));
        } else {
            for action in matches {
                actions = actions.push(
                    button_tone(
                        row![
                            column![
                                text(action.title()).size(14).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                                text(action.hint()).size(12).color(TEXT_MUTED)
                            ]
                            .spacing(2),
                            Space::new().width(Fill),
                            text("Run").size(12).color(TEXT_DIM),
                        ]
                        .spacing(10)
                        .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                        ButtonTone::Ghost,
                    )
                    .width(Fill)
                    .on_press(Message::CommandPaletteRun(action)),
                );
            }
        }

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Command palette").size(15).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    text("Search actions, panels, and SSH workflows")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("Close", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::ToggleCommandPalette),
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                text_input_tone(
                    "connect, sftp, forward, diagnostics...",
                    &self.command_palette_query,
                    Message::CommandPaletteQueryChanged
                ),
                actions,
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Section,
        )
        .padding(12)
        .into()
    }

    fn sidebar(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let mut hosts = Column::new().spacing(4);
        if self.hosts.is_empty() {
            hosts = hosts.push(self.empty_hosts_state());
            if self.has_target() {
                hosts = hosts.push(self.current_session_host_item());
            }
        } else {
            let matches = filtered_host_indices(&self.hosts, &self.host_search);
            if matches.is_empty() {
                hosts = hosts.push(
                    text(format!("No hosts match \"{}\"", self.host_search.trim()))
                        .size(14)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                );
            }
            for index in matches {
                let Some(host) = self.hosts.get(index) else {
                    continue;
                };
                hosts = hosts.push(
                    button(
                        column![
                            row![
                                text(host_list_title(host)).size(14).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                                Space::new().width(Fill),
                                text(host.port.to_string()).size(12).color(TEXT_DIM),
                            ]
                            .spacing(8)
                            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                            text(host_list_meta(host)).size(12).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                        ]
                        .spacing(3),
                    )
                    .width(Fill)
                    .padding([8, 10])
                    .style(move |_theme, status| {
                        list_row_style(
                            if Some(index) == self.selected {
                                ListRowTone::Selected
                            } else {
                                ListRowTone::Normal
                            },
                            status,
                        )
                    })
                    .on_press(Message::HostSelected(index)),
                );
            }
        }

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    column![
                        text("OpenTerm").size(19).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                        text(format!("{} hosts", self.hosts.len()))
                            .size(12)
                            .color(TEXT_DIM),
                    ]
                    .spacing(2),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("New", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::NewConnection),
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Search hosts",
                    &self.host_search,
                    Message::HostSearchChanged
                ),
                self.selected_host_actions(),
                self.sidebar_connection_editor(),
                app_scrollable(hosts),
                self.import_export_controls(),
                self.host_delete_confirmation()
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Sidebar,
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(SIDEBAR_WIDTH))
        .height(Fill)
        .padding([14, 10])
        .into()
    }

    fn empty_hosts_state(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let label = if self.has_target() {
            "Not saved"
        } else {
            "No hosts"
        };
        row![
            text(label).size(12).color(TEXT_DIM),
            Space::new().width(Fill),
            button_tone(self.empty_hosts_action_label(), ButtonTone::Ghost)
                .on_press(self.empty_hosts_action_message()),
        ]
        .spacing(8)
        .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
        .into()
    }

    fn empty_hosts_action_label(&self) -> &'static str {
        if self.has_target() {
            "Save"
        } else {
            "Add"
        }
    }

    fn empty_hosts_action_message(&self) -> Message {
        if self.has_target() {
            Message::SaveHost
        } else {
            Message::NewConnection
        }
    }

    fn current_session_host_item(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        button(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Current session").size(14).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    text(self.port.trim()).size(12).color(TEXT_DIM),
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                text(self.target_label()).size(12).color(TEXT_MUTED),
            ]
            .spacing(3),
        )
        .width(Fill)
        .padding([8, 10])
        .style(|_theme, status| list_row_style(ListRowTone::Selected, status))
        .on_press(Message::OpenConnectionEditor)
        .into()
    }

    fn selected_host_actions(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if self.selected.is_none() {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        panel(
            row![
                text("Selected host").size(12).color(TEXT_DIM),
                Space::new().width(Fill),
                button_tone("Edit", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::OpenConnectionEditor),
                button_tone("Remove", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::RequestDeleteHost),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
            PanelTone::Inset,
        )
        .padding([4, 6])
        .into()
    }

    fn import_export_controls(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.sidebar_tools_open {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        column![
            text("Import / Export").size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
            text_input_tone(
                "~/.ssh/config",
                &self.import_path,
                Message::ImportPathChanged
            ),
            button_tone("Import SSH config", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .width(Fill)
                .on_press(Message::ImportSshConfig),
            text_input_tone(
                "openterm-hosts.toml",
                &self.export_path,
                Message::ExportPathChanged
            ),
            button_tone("Export TOML", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .width(Fill)
                .on_press(Message::ExportHosts),
        ]
        .spacing(8)
        .into()
    }

    fn host_delete_confirmation(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.pending_delete_host {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        let label = self
            .selected
            .and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index))
            .map(|host| format!("Delete {}?", host.name))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "Delete selected host?".to_string());

        column![
            text(label).size(14).color(WARNING),
            row![
                button_tone("Cancel", ButtonTone::Secondary).on_press(Message::CancelDeleteHost),
                button_tone("Confirm", ButtonTone::Danger).on_press(Message::ConfirmDeleteHost)
            ]
            .spacing(8)
        ]
        .spacing(8)
        .into()
    }

    fn sidebar_connection_editor(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.connection_fields_visible() {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        panel(
            column![
                text("Connection").size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                text_input_tone("Name", &self.host_name, Message::HostNameChanged),
                text_input_tone("Host or IP", &self.host, Message::HostChanged),
                row![
                    text_input_tone("User", &self.user, Message::UserChanged),
                    text_input_tone("Port", &self.port, Message::PortChanged)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(74.0)),
                ]
                .spacing(8),
                self.sidebar_auth_controls(),
                row![
                    button_tone("Save & connect", ButtonTone::Primary)
                        .on_press(Message::SaveHostSecretAndConnect),
                    button_tone("Save", ButtonTone::Secondary).on_press(Message::SaveHost),
                    if self.testing_connection {
                        button_tone("Testing...", ButtonTone::Ghost)
                    } else {
                        button_tone("Test", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::TestConnection)
                    },
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                self.sidebar_connection_more_controls(),
                self.routing_controls(),
            ]
            .spacing(8),
            PanelTone::Section,
        )
        .padding(10)
        .into()
    }

    fn sidebar_auth_controls(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let auth_picker = pick_list(
            AuthMode::ALL,
            Some(self.auth_mode),
            Message::AuthModeChanged,
        )
        .width(Fill);

        match self.auth_mode {
            AuthMode::AgentOrDefault => column![
                auth_picker,
                text("Uses SSH agent or default local key")
                    .size(12)
                    .color(TEXT_DIM),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .into(),
            AuthMode::Password => column![
                auth_picker,
                text_input_tone("Password", &self.password, Message::PasswordChanged).secure(true),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .into(),
            AuthMode::PrivateKey => column![
                auth_picker,
                text_input_tone("Private key path", &self.key_path, Message::KeyPathChanged),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Key passphrase",
                    &self.passphrase,
                    Message::PassphraseChanged
                )
                .secure(true),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .into(),
        }
    }

    fn sidebar_connection_more_controls(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        row![button_tone(
            if self.routing_details_open {
                "Hide Routing"
            } else {
                "Routing"
            },
            ButtonTone::Ghost
        )
        .on_press(Message::ToggleRoutingDetails),]
        .spacing(8)
        .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
        .into()
    }

    fn workspace(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        panel(
            column![
                self.session_bar(),
                self.issue_panel(),
                self.host_key_confirmation(),
                self.active_panel()
            ]
            .spacing(0)
            .height(Fill)
            .width(Fill),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .width(Fill)
        .height(Fill)
        .padding(0)
        .into()
    }

    fn workspace_tab_bar(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let terminal = tab_button(
            text("Terminal").size(13),
            if self.panel == Panel::Terminal {
                TabTone::Active
            } else {
                TabTone::Inactive
            },
        )
        .on_press(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Terminal));
        let sftp = tab_button(
            text("SFTP").size(13),
            if self.panel == Panel::Sftp {
                TabTone::Active
            } else {
                TabTone::Inactive
            },
        )
        .on_press(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp));

        row![terminal, sftp, Space::new().width(Fill)]
            .spacing(4)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .padding([0, 10])
            .into()
    }

    fn session_bar(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let shell_control: Element<'_, Message> = if self.session_bar_shows_shell_controls() {
            match self.shell {
                ShellState::Connected { .. } => button_tone("Disconnect", ButtonTone::Danger)
                    .on_press(Message::DisconnectShell)
                    .into(),
                ShellState::Connecting { .. } => {
                    button_tone("Connecting...", ButtonTone::Active).into()
                }
                ShellState::Disconnected if self.has_target() => {
                    button_tone("Connect", ButtonTone::Primary)
                        .on_press(Message::ConnectShell)
                        .into()
                }
                ShellState::Disconnected => button_tone("No target", ButtonTone::Ghost).into(),
            }
        } else {
            Space::new().width(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into()
        };
        let status_text = self.session_bar_status_text();
        let panel_tabs: Element<'_, Message> = if matches!(self.panel, Panel::Terminal) {
            Space::new().width(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into()
        } else {
            self.workspace_tab_bar()
        };
        let status: Element<'_, Message> = if status_text.trim().is_empty() {
            Space::new().width(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into()
        } else {
            quiet_status_text(status_text)
        };
        let mut target = row![text(self.target_label()).size(15).color(TEXT_HIGH)]
            .spacing(8)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .width(Fill);
        if self.session_bar_shows_shell_controls() {
            target = target.push(status_chip(
                "Shell",
                self.shell.label(),
                shell_status_color(&self.shell),
            ));
        }
        target = target.push(status);
        panel(
            row![target, panel_tabs, shell_control,]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
            PanelTone::Toolbar,
        )
        .padding([6, 10])
        .into()
    }

    fn session_bar_status_text(&self) -> String {
        match self.shell {
            ShellState::Connected { .. } if matches!(self.panel, Panel::Terminal) => String::new(),
            _ if matches!(self.panel, Panel::Terminal) => {
                terminal_session_status_text(&self.status)
            }
            _ => format!("{}: {}", self.panel.label(), self.status),
        }
    }

    fn session_bar_shows_shell_controls(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self.panel, Panel::Terminal)
    }

    fn connection_fields_visible(&self) -> bool {
        self.advanced_connection_open && matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Disconnected)
    }

    fn vault_controls(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.vault_details_open {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        panel(
            row![
                text("Vault").size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Vault master password",
                    &self.vault_password,
                    Message::VaultPasswordChanged
                )
                .secure(true),
                button_tone(
                    if self.vault_unlocked {
                        "Vault unlocked"
                    } else {
                        "Unlock vault"
                    },
                    if self.vault_unlocked {
                        ButtonTone::Active
                    } else {
                        ButtonTone::Secondary
                    }
                )
                .on_press(Message::UnlockVault),
                button_tone("Lock", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::LockVault),
            ]
            .spacing(10)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
            PanelTone::Inset,
        )
        .padding(8)
        .into()
    }

    fn routing_controls(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.routing_details_open {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        let jump_options = jump_host_choices(&self.hosts, self.selected);

        panel(
            row![
                text("Routing").size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                pick_list(
                    jump_options,
                    Some(self.jump_host.clone()),
                    Message::JumpHostSelected
                )
                .placeholder("No jump host")
                .width(Fill),
            ]
            .spacing(10)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
            PanelTone::Inset,
        )
        .padding(8)
        .into()
    }

    fn target_label(&self) -> String {
        if !self.has_target() {
            "No target".to_string()
        } else if self.user.trim().is_empty() {
            format!("{}:{}", self.host.trim(), self.port.trim())
        } else {
            format!(
                "{}@{}:{}",
                self.user.trim(),
                self.host.trim(),
                self.port.trim()
            )
        }
    }

    fn has_target(&self) -> bool {
        !self.host.trim().is_empty()
    }

    fn activity_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Activity").size(18).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    status_chip("Target", self.target_label(), TEXT_MUTED),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("Copy", ButtonTone::Secondary).on_press(Message::CopyActivityLog),
                    button_tone("Copy transfers", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::CopyTransferLog),
                    button_tone("Copy forwards", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::CopyForwardLog),
                    button_tone("Clear", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::ClearActivityLog),
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                panel(
                    app_scrollable(
                        text(combined_activity_log(
                            &self.activity_log,
                            &self.transfer_log,
                            &self.forward_log
                        ))
                        .font(iced::Font::MONOSPACE)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                        .size(13)
                    ),
                    PanelTone::Terminal,
                )
                .height(Fill)
                .width(Fill)
                .padding(14),
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .height(Fill)
        .width(Fill)
        .padding(10)
        .into()
    }

    fn host_key_confirmation(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let Some(pending) = &self.pending_host_key else {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        };
        let challenge = &pending.challenge;

        container(
            column![
                text("Host key verification").size(18).color(WARNING),
                text(format!("Host: {}:{}", challenge.host, challenge.port))
                    .size(14)
                    .color(TEXT_HIGH),
                text(format!("Algorithm: {}", challenge.algorithm))
                    .size(14)
                    .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                text(format!("Fingerprint: {}", challenge.fingerprint))
                    .font(Font::MONOSPACE)
                    .color(TEXT_HIGH)
                    .size(14),
                text(format!("Known hosts: {}", challenge.known_hosts.display()))
                    .size(14)
                    .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                row![
                    button_tone("Cancel", ButtonTone::Secondary).on_press(Message::CancelHostKey),
                    button_tone("Trust and retry", ButtonTone::Primary)
                        .on_press(Message::AcceptHostKey),
                ]
                .spacing(10)
            ]
            .spacing(8),
        )
        .style(|_theme| panel_style(PanelTone::Alert))
        .width(Fill)
        .padding(12)
        .into()
    }

    fn issue_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let Some(issue) = &self.issue else {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        };
        let summary = compact_status_label(format!("{} {}", issue.detail, issue.guidance));

        panel(
            row![
                text(&issue.title).size(13).color(WARNING),
                text(summary).size(12).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                Space::new().width(Fill),
                button_tone("Fix", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::OpenIssueDetails),
                button_tone("Dismiss", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::DismissIssue),
            ]
            .spacing(8)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
            PanelTone::Alert,
        )
        .padding([5, 8])
        .into()
    }

    fn active_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        match self.panel {
            Panel::Terminal => self.shell_panel(),
            Panel::Command => self.command_panel(),
            Panel::Sftp => self.sftp_panel(),
            Panel::Forward => self.forward_panel(),
            Panel::Diagnostics => self.diagnostics_panel(),
            Panel::Activity => self.activity_panel(),
            Panel::Settings => self.settings_panel(),
        }
    }

    fn diagnostics_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let run = if self.running_diagnostics {
            button_tone("Running diagnostics...", ButtonTone::Secondary)
        } else {
            button_tone("Run diagnostics", ButtonTone::Primary).on_press(Message::RunDiagnostics)
        };

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Diagnostics").size(18).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    run,
                    button_tone("Copy diagnostics", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::CopyDiagnostics)
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                panel(
                    app_scrollable(
                        text(&self.diagnostics_output)
                            .font(iced::Font::MONOSPACE)
                            .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                            .size(14)
                    ),
                    PanelTone::Inset,
                )
                .height(Fill)
                .width(Fill)
                .padding(12),
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .padding(12)
        .into()
    }

    fn command_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let run = if self.running {
            button_tone("Running...", ButtonTone::Secondary)
        } else {
            button_tone("Run command", ButtonTone::Primary).on_press(Message::RunCommand)
        };

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Command").size(18).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    run
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                text_input_tone("hostname && whoami", &self.command, Message::CommandChanged)
                    .on_submit(Message::RunCommand),
                row![
                    text("Output").size(16).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("Copy output", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::CopyCommandOutput)
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                panel(
                    app_scrollable(
                        text(&self.output)
                            .font(iced::Font::MONOSPACE)
                            .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                            .size(14)
                    ),
                    PanelTone::Inset,
                )
                .height(Fill)
                .width(Fill)
                .padding(12),
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .padding(12)
        .into()
    }

    fn forward_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let start = match self.forward {
            ForwardState::Stopped => {
                button_tone("Start", ButtonTone::Primary).on_press(Message::StartForward)
            }
            ForwardState::Starting { .. } => button_tone("Starting...", ButtonTone::Secondary),
            ForwardState::Running { .. } => button_tone("Running", ButtonTone::Active),
        };
        let stop = match self.forward {
            ForwardState::Running { .. } | ForwardState::Starting { .. } => {
                button_tone("Stop", ButtonTone::Danger).on_press(Message::StopForward)
            }
            ForwardState::Stopped => button_tone("Stop", ButtonTone::Ghost),
        };
        let tools = button_tone(
            if self.forward_tools_open {
                "Hide tools"
            } else {
                "Tools"
            },
            ButtonTone::Ghost,
        )
        .on_press(Message::ToggleForwardTools);
        let direction_label = match self.forward_mode {
            ForwardMode::Local => "Local port forwards to remote service",
            ForwardMode::Dynamic => "Local SOCKS5 proxy forwards per client request",
            ForwardMode::Remote => "Remote port forwards back to this machine",
        };
        let endpoint_inputs: Element<'_, Message> = match self.forward_mode {
            ForwardMode::Local => row![
                text("->").size(16).color(TEXT_DIM),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Remote service",
                    &self.forward_remote_host,
                    Message::ForwardRemoteHostChanged
                ),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Port",
                    &self.forward_remote_port,
                    Message::ForwardRemotePortChanged
                )
                .width(Length::Fixed(110.0)),
            ]
            .spacing(10)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .into(),
            ForwardMode::Dynamic => text("Clients choose destinations through SOCKS5")
                .size(13)
                .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                .into(),
            ForwardMode::Remote => row![
                text("->").size(16).color(TEXT_DIM),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Local service",
                    &self.forward_remote_host,
                    Message::ForwardRemoteHostChanged
                ),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Port",
                    &self.forward_remote_port,
                    Message::ForwardRemotePortChanged
                )
                .width(Length::Fixed(110.0)),
            ]
            .spacing(10)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .into(),
        };

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Forward").size(18).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    status_chip(
                        "State",
                        self.forward.label(self.forward_mode),
                        forward_status_color(&self.forward)
                    ),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    start,
                    stop,
                    tools
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                text(direction_label).size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                row![
                    text_input_tone("Local bind host", &self.bind_host, Message::BindHostChanged),
                    text_input_tone("Local port", &self.bind_port, Message::BindPortChanged)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(120.0)),
                    endpoint_inputs,
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                self.forward_tools_panel(),
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .padding(12)
        .into()
    }

    fn forward_tools_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.forward_tools_open {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        let local_mode = if self.forward_mode == ForwardMode::Local {
            button_tone("Local selected", ButtonTone::Active)
        } else {
            button_tone("Local", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::ForwardModeChanged(ForwardMode::Local))
        };
        let dynamic_mode = if self.forward_mode == ForwardMode::Dynamic {
            button_tone("SOCKS selected", ButtonTone::Active)
        } else {
            button_tone("SOCKS", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::ForwardModeChanged(ForwardMode::Dynamic))
        };
        let remote_mode = if self.forward_mode == ForwardMode::Remote {
            button_tone("Remote selected", ButtonTone::Active)
        } else {
            button_tone("Remote", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::ForwardModeChanged(ForwardMode::Remote))
        };
        let target_inputs: Element<'_, Message> = match self.forward_mode {
            ForwardMode::Local => row![
                text("to").size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Remote host",
                    &self.forward_remote_host,
                    Message::ForwardRemoteHostChanged
                ),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Remote port",
                    &self.forward_remote_port,
                    Message::ForwardRemotePortChanged
                )
                .width(Length::Fixed(120.0)),
            ]
            .spacing(10)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .into(),
            ForwardMode::Dynamic => text("SOCKS5 proxy; client chooses destination")
                .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                .size(14)
                .into(),
            ForwardMode::Remote => row![
                text("remote points to local").size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Local host",
                    &self.forward_remote_host,
                    Message::ForwardRemoteHostChanged
                ),
                text_input_tone(
                    "Local port",
                    &self.forward_remote_port,
                    Message::ForwardRemotePortChanged
                )
                .width(Length::Fixed(120.0)),
            ]
            .spacing(10)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .into(),
        };

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Forward tools").size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    local_mode,
                    dynamic_mode,
                    remote_mode,
                    text(self.forward_mode.label()).size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED)
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![
                    text("Mode detail").size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    target_inputs,
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![
                    text("Activity").size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("Open", ButtonTone::Ghost)
                        .on_press(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Activity)),
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
            ]
            .spacing(10),
            PanelTone::Inset,
        )
        .padding(10)
        .into()
    }

    fn sftp_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if self.host.trim().is_empty() {
            return panel(
                column![
                    text("No SFTP session").size(20).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    text("Select a host from the sidebar or create a new one.")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    row![
                        button_tone("New host", ButtonTone::Primary)
                            .on_press(Message::NewConnection),
                        button_tone("Import / export", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(
                            Message::CommandPaletteRun(PaletteAction::ToggleImportExport)
                        ),
                    ]
                    .spacing(8)
                    .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                ]
                .spacing(10),
                PanelTone::Panel,
            )
            .height(Fill)
            .width(Fill)
            .padding(24)
            .into();
        }

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text(self.target_label()).size(14).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    status_chip(
                        "Transfer",
                        self.active_transfer.as_deref().unwrap_or("idle"),
                        if self.active_transfer.is_some() {
                            WARNING
                        } else {
                            TEXT_MUTED
                        }
                    ),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone(sftp_top_actions()[0], ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::RefreshFiles),
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![self.sftp_local_pane(), self.sftp_remote_pane(),]
                    .spacing(8)
                    .height(Fill),
                self.selected_remote_file_details(),
                self.delete_confirmation(),
            ]
            .spacing(8),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .height(Fill)
        .width(Fill)
        .padding(10)
        .into()
    }

    fn sftp_local_pane(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        self.sftp_pane(
            "Local",
            row![
                text_input_tone("Local path", &self.local_path, Message::LocalPathChanged)
                    .on_submit(Message::RefreshLocalFiles),
                button_tone("Up", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::GoLocalParentDirectory),
                button_tone("Open", ButtonTone::Ghost)
                    .on_press(Message::OpenSelectedLocalDirectory),
                button_tone("Reload", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::RefreshLocalFiles),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .into(),
            self.local_file_list(),
        )
    }

    fn sftp_remote_pane(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        self.sftp_pane(
            "Remote",
            row![
                text_input_tone("Remote path", &self.remote_path, Message::RemotePathChanged)
                    .on_submit(Message::RefreshFiles),
                button_tone("Up", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::GoParentDirectory),
                button_tone("Home", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::GoHomeDirectory),
                button_tone("Root", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(Message::GoRootDirectory),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
            .into(),
            self.remote_file_list(),
        )
    }

    fn sftp_pane<'a>(
        &'a self,
        title: &'static str,
        toolbar: Element<'a, Message>,
        files: Element<'a, Message>,
    ) -> Element<'a, Message> {
        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text(title).size(14).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    text(if title == "Local" {
                        format!("{} items", self.local_files.len())
                    } else {
                        format!("{} items", self.remote_files.len())
                    })
                    .size(12)
                    .color(TEXT_DIM),
                ]
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                toolbar,
                panel(files, PanelTone::Inset)
                    .height(Fill)
                    .width(Fill)
                    .padding(8),
            ]
            .spacing(7),
            PanelTone::Section,
        )
        .height(Fill)
        .width(Fill)
        .padding(8)
        .into()
    }

    fn local_file_list(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let mut files = Column::new().spacing(5);
        if self.local_files.is_empty() {
            files = files.push(text("No local files loaded").size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED));
        } else {
            for (index, entry) in self.local_files.iter().enumerate() {
                let selected = Some(index) == self.selected_local_file;
                files = files.push(
                    button(file_entry_row(
                        selected,
                        local_file_icon(entry.kind),
                        &entry.name,
                        entry.size.map(format_bytes).unwrap_or_default(),
                    ))
                    .width(Fill)
                    .padding([2, 4])
                    .style(move |_theme, status| {
                        list_row_style(
                            if selected {
                                ListRowTone::Selected
                            } else {
                                ListRowTone::Normal
                            },
                            status,
                        )
                    })
                    .on_press(Message::LocalFileSelected(index)),
                );
            }
        }

        app_scrollable(files).into()
    }

    fn remote_file_list(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let mut files = Column::new().spacing(5);
        if self.remote_files.is_empty() {
            files = files.push(text("No remote files loaded").size(14).color(TEXT_MUTED));
        } else {
            for (index, entry) in self.remote_files.iter().enumerate() {
                let selected = Some(index) == self.selected_remote_file;
                files = files.push(
                    button(file_entry_row(
                        selected,
                        remote_file_icon(entry),
                        &entry.name,
                        entry.size.map(format_bytes).unwrap_or_default(),
                    ))
                    .width(Fill)
                    .padding([2, 4])
                    .style(move |_theme, status| {
                        list_row_style(
                            if selected {
                                ListRowTone::Selected
                            } else {
                                ListRowTone::Normal
                            },
                            status,
                        )
                    })
                    .on_press(Message::RemoteFileSelected(index)),
                );
            }
        }

        app_scrollable(files).into()
    }

    fn remote_file_operations(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        row![
            text_input_tone("Name / directory", &self.new_name, Message::NewNameChanged),
            button_tone(sftp_selection_actions()[0], ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::UploadLocalFile),
            button_tone(sftp_selection_actions()[1], ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::DownloadSelected),
            button_tone(sftp_selection_actions()[2], ButtonTone::Ghost)
                .on_press(Message::OpenSelectedDirectory),
            button_tone(sftp_selection_actions()[3], ButtonTone::Ghost)
                .on_press(Message::CreateRemoteDirectory),
            button_tone(sftp_selection_actions()[4], ButtonTone::Ghost)
                .on_press(Message::RenameSelectedRemote),
            button_tone(sftp_selection_actions()[5], ButtonTone::Ghost)
                .on_press(Message::RequestDeleteSelectedRemote),
        ]
        .spacing(8)
        .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center)
        .into()
    }

    fn delete_confirmation(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if let Some(entry) = &self.pending_delete_remote {
            panel(
                row![
                    text(format!("Confirm delete: {}", entry.path))
                        .size(14)
                        .color(WARNING),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("Cancel", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::CancelDeleteRemote),
                    button_tone("Confirm delete", ButtonTone::Danger)
                        .on_press(Message::ConfirmDeleteRemote),
                ]
                .spacing(10)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                PanelTone::Alert,
            )
            .padding(10)
            .into()
        } else {
            Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into()
        }
    }

    fn selected_remote_file_details(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let Some(index) = self.selected_remote_file else {
            return text("No remote entry selected")
                .size(13)
                .color(TEXT_DIM)
                .into();
        };
        let Some(entry) = self.remote_files.get(index) else {
            return text("Selected remote entry is no longer available")
                .size(13)
                .color(WARNING)
                .into();
        };

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text(entry.path.clone()).size(13).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    text(format!("Type: {}", remote_file_kind_label(&entry.kind)))
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    text(format!(
                        "Size: {}",
                        entry
                            .size
                            .map(format_bytes)
                            .unwrap_or_else(|| "-".to_string())
                    ))
                    .size(13)
                    .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                    text(format!(
                        "Mode: {}",
                        entry
                            .permissions
                            .map(format_permissions)
                            .unwrap_or_else(|| "-".to_string())
                    ))
                    .size(13)
                    .color(TEXT_MUTED),
                ]
                .spacing(16),
                self.remote_file_operations(),
            ]
            .spacing(8),
            PanelTone::Inset,
        )
        .padding(8)
        .into()
    }

    fn terminal_tab_bar(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let mut tabs = row![].spacing(6);
        for (index, tab) in self.terminal_tabs.iter().enumerate() {
            let active = index == self.active_terminal_tab;
            tabs = tabs.push(
                tab_button(
                    text(tab.title.clone()).size(13),
                    if active {
                        TabTone::Active
                    } else {
                        TabTone::Inactive
                    },
                )
                .on_press(Message::TerminalTabSelected(index)),
            );
            if active && visible_terminal_tab_close_count(self.terminal_tabs.len(), index) > 0 {
                tabs = tabs
                    .push(tab_button("x", TabTone::Add).on_press(Message::CloseTerminalTab(index)));
            }
        }
        let tabs = tabs
            .push(Space::new().width(Fill))
            .push(
                tab_button(new_terminal_tab_label(), TabTone::Add)
                    .on_press(Message::NewTerminalTab),
            )
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center);

        panel(tabs, PanelTone::Toolbar).padding([5, 8]).into()
    }

    fn shell_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let shell_connected = matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. });
        let terminal_text = self.terminal_view_text();
        let terminal_font_size = self.terminal_font_size_value();
        let terminal_display: Element<'_, Message> = if self.shell_log.is_empty()
            && !shell_connected
            && !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connecting { .. })
        {
            if self.host.trim().is_empty() {
                self.empty_terminal_state()
            } else {
                self.ready_terminal_state()
            }
        } else {
            terminal_scrollable(match self.terminal_view_mode {
                TerminalViewMode::Screen => terminal_canvas(
                    self.terminal.snapshot(),
                    terminal_font_size,
                    self.terminal_search.clone(),
                    self.selected_terminal_rows,
                    self.selected_terminal_cells,
                ),
                TerminalViewMode::History => text(terminal_text)
                    .font(TERMINAL_FONT)
                    .color(TEXT_HIGH)
                    .size(u32::from(terminal_font_size))
                    .shaping(Shaping::Advanced)
                    .into(),
            })
            .into()
        };

        panel(
            column![
                self.terminal_tab_bar(),
                self.terminal_context_menu(),
                mouse_area(
                    panel(terminal_display, PanelTone::TerminalCanvas)
                        .height(Fill)
                        .width(Fill)
                        .padding([9, 14])
                )
                .on_press(Message::StartTerminalSelection)
                .on_move(Message::TerminalPointerMoved)
                .on_release(Message::FinishTerminalSelection)
                .on_right_press(Message::ToggleTerminalContextMenu),
            ]
            .spacing(0),
            PanelTone::TerminalCanvas,
        )
        .height(Fill)
        .width(Fill)
        .padding(0)
        .into()
    }

    fn empty_terminal_state(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        column![
            text("No session").size(20).color(TEXT_HIGH),
            text("Select a host from the sidebar or create a new one.")
                .size(13)
                .color(TEXT_MUTED),
            row![
                button_tone("New host", ButtonTone::Primary).on_press(Message::NewConnection),
                button_tone("Import / export", ButtonTone::Ghost).on_press(
                    Message::CommandPaletteRun(PaletteAction::ToggleImportExport)
                ),
            ]
            .spacing(8)
            .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
        ]
        .spacing(10)
        .into()
    }

    fn ready_terminal_state(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        column![
            text("Ready to connect").size(20).color(TEXT_HIGH),
            text(self.target_label()).size(13).color(TEXT_MUTED),
        ]
        .spacing(10)
        .into()
    }

    fn terminal_context_menu(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        if !self.terminal_context_menu_open {
            return Space::new().height(Length::Fixed(0.0)).into();
        }

        let paste: Element<'_, Message> = match self.shell {
            ShellState::Connected { .. } => button_tone("Paste", ButtonTone::Primary)
                .on_press(Message::PasteClipboardToTerminal)
                .into(),
            _ => button_tone("Paste", ButtonTone::Ghost).into(),
        };
        let copy_selected: Element<'_, Message> =
            if self.selected_terminal_rows.is_some() || self.selected_terminal_cells.is_some() {
                button_tone("Copy selected", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                    .on_press(Message::CopySelectedTerminalLine)
                    .into()
            } else {
                button_tone("Copy selected", ButtonTone::Ghost).into()
            };
        let bracketed_paste: Element<'_, Message> = match self.shell {
            ShellState::Connected { .. } => button_tone("Bracket paste", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::PasteClipboardBracketed)
                .into(),
            _ => button_tone("Bracket paste", ButtonTone::Ghost).into(),
        };

        panel(
            column![
                paste,
                copy_selected,
                button_tone("Copy screen", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                    .on_press(Message::CopyTerminalScreen),
                button_tone("Copy all", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                    .on_press(Message::CopyTerminalOutput),
                bracketed_paste,
                button_tone("Duplicate tab", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                    .on_press(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab),
                button_tone("Clear", ButtonTone::Secondary).on_press(Message::ClearTerminal),
                button_tone("Close", ButtonTone::Ghost)
                    .on_press(Message::ToggleTerminalContextMenu),
            ]
            .spacing(6)
            .width(Length::Fixed(TERMINAL_CONTEXT_MENU_WIDTH)),
            PanelTone::Inset,
        )
        .padding([8, 8])
        .into()
    }

    fn settings_panel(&self) -> Element<'_, Message> {
        let screen = if self.terminal_view_mode == TerminalViewMode::Screen {
            button_tone("Screen selected", ButtonTone::Active)
        } else {
            button_tone("Screen", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::TerminalViewModeChanged(TerminalViewMode::Screen))
        };
        let history = if self.terminal_view_mode == TerminalViewMode::History {
            button_tone("History selected", ButtonTone::Active)
        } else {
            button_tone("History", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                .on_press(Message::TerminalViewModeChanged(TerminalViewMode::History))
        };
        let clear = button_tone("Clear", ButtonTone::Secondary).on_press(Message::ClearTerminal);
        let theme_picker = pick_list(
            AppThemeMode::ALL,
            Some(self.theme_mode),
            Message::ThemeModeChanged,
        )
        .width(Length::Fixed(130.0));
        let auto_reconnect = button_tone(
            if self.auto_reconnect {
                "Auto reconnect on"
            } else {
                "Auto reconnect off"
            },
            if self.auto_reconnect {
                ButtonTone::Active
            } else {
                ButtonTone::Secondary
            },
        )
        .on_press(Message::ToggleAutoReconnect);
        let follow_output = button_tone(
            if self.follow_output {
                "Follow output on"
            } else {
                "Follow output off"
            },
            if self.follow_output {
                ButtonTone::Active
            } else {
                ButtonTone::Secondary
            },
        )
        .on_press(Message::ToggleFollowOutput);
        let capture_keys = button_tone(
            if self.capture_keys {
                "Capture keys on"
            } else {
                "Capture keys off"
            },
            if self.capture_keys {
                ButtonTone::Active
            } else {
                ButtonTone::Secondary
            },
        )
        .on_press(Message::ToggleCaptureKeys);
        let vault_toggle = button_tone(
            if self.vault_details_open {
                "Hide vault"
            } else if self.vault_unlocked {
                "Vault unlocked"
            } else {
                "Vault"
            },
            if self.vault_unlocked {
                ButtonTone::Active
            } else {
                ButtonTone::Secondary
            },
        )
        .on_press(Message::ToggleVaultDetails);

        panel(
            column![
                row![
                    text("Settings").size(18).color(TEXT_HIGH),
                    Space::new().width(Fill),
                    button_tone("Terminal", ButtonTone::Active),
                    button_tone("Connection", ButtonTone::Ghost)
                        .on_press(Message::ToggleAdvancedConnection),
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![
                    text("Appearance")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(76.0)),
                    theme_picker,
                    text_input_tone(
                        "Font size",
                        &self.terminal_font_size,
                        Message::TerminalFontSizeChanged
                    )
                    .width(Length::Fixed(105.0)),
                    text("10-24").size(13).color(TEXT_DIM)
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![
                    text("Behavior")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(76.0)),
                    auto_reconnect,
                    follow_output,
                    capture_keys,
                    text("Direct input is opt-in and releases in text fields.")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_DIM)
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![
                    text("History")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(76.0)),
                    screen,
                    history,
                    text_input_tone(
                        "Search output",
                        &self.terminal_search,
                        Message::TerminalSearchChanged
                    )
                    .id(TERMINAL_SEARCH_INPUT_ID)
                    .width(Length::Fixed(220.0)),
                    button_tone("Copy matches", ButtonTone::Secondary)
                        .on_press(Message::CopyTerminalSearchMatches),
                    clear,
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                row![
                    text("Security")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_MUTED)
                        .width(Length::Fixed(76.0)),
                    vault_toggle,
                    text("Saved passwords use local encrypted storage by default.")
                        .size(13)
                        .color(TEXT_DIM),
                ]
                .spacing(8)
                .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
                self.vault_controls(),
            ]
            .spacing(12),
            PanelTone::Panel,
        )
        .padding(12)
        .into()
    }

    fn send_terminal_bytes(&mut self, bytes: Vec<u8>) -> Task<Message> {
        if bytes.is_empty() {
            return Task::none();
        }

        if let ShellState::Connected { input } = &self.shell {
            let mut input = input.clone();
            return Task::perform(
                async move {
                    input
                        .send(ShellInput::Write(bytes))
                        .await
                        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
                },
                |result| match result {
                    Ok(()) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Output {
                        tab_id: usize::MAX,
                        bytes: Vec::new(),
                    }),
                    Err(_) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed { tab_id: usize::MAX }),
                },
            );
        }

        self.status = "Shell is not connected.".to_string();
        Task::none()
    }

    fn terminal_copy_text(&self) -> String {
        if self.shell_log.is_empty() {
            self.shell_output.clone()
        } else {
            strip_ansi_controls(&self.shell_log)
        }
    }

    fn terminal_view_text(&self) -> String {
        match self.terminal_view_mode {
            TerminalViewMode::Screen => terminal_text_with_search(
                &self.shell_output,
                &self.terminal_search,
                TerminalSearchPresentation::Screen,
            ),
            TerminalViewMode::History => terminal_text_with_search(
                &self.terminal_copy_text(),
                &self.terminal_search,
                TerminalSearchPresentation::History,
            ),
        }
    }

    fn terminal_font_size_value(&self) -> u16 {
        parse_terminal_font_size(&self.terminal_font_size).unwrap_or(DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE)
    }

    fn ui_settings(&self) -> UiSettings {
        UiSettings {
            theme_mode: self.theme_mode.storage_value().to_string(),
            terminal_font_size: self.terminal_font_size_value(),
        }
    }

    fn persist_ui_settings(&mut self) {
        match WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path)
            .and_then(|store| store.save_ui_settings(&self.ui_settings()))
        {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(error) => {
                self.status = format!("Settings save failed: {error}");
                self.append_activity("settings", &self.status.clone());
            }
        }
    }

    fn selected_terminal_text(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.selected_terminal_cells
            .and_then(|selection| {
                terminal_text_for_cell_selection(&self.terminal.snapshot(), selection)
            })
            .or_else(|| {
                self.selected_terminal_rows
                    .and_then(|rows| terminal_text_for_row_range(&self.terminal.snapshot(), rows))
            })
    }

    fn refresh_files(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP list", error);
            }
        };
        let remote_path = self.remote_path.trim().to_string();
        if remote_path.is_empty() {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP list",
                "Remote path is required.".to_string(),
            );
        }

        self.status = format!("Listing remote path {remote_path}");
        self.record_smoke_status("sftp_list_requested");
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::RefreshFiles { route, remote_path })
    }

    fn should_auto_reconnect(&self) -> bool {
        self.auto_reconnect
            && !self.manual_disconnect_requested
            && !self.host.trim().is_empty()
            && self.auth_method().is_ok()
    }

    fn reconnect_shell(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        self.manual_disconnect_requested = false;
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                self.status = format!("Auto reconnect skipped: {error}");
                self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                return Task::none();
            }
        };
        let size = match self.terminal_size() {
            Ok(size) => size,
            Err(error) => {
                self.status = format!("Auto reconnect skipped: {error}");
                self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
                return Task::none();
            }
        };

        let target = route.target.display_target();
        self.status = format!("Reconnecting shell on {target}");
        self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
        self.record_smoke_status("auto_reconnect_requested");
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::ConnectShell { route, size })
    }

    fn select_host(&mut self, index: usize) {
        if self.panel == Panel::Terminal {
            self.save_active_terminal_tab();
        }
        self.selected = Some(index);
        self.pending_delete_host = false;
        if let Some(host) = self.hosts.get(index).cloned() {
            self.host_name = host.name.clone();
            self.host = host.host.clone();
            self.user = host.username.clone().unwrap_or_default();
            self.port = host.port.to_string();
            self.jump_host = jump_choice_for_host(&self.hosts, &host);
            self.restore_auth_fields(&host);
            self.pending_delete_remote = None;
            self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
            self.workspace_tools_open = false;
            self.capture_keys = false;
            self.status = format!("Selected {}", host.display_target());
            self.append_activity("host", &self.status.clone());
            self.save_active_terminal_tab();
        }
    }

    fn select_terminal_tab(&mut self, index: usize) -> Task<Message> {
        if index >= self.terminal_tabs.len() {
            self.status = "Terminal tab is no longer available.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }
        if index == self.active_terminal_tab {
            return Task::none();
        }
        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
        self.restore_terminal_tab(index);
        self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
        self.status = format!("Selected {}.", self.terminal_tabs[index].title);
        Task::none()
    }

    fn close_terminal_tab(&mut self, index: usize) -> Task<Message> {
        if self.terminal_tabs.len() <= 1 {
            self.status = "At least one terminal tab is required.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }
        if index >= self.terminal_tabs.len() {
            self.status = "Terminal tab is no longer available.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }
        let tab_shell = self
            .terminal_tabs
            .get(index)
            .map(|tab| &tab.shell)
            .unwrap_or(&ShellState::Disconnected);
        if !matches!(tab_shell, ShellState::Disconnected) {
            self.status = "Disconnect this shell before closing its tab.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }

        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
        let removed = self.terminal_tabs.remove(index);
        if self.active_terminal_tab >= self.terminal_tabs.len() {
            self.active_terminal_tab = self.terminal_tabs.len() - 1;
        } else if index < self.active_terminal_tab {
            self.active_terminal_tab -= 1;
        }
        self.restore_terminal_tab(self.active_terminal_tab);
        self.status = format!("Closed {}.", removed.title);
        Task::none()
    }

    fn duplicate_terminal_tab(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        self.save_active_terminal_tab();
        let Some(source) = self.terminal_tabs.get(self.active_terminal_tab).cloned() else {
            self.status = "No terminal tab to duplicate.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };
        if source.host.trim().is_empty() {
            self.status = "Enter a host before duplicating this session.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }

        let new_index = self.terminal_tabs.len();
        let mut tab = source.clone_for_duplicate(new_index);
        tab.title = terminal_tab_title(&tab.host_name, &tab.host, new_index);
        self.terminal_tabs.push(tab);
        self.restore_terminal_tab(self.terminal_tabs.len() - 1);
        self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
        self.status = format!("Duplicated session for {}.", self.target_label());
        self.append_activity("terminal", &self.status.clone());
        Task::none()
    }

    fn save_active_terminal_tab(&mut self) {
        let terminal_size = self.terminal_size().unwrap_or(EngineTerminalSize {
            cols: 100,
            rows: 30,
        });
        let selected_host_id = self
            .selected
            .and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index).map(|host| host.id));
        let Some(tab) = self.terminal_tabs.get_mut(self.active_terminal_tab) else {
            return;
        };
        tab.title = terminal_tab_title(&self.host_name, &self.host, self.active_terminal_tab);
        tab.selected_host_id = selected_host_id;
        tab.host_name = self.host_name.clone();
        tab.host = self.host.clone();
        tab.user = self.user.clone();
        tab.port = self.port.clone();
        tab.jump_host = self.jump_host.clone();
        tab.auth_mode = self.auth_mode;
        tab.password = self.password.clone();
        tab.key_path = self.key_path.clone();
        tab.passphrase = self.passphrase.clone();
        tab.terminal_search = self.terminal_search.clone();
        tab.terminal_view_mode = self.terminal_view_mode;
        tab.terminal_context_menu_open = self.terminal_context_menu_open;
        tab.auto_reconnect = self.auto_reconnect;
        tab.follow_output = self.follow_output;
        tab.capture_keys = self.capture_keys;
        tab.manual_disconnect_requested = self.manual_disconnect_requested;
        tab.hovered_terminal_row = self.hovered_terminal_row;
        tab.terminal_selection_anchor = self.terminal_selection_anchor;
        tab.selected_terminal_rows = self.selected_terminal_rows;
        tab.hovered_terminal_cell = self.hovered_terminal_cell;
        tab.terminal_cell_selection_anchor = self.terminal_cell_selection_anchor;
