        tab.selected_terminal_cells = self.selected_terminal_cells;
        tab.shell_output = self.shell_output.clone();
        tab.shell_log = self.shell_log.clone();
        tab.terminal_size = terminal_size;
        tab.shell = self.shell.clone();
    }

    fn restore_terminal_tab(&mut self, index: usize) {
        let Some(tab) = self.terminal_tabs.get(index).cloned() else {
            return;
        };
        self.active_terminal_tab = index;
        self.selected = tab
            .selected_host_id
            .and_then(|host_id| selected_host_index_by_id(&self.hosts, host_id));
        self.host_name = tab.host_name;
        self.host = tab.host;
        self.user = tab.user;
        self.port = tab.port;
        self.jump_host = tab.jump_host;
        self.auth_mode = tab.auth_mode;
        self.password = tab.password;
        self.key_path = tab.key_path;
        self.passphrase = tab.passphrase;
        self.terminal_search = tab.terminal_search;
        self.terminal_view_mode = tab.terminal_view_mode;
        self.terminal_context_menu_open = tab.terminal_context_menu_open;
        self.auto_reconnect = tab.auto_reconnect;
        self.follow_output = tab.follow_output;
        self.capture_keys = tab.capture_keys;
        self.manual_disconnect_requested = tab.manual_disconnect_requested;
        self.hovered_terminal_row = tab.hovered_terminal_row;
        self.terminal_selection_anchor = tab.terminal_selection_anchor;
        self.selected_terminal_rows = tab.selected_terminal_rows;
        self.hovered_terminal_cell = tab.hovered_terminal_cell;
        self.terminal_cell_selection_anchor = tab.terminal_cell_selection_anchor;
        self.selected_terminal_cells = tab.selected_terminal_cells;
        self.shell_output = tab.shell_output;
        self.shell_log = tab.shell_log;
        self.terminal = AlacrittyTerminalBuffer::new(tab.terminal_size);
        self.shell = tab.shell;
        self.pending_delete_host = false;
    }

    fn apply_window_terminal_size(&mut self, window_size: Size) -> Task<Message> {
        if window_size.width < 480.0 || window_size.height < 360.0 {
            return Task::none();
        }

        let size = terminal_size_for_window(window_size, self.terminal_font_size_value());
        let current = self.terminal_size().unwrap_or(EngineTerminalSize {
            cols: 100,
            rows: 30,
        });
        if size == current {
            return Task::none();
        }

        self.terminal_cols = size.cols.to_string();
        self.terminal_rows = size.rows.to_string();
        self.apply_terminal_size(size)
    }

    fn apply_terminal_size(&mut self, size: EngineTerminalSize) -> Task<Message> {
        self.terminal.resize(size);
        self.shell_output = self.terminal.snapshot().render_plain_text();
        self.save_active_terminal_tab();

        if let ShellState::Connected { input } = &self.shell {
            let mut input = input.clone();
            self.status = format!("Terminal auto-resized to {}x{}", size.cols, size.rows);
            self.append_activity("terminal", &self.status.clone());
            return Task::perform(
                async move {
                    input
                        .send(ShellInput::Resize(size))
                        .await
                        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
                },
                |result| match result {
                    Ok(()) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Output {
                        tab_id: usize::MAX,
                        bytes: Vec::new(),
                    }),
                    Err(_) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed { tab_id: usize::MAX }),
                },
            );
        }

        self.status = format!("Terminal window size set to {}x{}", size.cols, size.rows);
        Task::none()
    }

    fn maybe_run_resize_smoke_probe(&mut self) -> Option<Task<Message>> {
        if self.smoke_resize_probe_done || !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. }) {
            return None;
        }

        if !self.smoke_resize_probe_sent {
            self.smoke_resize_probe_sent = true;
            return Some(
                self.send_terminal_bytes(b"printf 'OPENTERM_SIZE_BEFORE='; stty size\n".to_vec()),
            );
        }

        if self.shell_log.contains("OPENTERM_SIZE_BEFORE=") {
            self.smoke_resize_probe_done = true;
            let resize_task = self.apply_window_terminal_size(Size::new(1500.0, 940.0));
            let command_task =
                self.send_terminal_bytes(b"printf 'OPENTERM_SIZE_AFTER='; stty size\n".to_vec());
            self.record_smoke_status("resize_probe_requested");
            return Some(Task::batch([resize_task, command_task]));
        }

        None
    }

    fn maybe_run_input_smoke_probe(&mut self) -> Option<Task<Message>> {
        if self.smoke_input_probe_sent || !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. }) {
            return None;
        }

        if self.smoke_disconnect_reconnect && !self.smoke_reconnect_requested {
            return None;
        }

        let marker = self.smoke_input_marker.clone()?;
        self.smoke_input_probe_sent = true;
        self.record_smoke_status("input_probe_requested");
        Some(self.send_terminal_bytes(
            format!("printf '{}\\n'\n", sh_single_quote(&marker)).into_bytes(),
        ))
    }

    fn maybe_run_overflow_smoke_probe(&mut self) -> Option<Task<Message>> {
        if !self.smoke_overflow_probe
            || self.smoke_overflow_probe_sent
            || !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. })
        {
            return None;
        }

        self.smoke_overflow_probe_sent = true;
        self.record_smoke_status("overflow_probe_requested");
        Some(self.send_terminal_bytes(
            b"for i in $(seq 1 80); do printf 'OPENTERM_OVERFLOW_LINE_%03d\\n' \"$i\"; done; printf 'OPENTERM_OVERFLOW_DONE\\n'\n"
                .to_vec(),
        ))
    }

    fn maybe_run_disconnect_reconnect_smoke(&mut self) -> Option<Task<Message>> {
        if !self.smoke_disconnect_reconnect
            || self.smoke_disconnect_requested
            || !matches!(self.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. })
        {
            return None;
        }

        self.smoke_disconnect_requested = true;
        self.record_smoke_status("disconnect_reconnect_disconnect_requested");
        Some(self.update(Message::DisconnectShell))
    }

    fn maybe_continue_disconnect_reconnect_smoke(&mut self) -> Option<Task<Message>> {
        if !self.smoke_disconnect_reconnect
            || !self.smoke_disconnect_requested
            || self.smoke_reconnect_requested
        {
            return None;
        }

        self.smoke_reconnect_requested = true;
        self.record_smoke_status("disconnect_reconnect_connect_requested");
        Some(self.update(Message::ConnectShell))
    }

    fn apply_shell_event_to_tab(&mut self, tab_id: usize, event: ShellEvent) {
        let Some(tab) = self.terminal_tabs.get_mut(tab_id) else {
            return;
        };
        match event {
            ShellEvent::StartValidated { .. } => {}
            ShellEvent::Ready { input, .. } => {
                if let ShellState::Connecting { .. } = tab.shell {
                    tab.shell = ShellState::Connected { input };
                }
            }
            ShellEvent::Output { bytes, .. } => {
                if !bytes.is_empty() {
                    append_shell_log(&mut tab.shell_log, &bytes, 200_000);
                    tab.shell_output.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes));
                }
            }
            ShellEvent::ExitStatus { code, .. } => {
                self.status = format!("{} shell exited with status {code}", tab.title);
            }
            ShellEvent::Closed { .. } => {
                tab.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                self.status = format!("{} shell closed.", tab.title);
            }
            ShellEvent::Failed { error, .. } => {
                tab.shell = ShellState::Disconnected;
                self.status = format!("{} shell failed: {error}", tab.title);
            }
        }
    }

    fn selected_local_file_path(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        let index = self.selected_local_file?;
        let entry = self.local_files.get(index)?;
        Some(entry.path.clone())
    }

    fn refresh_local_files(&mut self) {
        let path = if self.local_path.trim().is_empty() {
            PathBuf::from(default_local_sftp_path())
        } else {
            PathBuf::from(self.local_path.trim())
        };
        match list_local_files(&path) {
            Ok((root, files)) => {
                self.local_path = root.display().to_string();
                self.local_files = files;
                self.selected_local_file = None;
                self.status = format!("Loaded local directory {}", self.local_path);
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.status = format!("Local directory failed: {error}");
            }
        }
    }

    fn open_selected_local_directory(&mut self) {
        let Some(index) = self.selected_local_file else {
            self.status = "Select a local directory first.".to_string();
            return;
        };
        let Some(entry) = self.local_files.get(index) else {
            self.status = "Selected local entry is no longer available.".to_string();
            return;
        };
        if entry.kind != LocalFileKind::Directory {
            self.status = "Selected local entry is not a directory.".to_string();
            return;
        }
        self.local_path = entry.path.display().to_string();
        self.refresh_local_files();
    }

    fn go_local_parent_directory(&mut self) {
        let path = PathBuf::from(self.local_path.trim());
        let parent = path
            .parent()
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/"));
        self.local_path = parent.display().to_string();
        self.refresh_local_files();
    }

    fn download_selected_file(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let Some(index) = self.selected_remote_file else {
            self.status = "Select a remote file first.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };
        let Some(entry) = self.remote_files.get(index).cloned() else {
            self.status = "Selected remote file is no longer available.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };
        if matches!(entry.kind, openterm_ssh::RemoteFileKind::Directory) {
            self.status = "Select a file to download, not a directory.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }
        let local_path = self.local_path.trim().to_string();
        if local_path.is_empty() {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP download",
                "Local file path is required for download.".to_string(),
            );
        }
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP download", error);
            }
        };

        let summary = format!("Downloading {} to {}", entry.path, local_path);
        self.status = summary.clone();
        self.start_transfer_log(summary);
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::Download {
            route,
            remote_path: entry.path,
            local_path: PathBuf::from(local_path),
        })
    }

    fn upload_local_file(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let local_path = match self.selected_local_file_path() {
            Some(path) => path,
            None => {
                let local_path = self.local_path.trim();
                if local_path.is_empty() {
                    return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                        "SFTP upload",
                        "Local file path is required for upload.".to_string(),
                    );
                }
                PathBuf::from(local_path)
            }
        };
        if local_path.is_dir() {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP upload",
                "Select a local file to upload, not a directory.".to_string(),
            );
        }
        let remote_path = self.remote_path.trim().to_string();
        if remote_path.is_empty() {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP upload",
                "Remote path is required for upload.".to_string(),
            );
        }
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP upload", error);
            }
        };

        let remote_file = join_remote_path(
            &remote_path,
            local_path
                .file_name()
                .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
                .unwrap_or("upload.bin"),
        );
        let summary = format!("Uploading {} to {}", local_path.display(), remote_file);
        self.status = summary.clone();
        self.start_transfer_log(summary);
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::Upload {
            route,
            local_path,
            remote_path: remote_file,
        })
    }

    fn start_smoke_sftp_roundtrip(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP roundtrip", error);
            }
        };
        let marker = format!("OPENTERM_UI_SFTP_ROUNDTRIP_{}", std::process::id());
        let local_source = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("{marker}.upload.txt"));
        let local_download = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("{marker}.download.txt"));
        if let Err(error) = std::fs::write(&local_source, format!("{marker}\n")) {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP roundtrip", error.to_string());
        }
        let remote_dir = format!("/tmp/openterm-ui-sftp-roundtrip-{}", std::process::id());
        self.remote_path = remote_dir.clone();
        self.local_path = local_download.display().to_string();
        self.status = format!("SFTP roundtrip running in {remote_dir}");
        self.start_transfer_log(self.status.clone());
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::SmokeSftpRoundTrip {
            route,
            remote_dir,
            local_source,
            local_download,
        })
    }

    fn open_selected_directory(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let Some(index) = self.selected_remote_file else {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP open",
                "Select a remote directory first.".to_string(),
            );
        };
        let Some(entry) = self.remote_files.get(index) else {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP open",
                "Selected remote entry is no longer available.".to_string(),
            );
        };
        if !matches!(entry.kind, RemoteFileKind::Directory) {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP open",
                "Selected remote entry is not a directory.".to_string(),
            );
        }

        self.pending_delete_remote = None;
        self.remote_path = entry.path.clone();
        self.refresh_files()
    }

    fn go_parent_directory(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        self.pending_delete_remote = None;
        self.remote_path = parent_remote_path(&self.remote_path);
        self.refresh_files()
    }

    fn create_remote_directory(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let name = self.new_name.trim().to_string();
        if name.is_empty() {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP mkdir",
                "New directory name is required.".to_string(),
            );
        }
        let path = join_remote_path(self.remote_path.trim(), &name);
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP mkdir", error);
            }
        };

        let summary = format!("Creating remote directory {path}");
        self.status = summary.clone();
        self.start_transfer_log(summary);
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::CreateDirectory {
            route,
            remote_path: path,
        })
    }

    fn request_delete_selected_remote(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let Some(index) = self.selected_remote_file else {
            self.status = "Select a remote entry first.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };
        let Some(entry) = self.remote_files.get(index).cloned() else {
            self.status = "Selected remote entry is no longer available.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };

        self.status = format!("Confirm delete for remote path {}", entry.path);
        self.pending_delete_remote = Some(entry);
        Task::none()
    }

    fn confirm_delete_remote(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let Some(entry) = self.pending_delete_remote.take() else {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP delete",
                "No remote delete is pending.".to_string(),
            );
        };
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP delete", error);
            }
        };

        let summary = format!("Deleting remote path {}", entry.path);
        self.status = summary.clone();
        self.start_transfer_log(summary);
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::DeleteRemote { route, entry })
    }

    fn rename_selected_remote(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let Some(index) = self.selected_remote_file else {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP rename",
                "Select a remote entry first.".to_string(),
            );
        };
        let Some(entry) = self.remote_files.get(index).cloned() else {
            return self.handle_local_validation_error(
                "SFTP rename",
                "Selected remote entry is no longer available.".to_string(),
            );
        };
        let new_name = self.new_name.trim().to_string();
        if new_name.is_empty() {
            return self
                .handle_local_validation_error("SFTP rename", "New name is required.".to_string());
        }
        let new_path = join_remote_path(self.remote_path.trim(), &new_name);
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("SFTP rename", error);
            }
        };

        let summary = format!("Renaming {} to {}", entry.path, new_path);
        self.status = summary.clone();
        self.start_transfer_log(summary);
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::RenameRemote {
            route,
            old_path: entry.path,
            new_path,
        })
    }

    fn start_transfer_log(&mut self, summary: String) {
        self.active_transfer = Some(summary.clone());
        self.append_transfer_log("started", &summary);
    }

    fn append_transfer_log(&mut self, state: &str, detail: &str) {
        append_transfer_log_line(&mut self.transfer_log, state, detail);
    }

    fn append_activity(&mut self, scope: &str, detail: &str) {
        append_activity_log_line(&mut self.activity_log, scope, detail);
    }

    fn record_smoke_status(&self, state: &str) {
        let Some(path) = &self.smoke_status_path else {
            return;
        };
        let output_tail = smoke_output_tail(&self.shell_log);
        let output_lines = self.shell_log.lines().count();
        let terminal_rows = self
            .terminal_rows
            .trim()
            .parse::<usize>()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| usize::from(self.terminal.snapshot().size.rows));
        let terminal_cols = self
            .terminal_cols
            .trim()
            .parse::<usize>()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| usize::from(self.terminal.snapshot().size.cols));
        let terminal_overflow = output_lines > terminal_rows;
        let selected_host = self
            .selected
            .and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index))
            .map(host_list_title)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "none".to_string())
            .replace(['\n', '\t'], " ");
        let line = format!(
            "state={state}\tshell={}\tpanel={}\tcapture_keys={}\tconnection_editor={}\ttab={}\ttabs={}\thosts={}\tselected_host={}\thost_name={}\ttarget={}\ttheme={}\tfont_size={}\tterminal_cols={}\tterminal_rows={}\toutput_lines={}\tterminal_overflow={}\tstatus={}\toutput_bytes={}\toutput_tail={}\n",
            self.shell.label(),
            self.panel.label(),
            self.capture_keys,
            self.advanced_connection_open,
            self.active_terminal_tab + 1,
            self.terminal_tabs.len(),
            self.hosts.len(),
            selected_host,
            self.host_name.replace(['\n', '\t'], " "),
            self.target_label(),
            self.theme_mode.storage_value(),
            self.terminal_font_size_value(),
            terminal_cols,
            terminal_rows,
            output_lines,
            terminal_overflow,
            self.status.replace(['\n', '\t'], " "),
            self.shell_log.len(),
            output_tail,
        );
        if let Ok(mut file) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(path)
        {
            let _ = file.write_all(line.as_bytes());
        }
    }

    fn mark_ok(&mut self, label: impl Into<String>) {
        self.last_ok = Some(compact_status_label(label.into()));
    }

    fn mark_connection_ok(&mut self, label: impl Into<String>) {
        self.remember_session_password();
        self.mark_ok(label);
        self.persist_selected_last_connected();
    }

    fn remember_session_password(&mut self) {
        if !matches!(self.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password) || self.password.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(key) = self.session_password_key() {
            self.session_passwords.insert(key, self.password.clone());
        }
    }

    fn session_password_key(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let host = self.host.trim();
        let user = self.user.trim();
        let port = self.port.trim();
        if host.is_empty() || user.is_empty() || port.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        Some(format!("{user}@{host}:{port}"))
    }

    fn session_password(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.session_password_key()
            .and_then(|key| self.session_passwords.get(&key).cloned())
            .filter(|password| !password.is_empty())
    }

    fn persist_selected_last_connected(&mut self) {
        let Some(index) = self.selected else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(host) = self.hosts.get(index).cloned() else {
            return;
        };
        let timestamp = current_timestamp_label();
        let mut updated = host;
        updated.last_connected_at = Some(timestamp);

        match WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path).and_then(|store| {
            store.save_host(&updated)?;
            store.list_hosts()
        }) {
            Ok(hosts) => {
                let saved_id = updated.id;
                self.hosts = hosts;
                self.selected = selected_host_index_by_id(&self.hosts, saved_id);
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.append_activity(
                    "host",
                    &format!("Could not update last connection time: {error}"),
                );
            }
        }
    }

    fn connection_parts(&self) -> Result<(ConnectRoute, String, AuthMethod), String> {
        let profile = self.host_profile()?;
        let username = self.user.trim();
        if username.is_empty() {
            return Err("User is required.".to_string());
        }
        let auth = self.auth_method()?;
        let route = self.connect_route(profile, username.to_string(), auth.clone())?;
        Ok((route, username.to_string(), auth))
    }

    fn connect_route(
        &self,
        profile: HostProfile,
        username: String,
        auth: AuthMethod,
    ) -> Result<ConnectRoute, String> {
        let target_options = ConnectOptions {
            username,
            auth,
            trust_unknown_host_keys: false,
            host_key_policy: HostKeyPolicy::ConfirmNew {
                known_hosts: default_known_hosts_path(),
            },
            timeout: Duration::from_secs(10),
        };
        let jump = match profile.jump.as_ref() {
            Some(jump) => {
                let jump_profile = self
                    .hosts
                    .iter()
                    .find(|host| host.id == jump.host_id)
                    .cloned()
                    .ok_or_else(|| "Selected jump host is not saved locally.".to_string())?;
                let jump_username = jump
                    .username
                    .clone()
                    .or_else(|| jump_profile.username.clone())
                    .ok_or_else(|| "Jump host user is required.".to_string())?;
                Some((
                    jump_profile,
                    ConnectOptions {
                        username: jump_username,
                        auth: AuthMethod::AgentOrDefault,
                        trust_unknown_host_keys: false,
                        host_key_policy: HostKeyPolicy::ConfirmNew {
                            known_hosts: default_known_hosts_path(),
                        },
                        timeout: Duration::from_secs(10),
                    },
                ))
            }
            None => None,
        };

        Ok(ConnectRoute {
            target: profile,
            target_options,
            jump,
        })
    }

    fn start_ssh_action(&mut self, action: PendingSshAction) -> Task<Message> {
        self.pending_ssh_action = Some(action.clone());
        self.run_pending_ssh_action(action)
    }

    fn run_pending_ssh_action(&self, action: PendingSshAction) -> Task<Message> {
        match action {
            PendingSshAction::TestConnection(route) => Task::perform(
                async move { test_ssh_connection(route).await },
                Message::ConnectionTestFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::RunDiagnostics(route) => Task::perform(
                async move { run_ssh_diagnostics(route).await },
                Message::DiagnosticsFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::RunCommand { route, command } => Task::perform(
                async move { run_remote_command(route, command).await },
                Message::CommandFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::RefreshFiles { route, remote_path } => Task::perform(
                async move { list_remote_files(route, remote_path).await },
                Message::FilesLoaded,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::Download {
                route,
                remote_path,
                local_path,
            } => Task::perform(
                async move { download_remote_file(route, remote_path, local_path).await },
                Message::FileTransferFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::Upload {
                route,
                local_path,
                remote_path,
            } => Task::perform(
                async move { upload_local_file(route, local_path, remote_path).await },
                Message::FileTransferFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::CreateDirectory { route, remote_path } => Task::perform(
                async move { create_remote_directory(route, remote_path).await },
                Message::FileTransferFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::DeleteRemote { route, entry } => Task::perform(
                async move { delete_remote_path(route, entry).await },
                Message::FileTransferFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::RenameRemote {
                route,
                old_path,
                new_path,
            } => Task::perform(
                async move { rename_remote_path(route, old_path, new_path).await },
                Message::FileTransferFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::SmokeSftpRoundTrip {
                route,
                remote_dir,
                local_source,
                local_download,
            } => Task::perform(
                async move {
                    smoke_sftp_roundtrip(route, remote_dir, local_source, local_download).await
                },
                Message::FileTransferFinished,
            ),
            PendingSshAction::ConnectShell { route, size } => {
                let target = route.target.display_target();
                Task::perform(
                    async move {
                        validate_ssh_route(route.clone()).await?;
                        Ok::<_, SshUiError>((route, size, target))
                    },
                    |result| match result {
                        Ok((route, size, target)) => {
                            Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::StartValidated {
                                route,
                                size,
                                target,
                            })
                        }
                        Err(error) => Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Failed {
                            tab_id: usize::MAX,
                            error,
                        }),
                    },
                )
            }
            PendingSshAction::StartForward { route, forward } => {
                let status = forward_start_status(&forward);
                Task::perform(
                    async move {
                        validate_ssh_route(route.clone()).await?;
                        Ok::<_, SshUiError>((route, forward, status))
                    },
                    |result| match result {
                        Ok((route, forward, status)) => {
                            Message::ForwardEvent(ForwardUiEvent::StartValidated {
                                route,
                                forward,
                                status,
                            })
                        }
                        Err(error) => Message::ForwardEvent(ForwardUiEvent::Failed(error)),
                    },
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fn handle_ssh_error(&mut self, error: SshUiError, context: &str) -> Task<Message> {
        if is_closed_channel_send_error(&error) {
            self.pending_ssh_action = None;
            self.issue = None;
            self.status = "Shell closed.".to_string();
            self.append_activity("shell", &self.status.clone());
            return Task::none();
        }

        match error {
            SshUiError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(challenge) => {
                self.advanced_connection_open = true;
                self.pending_host_key = Some(PendingHostKey {
                    challenge: (*challenge).clone(),
                    action: self.pending_ssh_action.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {
                        panic!("missing pending SSH action for host key challenge")
                    }),
                });
                let Some(pending) = &self.pending_host_key else {
                    self.status = "Host key verification required.".to_string();
                    return Task::none();
                };
                self.status = format!(
                    "Verify host key for {}:{} before connecting.",
                    pending.challenge.host, pending.challenge.port
                );
                self.issue = Some(SshIssue {
                    title: "Host key approval needed".to_string(),
                    detail: format!(
                        "{}:{} presented an unknown host key.",
                        pending.challenge.host, pending.challenge.port
                    ),
                    guidance: "Review the fingerprint below. If it matches the server you expect, trust it and retry the pending action.".to_string(),
                });
                self.append_activity("host-key", &self.status.clone());
                self.record_smoke_status("host_key_required");
                if self.smoke_auto_trust_host_key {
                    self.record_smoke_status("host_key_auto_trust");
                    return self.accept_host_key();
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            SshUiError::Message(message) => {
                self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                self.status = format!("{context}: {message}");
                self.issue = Some(classify_ssh_issue(context, &message));
                self.open_recovery_controls(context, &message);
                self.append_activity("error", &self.status.clone());
                self.record_smoke_status("ssh_error");
                Task::none()
            }
        }
    }

    fn handle_local_validation_error(&mut self, context: &str, message: String) -> Task<Message> {
        self.status = message.clone();
        self.issue = Some(classify_local_issue(context, &message));
        self.open_recovery_controls(context, &message);
        self.append_activity("validation", &self.status.clone());
        Task::none()
    }

    fn open_recovery_controls(&mut self, context: &str, message: &str) {
        let lower = format!("{context} {message}").to_ascii_lowercase();
        let auth_error = lower.contains("password")
            || lower.contains("authentication")
            || lower.contains("auth")
            || lower.contains("username")
            || lower.contains("user is required");
        let key_error = lower.contains("private key")
            || lower.contains("passphrase")
            || lower.contains("identity")
            || lower.contains("key");

        if auth_error {
            self.open_connection_editor();
            if lower.contains("password")
                || lower.contains("authentication")
                || lower.contains("auth")
            {
                self.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
            }
            self.status = format!("{context}: {message}. Enter the password or fix the auth method in the connection editor, then Connect again.");
        }

        if lower.contains("vault") {
            self.open_connection_editor();
            self.vault_details_open = true;
        }

        if key_error && !auth_error {
            self.open_connection_editor();
            self.auth_mode = AuthMode::PrivateKey;
        }

        if lower.contains("jump") {
            self.advanced_connection_open = true;
            self.routing_details_open = true;
        }

        if lower.contains("terminal columns") || lower.contains("terminal rows") {
            self.advanced_connection_open = true;
            self.pty_details_open = true;
        }

        if lower.contains("forward") || lower.contains("bind") {
            self.workspace_tools_open = true;
            self.panel = Panel::Forward;
            self.forward_tools_open = true;
        }

        if lower.contains("sftp") || lower.contains("remote path") || lower.contains("local file") {
            self.panel = Panel::Sftp;
            self.workspace_tools_open = false;
        }
    }

    fn accept_host_key(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        let Some(pending) = self.pending_host_key.take() else {
            self.status = "No host key is waiting for approval.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };
        match pending.challenge.accept() {
            Ok(()) => {
                self.status = format!(
                    "Trusted {}:{}; retrying SSH action.",
                    pending.challenge.host, pending.challenge.port
                );
                self.issue = None;
                self.append_activity("host-key", &self.status.clone());
                self.run_pending_ssh_action(pending.action)
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.status = format!("Could not save host key: {error}");
                self.append_activity("host-key", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
        }
    }

    fn start_forward(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        if !matches!(self.forward, ForwardState::Stopped) {
            self.status = "Forward is already running.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }
        let forward = match self.forward_options() {
            Ok(forward) => forward,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("Forward", error);
            }
        };
        let (route, _, _) = match self.connection_parts() {
            Ok(parts) => parts,
            Err(error) => {
                return self.handle_local_validation_error("Forward", error);
            }
        };

        self.forward_log.clear();
        self.status = forward_start_status(&forward);
        self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
        self.start_ssh_action(PendingSshAction::StartForward { route, forward })
    }

    fn handle_forward_event(&mut self, event: ForwardUiEvent) -> Task<Message> {
        match event {
            ForwardUiEvent::StartValidated {
                route,
                forward,
                status,
            } => {
                self.pending_ssh_action = None;
                self.forward = ForwardState::Starting { route, forward };
                self.status = status;
                self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
            ForwardUiEvent::Ready(mut input) => {
                if let ForwardState::Starting { route, forward } = &self.forward {
                    let route = route.clone();
                    let forward = forward.clone();
                    self.forward = ForwardState::Running {
                        stop: input.clone(),
                    };
                    self.forward_log_line("starting local forward worker");
                    self.append_activity("forward", "Starting forward worker");
                    return Task::perform(
                        async move {
                            input
                                .send(ForwardInput::Start { route, forward })
                                .await
                                .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
                        },
                        |result| match result {
                            Ok(()) => Message::ForwardEvent(ForwardUiEvent::Started),
                            Err(error) => Message::ForwardEvent(ForwardUiEvent::Failed(
                                SshUiError::Message(error),
                            )),
                        },
                    );
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            ForwardUiEvent::Started => {
                self.issue = None;
                self.mark_connection_ok("forward worker");
                self.status = "Forward worker started.".to_string();
                self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
            ForwardUiEvent::Event(ForwardEvent::Stopped)
                if matches!(self.forward, ForwardState::Running { .. }) =>
            {
                self.forward = ForwardState::Stopped;
                self.status = "Forward stopped.".to_string();
                self.forward_log_line("stopped");
                self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
            ForwardUiEvent::Event(event) => {
                let event_line = format_forward_event(&event);
                self.forward_log_line(&event_line);
                self.append_activity("forward", &event_line);
                match event {
                    ForwardEvent::Listening {
                        bind_host,
                        bind_port,
                    } => {
                        self.issue = None;
                        self.mark_connection_ok(format!("forward {bind_host}:{bind_port}"));
                        self.status = format!("Forward listening on {bind_host}:{bind_port}");
                        self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                        self.record_smoke_status("forward_listening");
                    }
                    ForwardEvent::Failed(error) => {
                        self.status = format!("Forward failed: {error}");
                        self.issue = Some(classify_ssh_issue("Forward failed", &error));
                        self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
                Task::none()
            }
            ForwardUiEvent::Failed(error) => {
                self.forward = ForwardState::Stopped;
                self.forward_log_line(&format!("failed {error}"));
                self.append_activity("forward", &format!("Forward failed: {error}"));
                self.handle_ssh_error(error, "Forward failed")
            }
            ForwardUiEvent::Stopped => {
                self.forward = ForwardState::Stopped;
                self.status = "Forward stopped.".to_string();
                self.forward_log_line("stopped");
                self.append_activity("forward", &self.status.clone());
                Task::none()
            }
        }
    }

    fn forward_options(&self) -> Result<ForwardConfig, String> {
        let bind_host = self.bind_host.trim();
        if bind_host.is_empty() {
            return Err("Bind host is required.".to_string());
        }
        let bind_port = parse_port(&self.bind_port, "Bind port")?;
        match self.forward_mode {
            ForwardMode::Local => {
                let remote_host = self.forward_remote_host.trim();
                if remote_host.is_empty() {
                    return Err("Forward remote host is required.".to_string());
                }
                let remote_port = parse_port(&self.forward_remote_port, "Remote port")?;
                Ok(ForwardConfig::Local(LocalForwardOptions {
                    bind_host: bind_host.to_string(),
                    bind_port,
                    remote_host: remote_host.to_string(),
                    remote_port,
                }))
            }
            ForwardMode::Dynamic => Ok(ForwardConfig::Dynamic(DynamicForwardOptions {
                bind_host: bind_host.to_string(),
                bind_port,
            })),
            ForwardMode::Remote => {
                let local_host = self.forward_remote_host.trim();
                if local_host.is_empty() {
                    return Err("Forward local host is required.".to_string());
                }
                let local_port = parse_port(&self.forward_remote_port, "Local port")?;
                Ok(ForwardConfig::Remote(RemoteForwardOptions {
                    bind_host: bind_host.to_string(),
                    bind_port,
                    local_host: local_host.to_string(),
                    local_port,
                }))
            }
        }
    }

    fn forward_log_line(&mut self, line: &str) {
        if !self.forward_log.is_empty() {
            self.forward_log.push('\n');
        }
        self.forward_log.push_str(line);
    }
}

fn terminal_key_bytes(key: Key, modifiers: Modifiers, text: Option<&str>) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    if modifiers.logo() || modifiers.alt() {
        return None;
    }

    if modifiers.shift() {
        if let Key::Character(value) = key.as_ref() {
            if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("c")
                || value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("v")
                || value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("l")
            {
                return None;
            }
        }
    }

    if modifiers.control() {
        if let Key::Character(value) = key.as_ref() {
            let mut chars = value.chars();
            let ch = chars.next()?;
            if chars.next().is_none() {
                let lower = ch.to_ascii_lowercase();
                if lower.is_ascii_alphabetic() {
                    return Some(vec![(lower as u8) - b'a' + 1]);
                }
            }
        }
        return None;
    }

    if let Some(text) = text {
        if !text.is_empty() && !text.chars().any(char::is_control) {
            return Some(text.as_bytes().to_vec());
        }
    }

    match key.as_ref() {
        Key::Named(key::Named::Enter) => Some(b"\n".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::Tab) => Some(b"\t".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::Backspace) => Some(vec![0x7f]),
        Key::Named(key::Named::Delete) => Some(b"\x1b[3~".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::Escape) => Some(vec![0x1b]),
        Key::Named(key::Named::ArrowUp) => Some(b"\x1b[A".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::ArrowDown) => Some(b"\x1b[B".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::ArrowRight) => Some(b"\x1b[C".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::ArrowLeft) => Some(b"\x1b[D".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::Home) => Some(b"\x1b[H".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::End) => Some(b"\x1b[F".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::PageUp) => Some(b"\x1b[5~".to_vec()),
        Key::Named(key::Named::PageDown) => Some(b"\x1b[6~".to_vec()),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn bracketed_paste_bytes(contents: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(contents.len() + 12);
    bytes.extend_from_slice(b"\x1b[200~");
    bytes.extend_from_slice(contents.as_bytes());
    bytes.extend_from_slice(b"\x1b[201~");
    bytes
}

fn terminal_shortcut_message(key: &Key, modifiers: Modifiers) -> Option<Message> {
    if !modifiers.shift() || !(modifiers.command() || modifiers.control() || modifiers.logo()) {
        return None;
    }

    match key.as_ref() {
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("c") => {
            Some(Message::CopyTerminalSmart)
        }
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("v") => {
            Some(Message::PasteClipboardToTerminal)
        }
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("l") => Some(Message::ClearTerminal),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn app_shortcut_message(key: &Key, modifiers: Modifiers) -> Option<Message> {
    let command_like = modifiers.command() || modifiers.logo();
    let safe_control_like = modifiers.control() && modifiers.shift();
    if !command_like && !safe_control_like {
        return None;
    }

    match key.as_ref() {
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("t") => command_like
            .then_some(Message::NewTerminalTab)
            .or_else(|| safe_control_like.then_some(Message::NewTerminalTab)),
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("w") => command_like
            .then_some(Message::CloseTerminalTab(usize::MAX))
            .or_else(|| safe_control_like.then_some(Message::CloseTerminalTab(usize::MAX))),
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("d") => command_like
            .then_some(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab)
            .or_else(|| safe_control_like.then_some(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab)),
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("s") && modifiers.shift() => {
            Some(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp))
        }
        Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("f") && modifiers.shift() => {
            Some(Message::FocusTerminalSearch)
        }
        Key::Character(value) if value == "," => Some(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Settings)),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn is_command_palette_shortcut(key: &Key, modifiers: Modifiers) -> bool {
    if !(modifiers.command() || modifiers.control() || modifiers.logo()) {
        return false;
    }

    matches!(key.as_ref(), Key::Character(value) if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("k"))
}

fn palette_matches(query: &str) -> Vec<PaletteAction> {
    let query = query.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();

    PaletteAction::ALL
        .iter()
        .copied()
        .filter(|action| {
            if query.is_empty() {
                return true;
            }

            action.title().to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&query)
                || action.hint().to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&query)
                || action.search_text().contains(&query)
        })
        .take(8)
        .collect()
}

fn terminal_size_for_window(window_size: Size, font_size: u16) -> EngineTerminalSize {
    let workspace_width = (window_size.width - SIDEBAR_WIDTH).max(480.0);
    let terminal_height = (window_size.height - 190.0).max(240.0);
    let metrics = terminal_metrics(font_size);
    let cols = ((workspace_width - 68.0) / metrics.cell_width)
        .floor()
        .clamp(40.0, 300.0) as u16;
    let rows = ((terminal_height - 52.0) / metrics.line_height)
        .floor()
        .clamp(10.0, 100.0) as u16;

    EngineTerminalSize { cols, rows }
}

fn smoke_output_tail(output: &str) -> String {
    let tail = output
        .chars()
        .rev()
        .take(240)
        .collect::<String>()
        .chars()
        .rev()
        .collect::<String>();

    tail.replace('\n', "\\n")
        .replace('\r', "\\r")
        .replace('\t', " ")
}

fn sh_single_quote(value: &str) -> String {
    format!("'{}'", value.replace('\'', "'\\''"))
}

fn count_openssh_host_blocks(input: &str) -> usize {
    input
        .lines()
        .filter_map(|raw_line| {
            let line = raw_line.trim();
            if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with('#') {
                return None;
            }
            let (key, value) = line.split_once(char::is_whitespace)?;
            key.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Host")
                .then_some(value.trim())
                .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
        })
        .filter(|value| *value != "*" && !value.contains('*') && !value.contains('?'))
        .count()
}

fn terminal_tab_title(host_name: &str, host: &str, index: usize) -> String {
    let name = host_name.trim();
    if !name.is_empty() {
        return name.to_string();
    }
    let host = host.trim();
    if !host.is_empty() {
        return host.to_string();
    }
    format!("Session {}", index + 1)
}

fn host_list_title(host: &HostProfile) -> String {
    let name = host.name.trim();
    if name.is_empty() {
        host.host.trim().to_string()
    } else {
        name.to_string()
    }
}

fn host_list_meta(host: &HostProfile) -> String {
    let target = host.display_target();
    match &host.last_connected_at {
        Some(last_connected_at) if !last_connected_at.trim().is_empty() => {
            format!("{target} - last {}", last_connected_at.trim())
        }
        _ => target,
    }
}

fn visible_terminal_tab_close_count(tab_count: usize, active_index: usize) -> usize {
    if tab_count > 1 && active_index < tab_count {
        1
    } else {
        0
    }
}

fn new_terminal_tab_label() -> &'static str {
    "New tab"
}

fn shell_event_tab_id(event: &ShellEvent) -> usize {
    match event {
        ShellEvent::StartValidated { .. } => usize::MAX,
        ShellEvent::Ready { tab_id, .. }
        | ShellEvent::Output { tab_id, .. }
        | ShellEvent::ExitStatus { tab_id, .. }
        | ShellEvent::Closed { tab_id }
        | ShellEvent::Failed { tab_id, .. } => *tab_id,
    }
}

fn auth_ref_for_fields(auth_mode: AuthMode, key_path: &str) -> AuthRef {
    match auth_mode {
        AuthMode::PrivateKey if !key_path.trim().is_empty() => AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
            path: key_path.trim().to_string(),
            passphrase: None,
        },
        AuthMode::AgentOrDefault => AuthRef::AgentOrDefault,
        _ => AuthRef::AgentOrDefault,
    }
}

fn jump_host_choices(hosts: &[HostProfile], selected: Option<usize>) -> Vec<JumpHostChoice> {
    let selected_host_id = selected.and_then(|index| hosts.get(index).map(|host| host.id));
    std::iter::once(JumpHostChoice::None)
        .chain(hosts.iter().filter_map(|host| {
            if Some(host.id) == selected_host_id {
                return None;
            }
            Some(JumpHostChoice::Host {
                id: host.id,
                label: format!("{} ({})", host.name, host.display_target()),
            })
        }))
        .collect()
}

fn jump_choice_for_host(hosts: &[HostProfile], host: &HostProfile) -> JumpHostChoice {
    let Some(jump) = host.jump.as_ref() else {
        return JumpHostChoice::None;
    };
    hosts
        .iter()
        .find(|candidate| candidate.id == jump.host_id)
        .map(|candidate| JumpHostChoice::Host {
            id: candidate.id,
            label: format!("{} ({})", candidate.name, candidate.display_target()),
        })
        .unwrap_or(JumpHostChoice::None)
}

fn selected_host_index_by_id(hosts: &[HostProfile], id: HostId) -> Option<usize> {
    hosts.iter().position(|host| host.id == id)
}

fn filtered_host_indices(hosts: &[HostProfile], query: &str) -> Vec<usize> {
    let query = query.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    if query.is_empty() {
        return (0..hosts.len()).collect();
    }

    hosts
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter_map(|(index, host)| {
            let name = host.name.to_ascii_lowercase();
            let host_address = host.host.to_ascii_lowercase();
            let username = host
                .username
                .as_deref()
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .to_ascii_lowercase();
            let port = host.port.to_string();
            (name.contains(&query)
                || host_address.contains(&query)
                || username.contains(&query)
                || port.contains(&query)
                || fuzzy_contains(&name, &query))
            .then_some(index)
        })
        .collect()
}

fn parse_terminal_font_size(value: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    value
        .trim()
        .parse::<u16>()
        .ok()
        .filter(|size| (MIN_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE..=MAX_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE).contains(size))
}

fn sanitize_terminal_font_size(value: &str) -> String {
    let trimmed = value.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return String::new();
    }

    let Ok(size) = trimmed.parse::<u16>() else {
        return DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE.to_string();
    };
    size.clamp(MIN_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE, MAX_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE)
        .to_string()
}

fn fuzzy_contains(haystack: &str, needle: &str) -> bool {
    if needle.is_empty() || haystack.contains(needle) {
        return true;
    }

    let mut chars = needle.chars();
    let Some(mut current) = chars.next() else {
        return true;
    };
    for ch in haystack.chars() {
        if ch == current {
            match chars.next() {
                Some(next) => current = next,
                None => return true,
            }
        }
    }
    false
}

fn append_shell_log(log: &mut String, bytes: &[u8], max_chars: usize) {
    log.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes));
    let char_count = log.chars().count();
    if char_count > max_chars {
        let remove_chars = char_count - max_chars;
        let keep_from = log
            .char_indices()
            .nth(remove_chars)
            .map(|(index, _)| index)
            .unwrap_or(0);
        log.drain(..keep_from);
    }
}

fn strip_ansi_controls(input: &str) -> String {
    let mut output = String::with_capacity(input.len());
    let mut chars = input.chars().peekable();

    while let Some(ch) = chars.next() {
        match ch {
            '\x1b' => match chars.peek().copied() {
                Some('[') => {
                    let _ = chars.next();
                    for next in chars.by_ref() {
                        if ('@'..='~').contains(&next) {
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Some(']') => {
                    let _ = chars.next();
                    let mut previous = '\0';
                    for next in chars.by_ref() {
                        if next == '\x07' || (previous == '\x1b' && next == '\\') {
                            break;
                        }
                        previous = next;
                    }
                }
                Some(_) => {
                    let _ = chars.next();
                }
                None => {}
            },
            '\r' => {
                if !matches!(chars.peek(), Some('\n')) {
                    output.push('\n');
                }
            }
            '\x08' => {
                output.pop();
            }
            '\t' | '\n' => output.push(ch),
            ch if ch.is_control() => {}
            ch => output.push(ch),
        }
    }

    output
}

fn forward_start_status(forward: &ForwardConfig) -> String {
    match forward {
        ForwardConfig::Local(forward) => format!(
            "Starting local forward {}:{} -> {}:{}",
            forward.bind_host, forward.bind_port, forward.remote_host, forward.remote_port
        ),
        ForwardConfig::Dynamic(forward) => format!(
            "Starting SOCKS5 forward on {}:{}",
            forward.bind_host, forward.bind_port
        ),
        ForwardConfig::Remote(forward) => format!(
            "Starting remote forward {}:{} -> local {}:{}",
            forward.bind_host, forward.bind_port, forward.local_host, forward.local_port
        ),
    }
}

fn terminal_search_matches(text: &str, query: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let query = query.trim();
    if query.is_empty() {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let query_lower = query.to_ascii_lowercase();
    text.lines()
        .filter(|line| line.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&query_lower))
        .map(str::to_string)
        .collect()
}

fn scroll_terminal_to_bottom() -> Task<Message> {
    operation::snap_to_end(TERMINAL_SCROLL_ID)
}

fn terminal_text_with_search(
    text: &str,
    query: &str,
    presentation: TerminalSearchPresentation,
) -> String {
    let query = query.trim();
    if query.is_empty() {
        return text.to_string();
    }

    let query_lower = query.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let mut rendered = String::new();
    for (index, line) in text.lines().enumerate() {
        if !rendered.is_empty() {
            rendered.push('\n');
        }
        if line.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&query_lower) {
            match presentation {
                TerminalSearchPresentation::Screen => {
                    rendered.push_str(">> ");
                    rendered.push_str(line);
                }
                TerminalSearchPresentation::History => {
                    rendered.push_str(&format!("{:>5}: {}", index + 1, line));
                }
            }
        } else {
            rendered.push_str(line);
        }
    }
    rendered
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct TerminalCellStyle {
    inverse: bool,
    bold: bool,
    underline: bool,
    search_match: bool,
    foreground: Option<TerminalColor>,
    background: Option<TerminalColor>,
}

impl TerminalCellStyle {
    fn from_cell(
        cell: &TerminalCell,
        snapshot: &TerminalSnapshot,
        row_index: usize,
        search_match: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        let cursor = snapshot.cursor.visible
            && snapshot.cursor.row == row_index
            && snapshot.cursor.col == cell.col;

        Self {
            inverse: cell.inverse || cursor,
            bold: cell.bold,
            underline: cell.underline,
            search_match,
            foreground: cell.foreground,
            background: cell.background,
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct TerminalScreenRun {
    text: String,
    style: TerminalCellStyle,
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn terminal_screen_rows(snapshot: &TerminalSnapshot, query: &str) -> Vec<Vec<TerminalScreenRun>> {
    let query = query.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    let mut rendered_rows = Vec::with_capacity(snapshot.cells.len());

    for (row_index, row) in snapshot.cells.iter().enumerate() {
        let mut runs = Vec::new();
        let plain_line = row
            .iter()
            .filter(|cell| !cell.wide_spacer)
            .map(|cell| cell.ch)
            .collect::<String>();
        let search_match = !query.is_empty() && plain_line.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&query);

        let end = row
            .iter()
            .rposition(|cell| {
                cell.ch != ' '
                    || (snapshot.cursor.visible
                        && snapshot.cursor.row == row_index
                        && snapshot.cursor.col == cell.col)
            })
            .map(|index| index + 1)
            .unwrap_or(0);

        for cell in row.iter().take(end) {
            if cell.wide_spacer {
                continue;
            }
            let cursor = snapshot.cursor.visible
                && snapshot.cursor.row == row_index
                && snapshot.cursor.col == cell.col;
            let ch = if cursor && cell.ch == ' ' {
                '█'
            } else {
                cell.ch
            };
            let style = TerminalCellStyle::from_cell(cell, snapshot, row_index, search_match);
            push_terminal_run(&mut runs, style, &ch.to_string());
        }

        rendered_rows.push(runs);
    }

    rendered_rows
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
struct TerminalMetrics {
    cell_width: f32,
    line_height: f32,
}

fn terminal_metrics(font_size: u16) -> TerminalMetrics {
    let font_size = f32::from(font_size);
    TerminalMetrics {
        cell_width: (font_size * 0.62).max(6.0),
        line_height: (font_size * 1.28).max(14.0),
    }
}

const TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING: f32 = 18.0;
const TERMINAL_POINTER_LEFT_PADDING: f32 = 22.0;

fn terminal_row_for_pointer(position: Point, row_count: usize, font_size: u16) -> Option<usize> {
    if row_count == 0 || position.y < TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING {
        return None;
    }

    let metrics = terminal_metrics(font_size);
    let row = ((position.y - TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING) / metrics.line_height).floor() as usize;
    (row < row_count).then_some(row)
}

fn terminal_cell_for_pointer(
    position: Point,
    size: EngineTerminalSize,
    font_size: u16,
) -> Option<TerminalCellPoint> {
    if size.rows == 0
        || size.cols == 0
        || position.x < TERMINAL_POINTER_LEFT_PADDING
        || position.y < TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING
    {
        return None;
    }

    let metrics = terminal_metrics(font_size);
    let row = ((position.y - TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING) / metrics.line_height).floor() as usize;
    let col = ((position.x - TERMINAL_POINTER_LEFT_PADDING) / metrics.cell_width).floor() as usize;

    (row < usize::from(size.rows) && col < usize::from(size.cols))
        .then_some(TerminalCellPoint { row, col })
}

fn normalize_row_range(a: usize, b: usize) -> (usize, usize) {
    if a <= b {
        (a, b)
    } else {
        (b, a)
    }
}

fn normalize_cell_selection(
    selection: TerminalSelection,
) -> (TerminalCellPoint, TerminalCellPoint) {
    let start_key = (selection.start.row, selection.start.col);
    let end_key = (selection.end.row, selection.end.col);
    if start_key <= end_key {
        (selection.start, selection.end)
    } else {
        (selection.end, selection.start)
    }
}

fn row_range_label((start, end): (usize, usize)) -> String {
    if start == end {
        format!("row {} selected", start + 1)
    } else {
        format!("rows {}-{} selected", start + 1, end + 1)
    }
}

fn selection_status_label(rows: (usize, usize)) -> String {
    format!("Selected terminal {}.", row_range_label(rows))
}

fn terminal_line_at_row(snapshot: &TerminalSnapshot, row: usize) -> Option<String> {
    let line = snapshot
        .cells
        .get(row)?
        .iter()
        .map(|cell| cell.ch)
        .collect::<String>()
        .trim_end()
        .to_string();

    (!line.is_empty()).then_some(line)
}

fn terminal_text_for_row_range(
    snapshot: &TerminalSnapshot,
    rows: (usize, usize),
) -> Option<String> {
    let (start, end) = normalize_row_range(rows.0, rows.1);
    let lines = (start..=end)
        .filter_map(|row| terminal_line_at_row(snapshot, row))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();

    (!lines.is_empty()).then(|| lines.join("\n"))
}

fn terminal_text_for_cell_selection(
    snapshot: &TerminalSnapshot,
    selection: TerminalSelection,
) -> Option<String> {
    let (start, end) = normalize_cell_selection(selection);
    let mut lines = Vec::new();

    for row_index in start.row..=end.row {
        let Some(row) = snapshot.cells.get(row_index) else {
            continue;
        };
        let start_col = if row_index == start.row { start.col } else { 0 };
        let end_col = if row_index == end.row {
            end.col
        } else {
            row.len().saturating_sub(1)
        };
        if start_col >= row.len() || start_col > end_col {
            continue;
        }

        let line = row
            .iter()
            .filter(|cell| (start_col..=end_col).contains(&cell.col))
            .filter(|cell| !cell.wide_spacer)
            .map(|cell| cell.ch)
            .collect::<String>()
            .trim_end()
            .to_string();
        if !line.is_empty() {
            lines.push(line);
        }
    }

    (!lines.is_empty()).then(|| lines.join("\n"))
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn push_terminal_run(runs: &mut Vec<TerminalScreenRun>, style: TerminalCellStyle, text: &str) {
    if text.is_empty() {
        return;
    }

    if let Some(last) = runs.last_mut() {
        if last.style == style {
            last.text.push_str(text);
            return;
        }
    }

    runs.push(TerminalScreenRun {
        text: text.to_string(),
        style,
    });
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct TerminalCanvas {
    snapshot: TerminalSnapshot,
    font_size: u16,
    search: String,
    selected_rows: Option<(usize, usize)>,
    selected_cells: Option<TerminalSelection>,
}

fn terminal_canvas(
    snapshot: TerminalSnapshot,
    font_size: u16,
    search: String,
    selected_rows: Option<(usize, usize)>,
    selected_cells: Option<TerminalSelection>,
) -> Element<'static, Message> {
    let (width, height) = terminal_canvas_size(snapshot.size, font_size);

    canvas_widget(TerminalCanvas {
        snapshot,
        font_size,
        search,
        selected_rows,
        selected_cells,
    })
    .width(Length::Fixed(width))
    .height(Length::Fixed(height))
    .into()
}

fn terminal_canvas_size(size: EngineTerminalSize, font_size: u16) -> (f32, f32) {
    let metrics = terminal_metrics(font_size);
    (
        f32::from(size.cols) * metrics.cell_width,
        f32::from(size.rows) * metrics.line_height,
    )
}

fn terminal_row_is_selected(row: usize, selected_rows: Option<(usize, usize)>) -> bool {
    selected_rows
        .map(|rows| {
            let (start, end) = normalize_row_range(rows.0, rows.1);
            (start..=end).contains(&row)
        })
        .unwrap_or(false)
}

fn terminal_cell_is_selected(
    point: TerminalCellPoint,
    selected_cells: Option<TerminalSelection>,
    selected_rows: Option<(usize, usize)>,
) -> bool {
    if let Some(selection) = selected_cells {
        let (start, end) = normalize_cell_selection(selection);
        if point.row < start.row || point.row > end.row {
            return false;
        }
        if start.row == end.row {
            return point.col >= start.col && point.col <= end.col;
        }
        if point.row == start.row {
            return point.col >= start.col;
        }
        if point.row == end.row {
            return point.col <= end.col;
        }
        return true;
    }

    terminal_row_is_selected(point.row, selected_rows)
}

fn terminal_cell_draw_width(cell: &TerminalCell, metrics: TerminalMetrics) -> f32 {
    metrics.cell_width * if cell.wide { 2.0 } else { 1.0 }
}

fn terminal_font_for_char(ch: char, bold: bool) -> Font {
    let base = if ch.is_ascii() {
        TERMINAL_FONT
    } else {
        TERMINAL_FALLBACK_FONT
    };
    if bold {
        Font {
            weight: font::Weight::Bold,
            ..base
        }
    } else {
        base
    }
}

impl canvas::Program<Message> for TerminalCanvas {
    type State = ();

    fn draw(
        &self,
        _state: &Self::State,
        renderer: &iced::Renderer,
        _theme: &Theme,
        bounds: Rectangle,
        _cursor: iced::mouse::Cursor,
    ) -> Vec<canvas::Geometry> {
        let metrics = terminal_metrics(self.font_size);
        let mut frame = canvas::Frame::new(renderer, bounds.size());
        let query = self.search.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();

        for (row_index, row) in self.snapshot.cells.iter().enumerate() {
            let plain_line = row
                .iter()
                .filter(|cell| !cell.wide_spacer)
                .map(|cell| cell.ch)
                .collect::<String>();
            let search_match =
                !query.is_empty() && plain_line.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&query);

            for cell in row {
                if cell.wide_spacer {
                    continue;
                }
                let cursor = self.snapshot.cursor.visible
                    && self.snapshot.cursor.row == row_index
                    && self.snapshot.cursor.col == cell.col;
                let selected = terminal_cell_is_selected(
                    TerminalCellPoint {
                        row: row_index,
                        col: cell.col,
                    },
                    self.selected_cells,
                    self.selected_rows,
                );
                let style =
                    TerminalCellStyle::from_cell(cell, &self.snapshot, row_index, search_match);
                let x = cell.col as f32 * metrics.cell_width;
                let y = row_index as f32 * metrics.line_height;

                if selected || style.inverse || style.background.is_some() || style.search_match {
                    let background = if style.inverse {
                        Color::WHITE
                    } else if selected {
                        Color::from_rgba(0.245, 0.655, 0.600, 0.210)
                    } else if style.search_match {
                        Color::from_rgb8(82, 64, 0)
                    } else {
                        style
                            .background
                            .map(terminal_color_to_iced)
                            .unwrap_or(Color::TRANSPARENT)
                    };
                    frame.fill_rectangle(
                        Point::new(x, y),
                        Size::new(terminal_cell_draw_width(cell, metrics), metrics.line_height),
                        background,
                    );
                }

                if cursor && cell.ch == ' ' {
                    frame.fill_rectangle(
                        Point::new(x, y + metrics.line_height - 3.0),
                        Size::new(terminal_cell_draw_width(cell, metrics), 2.0),
                        Color::WHITE,
                    );
                    continue;
                }
                let ch = cell.ch;
                if ch == ' ' {
                    continue;
                }

                let color = if style.inverse {
                    Color::BLACK
                } else if selected {
                    TEXT_HIGH
                } else {
                    style
                        .foreground
                        .map(terminal_color_to_iced)
                        .unwrap_or(TEXT_HIGH)
                };
                frame.fill_text(canvas::Text {
                    content: ch.to_string(),
                    position: Point::new(x, y),
                    max_width: terminal_cell_draw_width(cell, metrics),
                    color,
                    size: f32::from(self.font_size).into(),
                    line_height: LineHeight::Absolute(metrics.line_height.into()),
                    font: terminal_font_for_char(ch, style.bold),
                    align_x: alignment::Horizontal::Left.into(),
                    align_y: alignment::Vertical::Top,
                    shaping: Shaping::Advanced,
                });
            }
        }

        vec![frame.into_geometry()]
    }
}

fn terminal_color_to_iced(color: TerminalColor) -> Color {
    Color::from_rgb8(color.r, color.g, color.b)
}

fn file_entry_row<'a>(
    selected: bool,
    kind: &'static str,
    name: &'a str,
    size: String,
) -> Element<'a, Message> {
    let color = if selected { ACCENT_STRONG } else { TEXT_MUTED };
    let name = compact_file_name(name, FILE_NAME_DISPLAY_CHARS);

    container(
        row![
            text(kind)
                .font(Font::MONOSPACE)
                .size(12)
                .color(color)
                .wrapping(Wrapping::None)
                .width(Length::Fixed(FILE_KIND_COLUMN_WIDTH)),
            text(name)
                .font(Font::MONOSPACE)
                .size(13)
                .color(color)
                .wrapping(Wrapping::None)
                .width(Fill),
            text(size)
                .font(Font::MONOSPACE)
                .size(13)
                .color(color)
                .wrapping(Wrapping::None)
                .width(Length::Fixed(FILE_SIZE_COLUMN_WIDTH)),
        ]
        .spacing(10)
        .align_y(iced::Alignment::Center),
    )
    .height(Length::Fixed(26.0))
    .width(Fill)
    .into()
}

fn compact_file_name(name: &str, max_chars: usize) -> String {
    let trimmed = name.trim();
    if trimmed.chars().count() <= max_chars {
        return trimmed.to_string();
    }

    let keep = max_chars.saturating_sub(3);
    let mut compact = trimmed.chars().take(keep).collect::<String>();
    compact.push_str("...");
    compact
}

fn sftp_top_actions() -> &'static [&'static str] {
    SFTP_TOP_ACTIONS
}

fn sftp_selection_actions() -> &'static [&'static str] {
    SFTP_SELECTION_ACTIONS
}

fn shell_worker(tab_id: &usize) -> impl iced::futures::Stream<Item = ShellEvent> {
    let tab_id = *tab_id;
    iced::stream::channel(100, async move |mut output| {
        let (sender, mut receiver) = iced_mpsc::channel::<ShellInput>(100);
        let _ = output
            .send(ShellEvent::Ready {
                tab_id,
                input: sender,
            })
            .await;

        while let Some(message) = receiver.next().await {
            let ShellInput::Start {
                route,
                term,
                cols,
                rows,
            } = message
            else {
                continue;
            };

            let (ssh_input_sender, ssh_input_receiver) = mpsc::channel::<PtyInput>(100);
            let (ssh_event_sender, mut ssh_event_receiver) = mpsc::channel::<PtyEvent>(100);

            let bridge = tokio::spawn({
                let mut receiver = receiver;
                async move {
                    while let Some(input) = receiver.next().await {
                        match input {
                            ShellInput::Write(bytes) => {
                                if ssh_input_sender.send(PtyInput::Write(bytes)).await.is_err() {
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                            ShellInput::Resize(size) => {
                                let pty_size = PtySize {
                                    cols: size.cols,
                                    rows: size.rows,
                                };
                                if ssh_input_sender
                                    .send(PtyInput::Resize(pty_size))
                                    .await
                                    .is_err()
                                {
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                            ShellInput::Disconnect => break,
                            ShellInput::Start { .. } => {}
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            let mut shell = Box::pin(tokio::spawn(async move {
                RusshBackend
                    .event_shell_with_route(
                        route,
                        ShellOptions {
                            term,
                            size: PtySize { cols, rows },
                        },
                        ssh_input_receiver,
                        ssh_event_sender,
                    )
                    .await
            }));

            loop {
                tokio::select! {
                    maybe_event = ssh_event_receiver.recv() => {
                        match maybe_event {
                            Some(PtyEvent::Output(bytes)) => {
                                let _ = output
                                    .send(ShellEvent::Output { tab_id, bytes })
                                    .await;
                            }
                            Some(PtyEvent::ExitStatus(code)) => {
                                let _ = output.send(ShellEvent::ExitStatus { tab_id, code }).await;
                            }
                            Some(PtyEvent::Closed) | None => {
                                let _ = output.send(ShellEvent::Closed { tab_id }).await;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    result = &mut shell => {
                        match result {
                            Ok(Ok(code)) => {
                                let _ = output.send(ShellEvent::ExitStatus { tab_id, code }).await;
                                let _ = output.send(ShellEvent::Closed { tab_id }).await;
                            }
                            Ok(Err(error)) => {
                                let _ = output
                                    .send(ShellEvent::Failed {
                                        tab_id,
                                        error: SshUiError::from(error),
                                    })
                                    .await;
                            }
                            Err(error) => {
                                let _ = output
                                    .send(ShellEvent::Failed {
                                        tab_id,
                                        error: SshUiError::Message(error.to_string()),
                                    })
                                    .await;
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            bridge.abort();
            break;
        }
    })
}

fn forward_worker() -> impl iced::futures::Stream<Item = ForwardUiEvent> {
    iced::stream::channel(100, async |mut output| {
        let (sender, mut receiver) = iced_mpsc::channel::<ForwardInput>(100);
        let _ = output.send(ForwardUiEvent::Ready(sender)).await;

        while let Some(input) = receiver.next().await {
