            let ForwardInput::Start { route, forward } = input else {
                continue;
            };

            let (event_sender, mut event_receiver) = mpsc::channel::<ForwardEvent>(100);
            let (stop_sender, stop_receiver) = mpsc::channel::<()>(1);

            let bridge = tokio::spawn({
                let mut receiver = receiver;
                async move {
                    while let Some(input) = receiver.next().await {
                        match input {
                            ForwardInput::Stop => {
                                let _ = stop_sender.send(()).await;
                                break;
                            }
                            ForwardInput::Start { .. } => {}
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            let mut runner = Box::pin(tokio::spawn(async move {
                match forward {
                    ForwardConfig::Local(forward) => {
                        RusshBackend
                            .run_local_forward_with_route(
                                route,
                                forward,
                                event_sender,
                                stop_receiver,
                            )
                            .await
                    }
                    ForwardConfig::Dynamic(forward) => {
                        RusshBackend
                            .run_dynamic_forward_with_route(
                                route,
                                forward,
                                event_sender,
                                stop_receiver,
                            )
                            .await
                    }
                    ForwardConfig::Remote(forward) => {
                        RusshBackend
                            .run_remote_forward_with_route(
                                route,
                                forward,
                                event_sender,
                                stop_receiver,
                            )
                            .await
                    }
                }
            }));

            loop {
                tokio::select! {
                    event = event_receiver.recv() => {
                        match event {
                            Some(ForwardEvent::Stopped) => {
                                let _ = output.send(ForwardUiEvent::Event(ForwardEvent::Stopped)).await;
                                break;
                            }
                            Some(event) => {
                                let _ = output.send(ForwardUiEvent::Event(event)).await;
                            }
                            None => break,
                        }
                    }
                    result = &mut runner => {
                        match result {
                            Ok(Ok(())) => {
                                let _ = output.send(ForwardUiEvent::Stopped).await;
                            }
                            Ok(Err(error)) => {
                                let _ = output
                                    .send(ForwardUiEvent::Failed(SshUiError::from(error)))
                                    .await;
                            }
                            Err(error) => {
                                let _ = output
                                    .send(ForwardUiEvent::Failed(SshUiError::Message(
                                        error.to_string(),
                                    )))
                                    .await;
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            bridge.abort();
            break;
        }
    })
}

impl OpenTerm {
    fn start_new_connection(&mut self) {
        self.selected = None;
        self.pending_delete_host = false;
        self.pending_delete_remote = None;
        self.selected_local_file = None;
        self.selected_remote_file = None;
        self.remote_files.clear();
        self.active_transfer = None;
        self.issue = None;
        self.pending_host_key = None;
        self.pending_ssh_action = None;
        self.host_name.clear();
        self.host.clear();
        self.user = std::env::var("USER").unwrap_or_default();
        self.port = "22".to_string();
        self.jump_host = JumpHostChoice::None;
        self.auth_mode = AuthMode::AgentOrDefault;
        self.password.clear();
        self.passphrase.clear();
        self.key_path = default_key_path();
        self.auto_reconnect = false;
        self.manual_disconnect_requested = false;
        self.remote_path = ".".to_string();
        self.local_path = default_local_sftp_path();
        self.new_name.clear();
        self.panel = Panel::Terminal;
        self.workspace_tools_open = false;
        self.capture_keys = false;
        self.advanced_connection_open = true;
        self.status = "New connection ready. Enter Host or IP, then Connect.".to_string();
        self.append_activity("host", &self.status.clone());
    }

    fn host_profile(&self) -> Result<HostProfile, String> {
        let host = self.host.trim();
        if host.is_empty() {
            return Err("Host is required.".to_string());
        }
        let port = self
            .port
            .trim()
            .parse::<u16>()
            .map_err(|_| "Port must be a number between 1 and 65535.".to_string())?;
        if port == 0 {
            return Err("Port must be greater than zero.".to_string());
        }

        let name = if self.host_name.trim().is_empty() {
            host
        } else {
            self.host_name.trim()
        };
        let mut profile = self
            .selected
            .and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index).cloned())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| HostProfile::new(name, host));
        profile.name = name.to_string();
        profile.host = host.to_string();
        profile.username = (!self.user.trim().is_empty()).then(|| self.user.trim().to_string());
        profile.port = port;
        profile.auth = self.host_auth_ref();
        let selected_host_id = self
            .selected
            .and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index).map(|host| host.id));
        profile.jump = self
            .jump_host
            .id()
            .filter(|id| Some(*id) != selected_host_id)
            .map(|host_id| JumpConfig {
                host_id,
                username: None,
            });
        Ok(profile)
    }

    fn host_profile_preserving_saved_auth(&self) -> Result<HostProfile, String> {
        let mut profile = self.host_profile()?;
        if let Some(saved_auth) = self.saved_auth_to_preserve() {
            profile.auth = saved_auth;
        }
        Ok(profile)
    }

    fn host_profile_for_primary_save(&self) -> Result<HostProfile, String> {
        let mut profile = self.host_profile_preserving_saved_auth()?;

        match self.auth_mode {
            AuthMode::Password if !self.password.is_empty() => {
                let secret = encrypt_gui_secret(self.active_vault_password(), &self.password)?;
                let secret_id = secret.id;
                WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path)
                    .and_then(|store| store.save_secret(&secret))
                    .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
                profile.auth = AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id);
            }
            AuthMode::PrivateKey if !self.passphrase.is_empty() => {
                let secret = encrypt_gui_secret(self.active_vault_password(), &self.passphrase)?;
                let secret_id = secret.id;
                WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path)
                    .and_then(|store| store.save_secret(&secret))
                    .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
                profile.auth = AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
                    path: self.key_path.trim().to_string(),
                    passphrase: Some(secret_id),
                };
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        Ok(profile)
    }

    fn active_vault_password(&self) -> &str {
        if !self.vault_password.is_empty() {
            &self.vault_password
        } else {
            DEFAULT_VAULT_PASSWORD
        }
    }

    fn saved_auth_to_preserve(&self) -> Option<AuthRef> {
        let saved = self.selected.and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index))?;
        match (&self.auth_mode, &saved.auth) {
            (AuthMode::Password, AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id))
                if self.password.is_empty() =>
            {
                Some(AuthRef::PasswordSecret(*secret_id))
            }
            (
                AuthMode::PrivateKey,
                AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
                    path,
                    passphrase: Some(secret_id),
                },
            ) if self.passphrase.is_empty() && self.key_path.trim() == path => {
                Some(AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
                    path: path.clone(),
                    passphrase: Some(*secret_id),
                })
            }
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    fn save_host_profile(&mut self, profile: HostProfile, status_prefix: &str) -> bool {
        match WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path).and_then(|store| {
            let saved_id = profile.id;
            store.save_host(&profile)?;
            let hosts = store.list_hosts()?;
            Ok((saved_id, hosts))
        }) {
            Ok((saved_id, hosts)) => {
                self.hosts = hosts;
                self.selected = selected_host_index_by_id(&self.hosts, saved_id);
                self.pending_delete_host = false;
                self.advanced_connection_open = false;
                self.issue = None;
                self.status = self
                    .selected
                    .and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index))
                    .map(|host| format!("{status_prefix} Selected {}.", host.name))
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| status_prefix.to_string());
                self.append_activity("host", &self.status.clone());
                self.record_smoke_status("host_saved");
                true
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.status = format!("Save failed: {error}");
                self.append_activity("host", &self.status.clone());
                self.record_smoke_status("host_save_failed");
                false
            }
        }
    }

    fn host_auth_ref(&self) -> AuthRef {
        auth_ref_for_fields(self.auth_mode, &self.key_path)
    }

    fn restore_auth_fields(&mut self, host: &HostProfile) {
        match &host.auth {
            AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile { path, passphrase } => {
                self.auth_mode = AuthMode::PrivateKey;
                self.key_path = path.clone();
                self.passphrase = if self.vault_unlocked {
                    passphrase
                        .and_then(|secret_id| self.decrypt_saved_secret(secret_id).ok())
                        .unwrap_or_default()
                } else {
                    String::new()
                };
            }
            AuthRef::AgentOrDefault => {
                self.auth_mode = AuthMode::AgentOrDefault;
            }
            AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id) => {
                self.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
                self.password = if self.vault_unlocked {
                    self.decrypt_saved_secret(*secret_id).unwrap_or_default()
                } else {
                    String::new()
                };
            }
            AuthRef::ManagedPrivateKey(_) => {
                self.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
            }
        }
        if !matches!(host.auth, AuthRef::PasswordSecret(_)) {
            self.password.clear();
        }
        if !matches!(
            host.auth,
            AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
                passphrase: Some(_),
                ..
            }
        ) {
            self.passphrase.clear();
        }
    }

    fn unlock_vault(&mut self) -> Task<Message> {
        if self.vault_password.is_empty() {
            self.vault_unlocked = false;
            self.status = "Vault master password is required.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        }

        let Some(index) = self.selected else {
            self.vault_unlocked = true;
            self.status = "Vault unlocked for new saved secrets.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };
        let Some(host) = self.hosts.get(index).cloned() else {
            self.vault_unlocked = false;
            self.status = "Selected host is no longer available.".to_string();
            return Task::none();
        };

        match self.restore_saved_secret_fields(&host) {
            Ok(restored) => {
                self.vault_unlocked = true;
                self.status = if restored {
                    format!("Vault unlocked for {}.", host.name)
                } else {
                    "Vault unlocked; selected host has no saved secret.".to_string()
                };
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.vault_unlocked = false;
                self.password.clear();
                self.passphrase.clear();
                self.status = format!("Vault unlock failed: {error}");
            }
        }
        Task::none()
    }

    fn restore_saved_secret_fields(&mut self, host: &HostProfile) -> Result<bool, String> {
        match &host.auth {
            AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id) => {
                self.password = self.decrypt_saved_secret(*secret_id)?;
                Ok(true)
            }
            AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
                passphrase: Some(secret_id),
                ..
            } => {
                self.passphrase = self.decrypt_saved_secret(*secret_id)?;
                Ok(true)
            }
            _ => Ok(false),
        }
    }

    fn decrypt_saved_secret(&self, secret_id: openterm_core::SecretId) -> Result<String, String> {
        let secret = WorkspaceStore::open(&self.db_path)
            .and_then(|store| store.get_secret(secret_id))
            .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
            .ok_or_else(|| "Saved secret was not found.".to_string())?;
        let first_password = self.active_vault_password();
        match decrypt_gui_secret(first_password, &secret) {
            Ok(value) => Ok(value),
            Err(error) if first_password != DEFAULT_VAULT_PASSWORD => {
                decrypt_gui_secret(DEFAULT_VAULT_PASSWORD, &secret).map_err(|_| error)
            }
            Err(error) => Err(error),
        }
    }

    fn auth_method(&self) -> Result<AuthMethod, String> {
        match self.auth_mode {
            AuthMode::AgentOrDefault => Ok(AuthMethod::AgentOrDefault),
            AuthMode::Password => {
                let password = if !self.password.is_empty() {
                    Some(self.password.clone())
                } else if let Some(password) = self.session_password() {
                    Some(password)
                } else if self.selected.is_some() {
                    self.selected_saved_password()
                } else {
                    None
                };
                let Some(password) = password else {
                    return Err("Password is required.".to_string());
                };
                Ok(AuthMethod::Password(password))
            }
            AuthMode::PrivateKey => {
                let path = self.key_path.trim();
                if path.is_empty() {
                    return Err("Private key path is required.".to_string());
                }
                Ok(AuthMethod::PrivateKey {
                    path: PathBuf::from(path),
                    passphrase: (!self.passphrase.is_empty()).then(|| self.passphrase.clone()),
                })
            }
        }
    }

    fn selected_saved_password(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let host = self.selected.and_then(|index| self.hosts.get(index))?;
        let AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id) = host.auth else {
            return None;
        };
        self.decrypt_saved_secret(secret_id).ok()
    }

    fn terminal_size(&self) -> Result<EngineTerminalSize, String> {
        let cols = self
            .terminal_cols
            .trim()
            .parse::<u16>()
            .map_err(|_| "Terminal columns must be a number.".to_string())?;
        let rows = self
            .terminal_rows
            .trim()
            .parse::<u16>()
            .map_err(|_| "Terminal rows must be a number.".to_string())?;

        if !(40..=300).contains(&cols) {
            return Err("Terminal columns must be between 40 and 300.".to_string());
        }
        if !(10..=100).contains(&rows) {
            return Err("Terminal rows must be between 10 and 100.".to_string());
        }

        Ok(EngineTerminalSize { cols, rows })
    }
}

async fn run_remote_command(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    command: String,
) -> Result<ExecOutputView, SshUiError> {
    let output = RusshBackend
        .exec_with_route(route, &command)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    Ok(ExecOutputView {
        exit_status: output.exit_status,
        stdout: String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string(),
        stderr: String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string(),
    })
}

async fn test_ssh_connection(route: ConnectRoute) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    let target = route.target.display_target();
    validate_ssh_route(route).await?;
    Ok(format!("SSH connection OK: {target}"))
}

async fn run_ssh_diagnostics(route: ConnectRoute) -> Result<DiagnosticReport, SshUiError> {
    let target = route.target.display_target();
    let mut steps = Vec::new();

    steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
        name: "route",
        ok: true,
        detail: match &route.jump {
            Some((jump, _)) => format!("via jump {} to {}", jump.display_target(), target),
            None => format!("direct to {target}"),
        },
    });

    match TcpStream::connect((route.target.host.as_str(), route.target.port)).await {
        Ok(stream) => {
            drop(stream);
            steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
                name: "tcp",
                ok: true,
                detail: format!("{}:{} reachable", route.target.host, route.target.port),
            });
        }
        Err(error) if route.jump.is_none() => {
            steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
                name: "tcp",
                ok: false,
                detail: error.to_string(),
            });
            return Ok(DiagnosticReport { target, steps });
        }
        Err(error) => {
            steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
                name: "tcp",
                ok: true,
                detail: format!("direct target TCP failed; jump route may still work: {error}"),
            });
        }
    }

    let mut session = match RusshBackend.connect_with_route(route).await {
        Ok(session) => {
            steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
                name: "ssh-auth",
                ok: true,
                detail: "handshake and authentication succeeded".to_string(),
            });
            session
        }
        Err(error) => match error {
            SshError::HostKeyVerificationRequired(_) => return Err(SshUiError::from(error)),
            error => {
                steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
                    name: "ssh-auth",
                    ok: false,
                    detail: error.to_string(),
                });
                return Ok(DiagnosticReport { target, steps });
            }
        },
    };

    match session.exec("printf openterm-diagnostic").await {
        Ok(output) => steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
            name: "exec",
            ok: output.exit_status == 0 && output.stdout == b"openterm-diagnostic",
            detail: format!("exit {}", output.exit_status),
        }),
        Err(error) => steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
            name: "exec",
            ok: false,
            detail: error.to_string(),
        }),
    }

    match session.list_dir(".").await {
        Ok(entries) => steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
            name: "sftp",
            ok: true,
            detail: format!("listed {} entries from .", entries.len()),
        }),
        Err(error) => steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
            name: "sftp",
            ok: false,
            detail: error.to_string(),
        }),
    }

    match session.close().await {
        Ok(()) => steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
            name: "close",
            ok: true,
            detail: "session closed cleanly".to_string(),
        }),
        Err(error) => steps.push(DiagnosticStep {
            name: "close",
            ok: false,
            detail: error.to_string(),
        }),
    }

    Ok(DiagnosticReport { target, steps })
}

async fn validate_ssh_route(route: ConnectRoute) -> Result<(), SshUiError> {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(15), async move {
        let mut session = RusshBackend
            .connect_with_route(route)
            .await
            .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

        session.close().await.map_err(SshUiError::from)?;
        Ok(())
    })
    .await
    .map_err(|_| SshUiError::Message("SSH validation timed out after 15 seconds".to_string()))?
}

async fn list_remote_files(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    remote_path: String,
) -> Result<Vec<RemoteFileEntry>, SshUiError> {
    RusshBackend
        .list_dir_with_route(route, &remote_path)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)
}

async fn download_remote_file(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    remote_path: String,
    local_path: PathBuf,
) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    let bytes = RusshBackend
        .read_file_with_route(route, &remote_path)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    tokio::fs::write(&local_path, bytes)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| SshUiError::Message(error.to_string()))?;

    Ok(format!(
        "Downloaded {remote_path} to {}",
        local_path.display()
    ))
}

async fn upload_local_file(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    local_path: PathBuf,
    remote_path: String,
) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    let bytes = tokio::fs::read(&local_path)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| SshUiError::Message(error.to_string()))?;

    RusshBackend
        .write_file_with_route(route, &remote_path, bytes)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    Ok(format!(
        "Uploaded {} to {remote_path}",
        local_path.display()
    ))
}

async fn smoke_sftp_roundtrip(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    remote_dir: String,
    local_source: PathBuf,
    local_download: PathBuf,
) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    let remote_file = join_remote_path(&remote_dir, "roundtrip.txt");

    RusshBackend
        .create_dir_with_route(route.clone(), &remote_dir)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    upload_local_file(route.clone(), local_source.clone(), remote_file.clone()).await?;
    let entries = list_remote_files(route.clone(), remote_dir.clone()).await?;
    if !entries.iter().any(|entry| entry.path == remote_file) {
        return Err(SshUiError::Message(format!(
            "SFTP roundtrip upload missing from listing: {remote_file}"
        )));
    }
    download_remote_file(route.clone(), remote_file.clone(), local_download.clone()).await?;

    let original = tokio::fs::read(&local_source)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| SshUiError::Message(error.to_string()))?;
    let downloaded = tokio::fs::read(&local_download)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| SshUiError::Message(error.to_string()))?;
    if original != downloaded {
        return Err(SshUiError::Message(
            "SFTP roundtrip downloaded bytes did not match upload".to_string(),
        ));
    }

    let _ = RusshBackend
        .remove_path_with_route(route.clone(), &remote_file, RemoteFileKind::File)
        .await;
    let _ = RusshBackend
        .remove_path_with_route(route, &remote_dir, RemoteFileKind::Directory)
        .await;
    let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(local_source).await;

    Ok(format!(
        "SFTP roundtrip complete: {remote_file} -> {}",
        local_download.display()
    ))
}

async fn create_remote_directory(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    remote_path: String,
) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    RusshBackend
        .create_dir_with_route(route, &remote_path)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    Ok(format!("Created remote directory {remote_path}"))
}

async fn delete_remote_path(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    entry: RemoteFileEntry,
) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    RusshBackend
        .remove_path_with_route(route, &entry.path, entry.kind.clone())
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    Ok(format!("Deleted remote path {}", entry.path))
}

async fn rename_remote_path(
    route: ConnectRoute,
    old_path: String,
    new_path: String,
) -> Result<String, SshUiError> {
    RusshBackend
        .rename_path_with_route(route, &old_path, &new_path)
        .await
        .map_err(SshUiError::from)?;

    Ok(format!("Renamed {old_path} to {new_path}"))
}

fn encrypt_gui_secret(
    vault_password: &str,
    plaintext: &str,
) -> Result<openterm_core::EncryptedSecret, String> {
    LocalVault::new(VaultConfig::default())
        .encrypt_secret(vault_password.as_bytes(), plaintext.as_bytes())
        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
}

fn decrypt_gui_secret(
    vault_password: &str,
    secret: &openterm_core::EncryptedSecret,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let plaintext = LocalVault::new(VaultConfig::default())
        .decrypt_secret(vault_password.as_bytes(), secret)
        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    String::from_utf8(plaintext).map_err(|_| "Saved secret is not UTF-8.".to_string())
}

async fn import_ssh_config_file(
    db_path: PathBuf,
    input_path: PathBuf,
) -> Result<ImportSummary, String> {
    let contents = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&input_path)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| format!("read {}: {error}", input_path.display()))?;
    let imported_hosts = import_openssh_config(&contents);
    let imported = imported_hosts.len();
    let store = WorkspaceStore::open(&db_path).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    for host in &imported_hosts {
        store.save_host(host).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    }
    let hosts = store.list_hosts().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    Ok(ImportSummary {
        imported,
        skipped: count_openssh_host_blocks(&contents).saturating_sub(imported),
        hosts,
    })
}

async fn export_hosts_file(
    hosts: Vec<HostProfile>,
    output_path: PathBuf,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let toml = export_hosts_toml(&hosts).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    if let Some(parent) = output_path
        .parent()
        .filter(|parent| !parent.as_os_str().is_empty())
    {
        tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
            .await
            .map_err(|error| format!("create {}: {error}", parent.display()))?;
    }
    tokio::fs::write(&output_path, toml)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| format!("write {}: {error}", output_path.display()))?;
    Ok(format!(
        "Exported {} hosts to {}",
        hosts.len(),
        output_path.display()
    ))
}

fn remote_file_icon(entry: &RemoteFileEntry) -> &'static str {
    match entry.kind {
        RemoteFileKind::Directory => "dir",
        RemoteFileKind::File => "file",
        RemoteFileKind::Symlink => "link",
        RemoteFileKind::Other => "other",
    }
}

fn remote_file_kind_label(kind: &RemoteFileKind) -> &'static str {
    match kind {
        RemoteFileKind::Directory => "directory",
        RemoteFileKind::File => "file",
        RemoteFileKind::Symlink => "symlink",
        RemoteFileKind::Other => "other",
    }
}

fn local_file_icon(kind: LocalFileKind) -> &'static str {
    match kind {
        LocalFileKind::Directory => "[DIR]",
        LocalFileKind::File => "[FILE]",
        LocalFileKind::Symlink => "[LINK]",
        LocalFileKind::Other => "[OTHER]",
    }
}

fn list_local_files(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<(PathBuf, Vec<LocalFileEntry>), String> {
    let root = if path.is_file() {
        path.parent()
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
    } else {
        path.to_path_buf()
    };
    let read_dir =
        std::fs::read_dir(&root).map_err(|error| format!("read {}: {error}", root.display()))?;
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    for entry in read_dir {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        let path = entry.path();
        let metadata = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        let file_type = metadata.file_type();
        let kind = if file_type.is_dir() {
            LocalFileKind::Directory
        } else if file_type.is_file() {
            LocalFileKind::File
        } else if file_type.is_symlink() {
            LocalFileKind::Symlink
        } else {
            LocalFileKind::Other
        };
        files.push(LocalFileEntry {
            name: entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            path,
            kind,
            size: (kind == LocalFileKind::File).then_some(metadata.len()),
        });
    }
    Ok((root, sorted_local_files(files)))
}

fn sorted_local_files(mut files: Vec<LocalFileEntry>) -> Vec<LocalFileEntry> {
    files.sort_by(|left, right| {
        local_file_sort_rank(left.kind)
            .cmp(&local_file_sort_rank(right.kind))
            .then_with(|| left.name.to_lowercase().cmp(&right.name.to_lowercase()))
            .then_with(|| left.name.cmp(&right.name))
    });
    files
}

fn local_file_sort_rank(kind: LocalFileKind) -> u8 {
    match kind {
        LocalFileKind::Directory => 0,
        LocalFileKind::File => 1,
        LocalFileKind::Symlink => 2,
        LocalFileKind::Other => 3,
    }
}

fn sorted_remote_files(mut files: Vec<RemoteFileEntry>) -> Vec<RemoteFileEntry> {
    files.sort_by(|left, right| {
        remote_file_sort_rank(&left.kind)
            .cmp(&remote_file_sort_rank(&right.kind))
            .then_with(|| left.name.to_lowercase().cmp(&right.name.to_lowercase()))
            .then_with(|| left.name.cmp(&right.name))
    });
    files
}

fn remote_file_sort_rank(kind: &RemoteFileKind) -> u8 {
    match kind {
        RemoteFileKind::Directory => 0,
        RemoteFileKind::File => 1,
        RemoteFileKind::Symlink => 2,
        RemoteFileKind::Other => 3,
    }
}

fn format_bytes(bytes: u64) -> String {
    const KB: u64 = 1024;
    const MB: u64 = KB * 1024;
    const GB: u64 = MB * 1024;

    if bytes >= GB {
        format!("{:.1} GB", bytes as f64 / GB as f64)
    } else if bytes >= MB {
        format!("{:.1} MB", bytes as f64 / MB as f64)
    } else if bytes >= KB {
        format!("{:.1} KB", bytes as f64 / KB as f64)
    } else {
        format!("{bytes} B")
    }
}

fn format_permissions(permissions: u32) -> String {
    format!("{:o}", permissions & 0o7777)
}

fn join_remote_path(parent: &str, file_name: &str) -> String {
    if parent.is_empty() || parent == "." {
        file_name.to_string()
    } else if parent.ends_with('/') {
        format!("{parent}{file_name}")
    } else {
        format!("{parent}/{file_name}")
    }
}

fn parent_remote_path(path: &str) -> String {
    let trimmed = path.trim_end_matches('/');
    if trimmed.is_empty() || trimmed == "." || trimmed == "/" {
        return ".".to_string();
    }
    match trimmed.rsplit_once('/') {
        Some(("", _)) => "/".to_string(),
        Some((parent, _)) if !parent.is_empty() => parent.to_string(),
        _ => ".".to_string(),
    }
}

fn append_transfer_log_line(log: &mut String, state: &str, detail: &str) {
    const MAX_TRANSFER_LOG_CHARS: usize = 12_000;

    if !log.is_empty() {
        log.push('\n');
    }
    log.push('[');
    log.push_str(state);
    log.push_str("] ");
    log.push_str(detail);

    let overflow = log.chars().count().saturating_sub(MAX_TRANSFER_LOG_CHARS);
    if overflow > 0 {
        let byte_index = log
            .char_indices()
            .nth(overflow)
            .map(|(index, _)| index)
            .unwrap_or(log.len());
        log.drain(..byte_index);
    }
}

fn append_activity_log_line(log: &mut String, scope: &str, detail: &str) {
    append_capped_log_line(log, &format!("[{scope}] {detail}"), 20_000);
}

fn append_capped_log_line(log: &mut String, line: &str, max_chars: usize) {
    if !log.is_empty() {
        log.push('\n');
    }
    log.push_str(line);

    let overflow = log.chars().count().saturating_sub(max_chars);
    if overflow > 0 {
        let byte_index = log
            .char_indices()
            .nth(overflow)
            .map(|(index, _)| index)
            .unwrap_or(log.len());
        log.drain(..byte_index);
    }
}

fn classify_ssh_issue(context: &str, message: &str) -> SshIssue {
    let lower = message.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let title = if lower.contains("authentication") || lower.contains("permission denied") {
        "Authentication failed"
    } else if lower.contains("timed out")
        || lower.contains("timeout")
        || lower.contains("connection refused")
        || lower.contains("failed to lookup address")
        || lower.contains("name or service not known")
        || lower.contains("no route")
        || lower.contains("network")
        || lower.contains("operation not permitted")
    {
        "Network or address problem"
    } else if lower.contains("key") || lower.contains("identity") || lower.contains("passphrase") {
        "Key or passphrase problem"
    } else if lower.contains("sftp") || lower.contains("subsystem") {
        "SFTP problem"
    } else if lower.contains("forward")
        || lower.contains("bind")
        || lower.contains("address already")
    {
        "Forwarding problem"
    } else {
        "SSH action failed"
    };

    let guidance = match title {
        "Authentication failed" => {
            "Check the username and selected auth method. If using password auth, open connection settings and enter the server password; if using a key, verify the key path and passphrase."
        }
        "Network or address problem" => {
            "Check host/IP, port, VPN or firewall access, and whether the SSH daemon is listening. Try Test before opening Terminal/SFTP/Forward."
        }
        "Key or passphrase problem" => {
            "Open connection settings, verify the private key path, passphrase, and whether SSH agent/default key auth is appropriate for this server."
        }
        "SFTP problem" => {
            "Confirm the server supports the SFTP subsystem and that the selected user can access the remote path."
        }
        "Forwarding problem" => {
            "Check that the local bind port is free, the bind host is valid, and the remote/local service target is reachable from the SSH server."
        }
        _ => {
            "Review the target, auth method, jump host, and activity log. Run Diagnostics if the same host keeps failing."
        }
    };

    SshIssue {
        title: format!("{title}: {context}"),
        detail: message.to_string(),
        guidance: guidance.to_string(),
    }
}

fn classify_local_issue(context: &str, message: &str) -> SshIssue {
    let lower = message.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let (title, guidance) = if lower.contains("host") && lower.contains("required") {
        (
            "Missing host",
            "Enter a server hostname or IP address in the Host or IP field, or select a saved host from the sidebar.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("user") && lower.contains("required") {
        (
            "Missing username",
            "Enter the SSH username for this server. For the provided test server, use ubuntu.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("port") {
        (
            "Invalid port",
            "Use a numeric TCP port between 1 and 65535. SSH usually listens on 22 unless the server was configured differently.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("password") && lower.contains("required") {
        (
            "Missing password",
            "Open connection settings, keep auth mode on Password, and enter the server password before connecting.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("private key") && lower.contains("required") {
        (
            "Missing private key",
            "Open connection settings and enter the private key path, or switch auth mode to agent/default or password.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("terminal columns") || lower.contains("terminal rows") {
        (
            "Invalid terminal size",
            "Use a terminal size within the allowed range: columns 40-300 and rows 10-100.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("bind") || lower.contains("forward") {
        (
            "Invalid forwarding settings",
            "Check bind host, bind port, and the service target before starting the forward.",
        )
    } else if lower.contains("remote path")
        || lower.contains("local file")
        || lower.contains("new name")
    {
        (
            "Missing file operation input",
            "Fill the required SFTP path or name field, then retry the file operation.",
        )
    } else {
        (
            "Input needs attention",
            "Review the highlighted action inputs and retry once the required values are present.",
        )
    };

    SshIssue {
        title: format!("{title}: {context}"),
        detail: message.to_string(),
        guidance: guidance.to_string(),
    }
}

fn compact_status_label(label: String) -> String {
    const MAX_CHARS: usize = 32;
    if label.chars().count() <= MAX_CHARS {
        return label;
    }
    let mut compact: String = label.chars().take(MAX_CHARS.saturating_sub(1)).collect();
    compact.push('…');
    compact
}

fn current_timestamp_label() -> String {
    let seconds = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|duration| duration.as_secs())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    format!("unix:{seconds}")
}

fn parse_port(value: &str, label: &str) -> Result<u16, String> {
    let port = value
        .trim()
        .parse::<u16>()
        .map_err(|_| format!("{label} must be a number between 1 and 65535."))?;
    if port == 0 {
        return Err(format!("{label} must be greater than zero."));
    }
    Ok(port)
}

fn format_forward_event(event: &ForwardEvent) -> String {
    match event {
        ForwardEvent::Listening {
            bind_host,
            bind_port,
        } => {
            format!("listening {bind_host}:{bind_port}")
        }
        ForwardEvent::ConnectionAccepted { peer } => format!("accepted {peer}"),
        ForwardEvent::ConnectionClosed { peer } => format!("closed {peer}"),
        ForwardEvent::Failed(error) => format!("failed {error}"),
        ForwardEvent::Stopped => "stopped".to_string(),
    }
}

fn default_key_path() -> String {
    std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
        .join(".ssh")
        .join("id_ed25519")
        .display()
        .to_string()
}

fn default_local_sftp_path() -> String {
    std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
        .display()
        .to_string()
}

fn default_known_hosts_path() -> PathBuf {
    if let Some(path) = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_KNOWN_HOSTS") {
        return PathBuf::from(path);
    }

    std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
        .join(".ssh")
        .join("known_hosts")
}

fn default_ssh_config_path() -> String {
    std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
        .join(".ssh")
        .join("config")
        .display()
        .to_string()
}

fn default_export_path() -> String {
    std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
        .join("openterm-hosts.toml")
        .display()
        .to_string()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn terminal_key_bytes_sends_printable_text() {
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("a".into()), Modifiers::NONE, Some("a")),
            Some(b"a".to_vec())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_key_bytes_maps_control_letters() {
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("c".into()), Modifiers::CTRL, None),
            Some(vec![0x03])
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_key_bytes_maps_navigation_keys() {
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Named(key::Named::ArrowUp), Modifiers::NONE, None),
            Some(b"\x1b[A".to_vec())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Named(key::Named::Backspace), Modifiers::NONE, None),
            Some(vec![0x7f])
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_key_bytes_ignores_command_shortcuts() {
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("c".into()), Modifiers::LOGO, Some("c")),
            None
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_shortcuts_copy_and_paste_do_not_send_pty_bytes() {
        let copy_modifiers = Modifiers::CTRL | Modifiers::SHIFT;
        let paste_modifiers = Modifiers::LOGO | Modifiers::SHIFT;

        assert!(matches!(
            terminal_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("c".into()), copy_modifiers),
            Some(Message::CopyTerminalSmart)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            terminal_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("v".into()), paste_modifiers),
            Some(Message::PasteClipboardToTerminal)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            terminal_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("l".into()), copy_modifiers),
            Some(Message::ClearTerminal)
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("c".into()), copy_modifiers, Some("C")),
            None
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("l".into()), copy_modifiers, Some("L")),
            None
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("c".into()), Modifiers::CTRL, None),
            Some(vec![0x03])
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_key_bytes(Key::Character("l".into()), Modifiers::CTRL, None),
            Some(vec![0x0c])
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn smart_terminal_copy_prefers_selection_then_output() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.terminal
            .write_remote_output(b"abcdef\r\n")
            .expect("terminal output should parse");
        app.shell_output = app.terminal.snapshot().render_plain_text();
        app.shell_log = "abcdef\n".to_string();
        app.selected_terminal_cells = Some(TerminalSelection {
            start: TerminalCellPoint { row: 0, col: 1 },
            end: TerminalCellPoint { row: 0, col: 3 },
        });

        let _ = app.update(Message::CopyTerminalSmart);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal selection copied.");

        app.selected_terminal_cells = None;
        let _ = app.update(Message::CopyTerminalSmart);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal output copied.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn app_shortcuts_cover_common_desktop_ssh_workflows_without_stealing_raw_control_keys() {
        assert!(matches!(
            app_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("t".into()), Modifiers::LOGO),
            Some(Message::NewTerminalTab)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            app_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("w".into()), Modifiers::LOGO),
            Some(Message::CloseTerminalTab(usize::MAX))
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            app_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("d".into()), Modifiers::LOGO),
            Some(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab)
        ));
        assert!(app_shortcut_message(&Key::Character("d".into()), Modifiers::CTRL).is_none());
        assert!(matches!(
            app_shortcut_message(
                &Key::Character("f".into()),
                Modifiers::CTRL | Modifiers::SHIFT
            ),
            Some(Message::FocusTerminalSearch)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            app_shortcut_message(
                &Key::Character("s".into()),
                Modifiers::CTRL | Modifiers::SHIFT
            ),
            Some(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp))
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            app_shortcut_message(&Key::Character(",".into()), Modifiers::LOGO),
            Some(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Settings))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn focusing_terminal_search_returns_to_terminal_panel_and_closes_actions() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.panel = Panel::Sftp;
        app.terminal_context_menu_open = true;
        app.capture_keys = true;

        let _ = app.update(Message::FocusTerminalSearch);

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(!app.terminal_context_menu_open);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal search focused.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_output_scroll_task_is_available_for_remote_output() {
        let _ = scroll_terminal_to_bottom();
    }

    #[test]
    fn close_current_tab_shortcut_uses_active_tab() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let _ = app.update(Message::NewTerminalTab);
        let _ = app.update(Message::NewTerminalTab);
        app.host = "one.example".to_string();
        app.restore_terminal_tab(1);
        let _ = app.update(Message::CloseTerminalTab(usize::MAX));

        assert_eq!(app.terminal_tabs.len(), 2);
        assert!(app
            .terminal_tabs
            .iter()
            .all(|tab| tab.host != "one.example"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bracketed_paste_wraps_clipboard_contents() {
        assert_eq!(
            bracketed_paste_bytes("echo ok\n"),
            b"\x1b[200~echo ok\n\x1b[201~".to_vec()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn command_palette_filters_common_ssh_actions() {
        assert!(palette_matches("").len() >= 5);
        assert_eq!(
            palette_matches("sftp"),
            vec![PaletteAction::OpenSftp, PaletteAction::RefreshSftp]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            palette_matches("tunnel"),
            vec![
                PaletteAction::OpenForward,
                PaletteAction::StartForward,
                PaletteAction::StopForward
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn command_palette_shortcut_uses_command_k() {
        assert!(is_command_palette_shortcut(
            &Key::Character("k".into()),
            Modifiers::CTRL
        ));
        assert!(is_command_palette_shortcut(
            &Key::Character("K".into()),
            Modifiers::LOGO
        ));
        assert!(!is_command_palette_shortcut(
            &Key::Character("k".into()),
            Modifiers::SHIFT
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_size_for_window_is_bounded_and_responsive() {
        let small = terminal_size_for_window(Size::new(360.0, 240.0), DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE);
        let large = terminal_size_for_window(Size::new(1600.0, 1000.0), DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE);

        assert!((40..=300).contains(&small.cols));
        assert!((10..=100).contains(&small.rows));
        assert!(large.cols > small.cols);
        assert!(large.rows > small.rows);
        assert!(large.cols <= 300);
        assert!(large.rows <= 100);
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_and_sftp_rows_do_not_depend_on_text_wrapping() {
        assert_eq!(TERMINAL_TEXT_WRAPPING, Wrapping::None);
        assert!(FILE_KIND_COLUMN_WIDTH >= 56.0);
        assert!(FILE_SIZE_COLUMN_WIDTH >= 78.0);
        assert!(terminal_metrics(DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE).line_height >= 14.0);
        assert_eq!(
            compact_file_name("short.txt", FILE_NAME_DISPLAY_CHARS),
            "short.txt"
        );
        let compact = compact_file_name(
            "very-long-file-name-with-spaces-and-中文-that-should-not-wrap.csv",
            24,
        );
        assert!(compact.ends_with("..."));
        assert!(compact.chars().count() <= 24);
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_canvas_size_tracks_cell_grid_exactly() {
        let size = EngineTerminalSize { cols: 80, rows: 24 };
        let metrics = terminal_metrics(DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE);
        let (width, height) = terminal_canvas_size(size, DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE);

        assert_eq!(width, 80.0 * metrics.cell_width);
        assert_eq!(height, 24.0 * metrics.line_height);
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_canvas_tracks_selected_rows_and_wide_cells() {
        let metrics = terminal_metrics(DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE);
        let normal = terminal_test_cell(0, 0, 'a', false, false, false);
        let mut wide = terminal_test_cell(0, 1, '中', false, false, false);
        wide.wide = true;

        assert!(terminal_row_is_selected(2, Some((1, 3))));
        assert!(terminal_row_is_selected(2, Some((3, 1))));
        assert!(!terminal_row_is_selected(4, Some((1, 3))));
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_cell_draw_width(&normal, metrics),
            metrics.cell_width
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_cell_draw_width(&wide, metrics),
            metrics.cell_width * 2.0
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn window_resize_updates_terminal_fields_without_manual_apply() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        let _ = app.update(Message::WindowResized(Size::new(1400.0, 900.0)));

        let expected =
            terminal_size_for_window(Size::new(1400.0, 900.0), app.terminal_font_size_value());
        assert_eq!(app.terminal_cols, expected.cols.to_string());
        assert_eq!(app.terminal_rows, expected.rows.to_string());
        assert!(app.status.contains("Terminal window size set"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_context_menu_toggles_and_clear_closes_it() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.capture_keys = true;

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleTerminalContextMenu);
        assert!(app.terminal_context_menu_open);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert!(!app.terminal_tabs[0].capture_keys);

        let _ = app.update(Message::ClearTerminal);
        assert!(!app.terminal_context_menu_open);
        assert!(app.shell_output.is_empty());
        assert!(app.shell_log.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_cell_selection_counts_as_copyable_context_menu_selection() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.terminal
            .write_remote_output(b"abcdef\r\n")
            .expect("terminal output should parse");
        app.shell_output = app.terminal.snapshot().render_plain_text();
        app.selected_terminal_cells = Some(TerminalSelection {
            start: TerminalCellPoint { row: 0, col: 1 },
            end: TerminalCellPoint { row: 0, col: 3 },
        });

        assert_eq!(app.selected_terminal_text(), Some("bcd".to_string()));
        let _ = app.terminal_context_menu();
    }

    #[test]
    fn duplicate_session_copies_connection_fields_into_disconnected_tab() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.host_name = "prod".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.10".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.port = "2222".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::PrivateKey;
        app.key_path = "/tmp/id_ed25519".to_string();
        app.passphrase = "secret".to_string();
        app.shell_output = "old output".to_string();
        app.shell_log = "old log".to_string();
        app.terminal_context_menu_open = true;
        app.selected_terminal_rows = Some((2, 2));

        let _ = app.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);

        assert_eq!(app.terminal_tabs.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(app.active_terminal_tab, 1);
        assert_eq!(app.host_name, "prod");
        assert_eq!(app.host, "10.0.0.10");
        assert_eq!(app.user, "ubuntu");
        assert_eq!(app.port, "2222");
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::PrivateKey);
        assert_eq!(app.key_path, "/tmp/id_ed25519");
        assert_eq!(app.passphrase, "secret");
        assert!(app.shell_output.is_empty());
        assert!(app.shell_log.is_empty());
        assert!(!app.terminal_context_menu_open);
        assert_eq!(app.selected_terminal_rows, None);
        assert!(matches!(app.shell, ShellState::Disconnected));
        assert!(app.status.contains("Duplicated session"));
    }

        assert_eq!(app.terminal_tabs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Enter a host before duplicating this session.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_host_persists_password_for_direct_reconnect() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-password-required");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "prod password".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.45".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "remote-password".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        let saved = app.hosts.first().expect("host saved");
        let secret_id = match saved.auth {
            AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id) => secret_id,
            ref auth => panic!("expected password secret, got {auth:?}"),
        };
        assert!(app.status.contains("Saved host settings"));
        let secret = WorkspaceStore::open(&path)
            .unwrap()
            .get_secret(secret_id)
            .unwrap()
            .expect("secret saved");
        assert_ne!(secret.ciphertext, b"remote-password");

        let mut reloaded = OpenTerm::load().0;
        reloaded.db_path = path.clone();
        reloaded.hosts = WorkspaceStore::open(&path).unwrap().list_hosts().unwrap();
        reloaded.select_host(0);
        assert!(reloaded.password.is_empty());
        assert!(matches!(
            reloaded.auth_method().unwrap(),
            AuthMethod::Password(password) if password == "remote-password"
        ));

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn encrypt_gui_secret_round_trips_without_plaintext_ciphertext() {
        let secret = encrypt_gui_secret("master", "ssh-password").unwrap();

        assert_ne!(secret.ciphertext, b"ssh-password");
        let plaintext = decrypt_gui_secret("master", &secret).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(plaintext, "ssh-password");
        assert!(decrypt_gui_secret("wrong", &secret).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn jump_host_choices_include_none_and_exclude_selected_host() {
        let jump = HostProfile::new("jump", "jump.example.com");
        let target = HostProfile::new("target", "target.example.com");
        let hosts = vec![jump.clone(), target.clone()];

        assert_eq!(
            jump_host_choices(&hosts, Some(1)),
            vec![
                JumpHostChoice::None,
                JumpHostChoice::Host {
                    id: jump.id,
                    label: "jump (jump.example.com:22)".to_string()
                }
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn jump_choice_for_host_restores_saved_jump_config() {
        let jump = HostProfile::new("jump", "jump.example.com");
        let mut target = HostProfile::new("target", "target.example.com");
        target.jump = Some(JumpConfig {
            host_id: jump.id,
            username: None,
        });

        assert_eq!(
            jump_choice_for_host(
                &[jump.clone(), target],
                &HostProfile {
                    jump: Some(JumpConfig {
                        host_id: jump.id,
                        username: None,
                    }),
                    ..HostProfile::new("edited", "edited.example.com")
                }
            ),
            JumpHostChoice::Host {
                id: jump.id,
                label: "jump (jump.example.com:22)".to_string()
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn selected_host_index_by_id_finds_saved_host_after_sorting() {
        let alpha = HostProfile::new("alpha", "10.0.0.1");
        let target = HostProfile::new("target", "10.0.0.2");
        let target_id = target.id;
        let zulu = HostProfile::new("zulu", "10.0.0.3");
        let hosts = vec![alpha, target, zulu];

        assert_eq!(selected_host_index_by_id(&hosts, target_id), Some(1));
        assert_eq!(selected_host_index_by_id(&hosts, HostId::new()), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_host_selects_persisted_profile_and_restores_connection_fields() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-host-flow");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "prod api".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.42".to_string();
        app.user = "deploy".to_string();
        app.port = "2202".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::PrivateKey;
        app.key_path = "/tmp/prod_id_ed25519".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        assert_eq!(app.hosts.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(app.selected, Some(0));
        assert!(!app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Saved host settings. Selected prod api.");

        app.start_new_connection();
        app.panel = Panel::Forward;
        app.workspace_tools_open = true;
        app.capture_keys = true;
        assert!(app.host.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::AgentOrDefault);

        let _ = app.update(Message::HostSelected(0));

        assert_eq!(app.host_name, "prod api");
        assert_eq!(app.host, "10.0.0.42");
        assert_eq!(app.user, "deploy");
        assert_eq!(app.port, "2202");
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::PrivateKey);
        assert_eq!(app.key_path, "/tmp/prod_id_ed25519");
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Selected deploy@10.0.0.42:2202");

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_password_host_encrypts_secret_and_unlock_restores_it() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-password-vault");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "prod password".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.45".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "remote-password".to_string();
        app.vault_password = "vault-master".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::UnlockVault);
        assert!(app.vault_unlocked);

        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        let saved = app.hosts.first().expect("host saved");
        let secret_id = match saved.auth {
            AuthRef::PasswordSecret(secret_id) => secret_id,
            ref auth => panic!("expected password secret, got {auth:?}"),
        };
        let secret = WorkspaceStore::open(&path)
            .unwrap()
            .get_secret(secret_id)
            .unwrap()
            .expect("secret saved");
        assert_ne!(secret.ciphertext, b"remote-password");

        app.password.clear();
        app.vault_unlocked = false;
        let _ = app.update(Message::HostSelected(0));
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert!(app.password.is_empty());

        assert!(matches!(
            app.auth_method().unwrap(),
            AuthMethod::Password(password) if password == "remote-password"
        ));

        app.vault_password = "vault-master".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::UnlockVault);

        assert!(app.vault_unlocked);
        assert_eq!(app.password, "remote-password");

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_host_preserves_existing_password_secret_for_direct_reconnect() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-password-preserve");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "prod password".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.45".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "remote-password".to_string();
        app.vault_password = "vault-master".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::UnlockVault);
        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);
        let original_auth = app.hosts.first().expect("host saved").auth.clone();
        assert!(matches!(original_auth, AuthRef::PasswordSecret(_)));

        app.password.clear();
        app.host_name = "prod renamed".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        let saved = app.hosts.first().expect("host still saved");
        assert_eq!(saved.name, "prod renamed");
        assert_eq!(saved.auth, original_auth);

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_secret_and_connect_saves_profile_then_starts_shell_flow() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-save-connect");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "prod password".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.45".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();

        app.vault_password = "vault-master".to_string();

    #[test]
    fn save_private_key_passphrase_encrypts_secret_and_unlock_restores_it() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-key-vault");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "prod key".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.46".to_string();
        app.user = "deploy".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::PrivateKey;
        app.key_path = "/tmp/id_ed25519".to_string();
        app.passphrase = "key-passphrase".to_string();
        app.vault_password = "vault-master".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::UnlockVault);
        assert!(app.vault_unlocked);

        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        let saved = app.hosts.first().expect("host saved");
        let secret_id = match &saved.auth {
            AuthRef::PrivateKeyFile {
                path,
                passphrase: Some(secret_id),
            } => {
                assert_eq!(path, "/tmp/id_ed25519");
                *secret_id
            }
            auth => panic!("expected private key passphrase secret, got {auth:?}"),
        };
        let secret = WorkspaceStore::open(&path)
            .unwrap()
            .get_secret(secret_id)
            .unwrap()
            .expect("secret saved");
        assert_ne!(secret.ciphertext, b"key-passphrase");

        app.passphrase.clear();
        app.vault_unlocked = false;
        let _ = app.update(Message::HostSelected(0));
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::PrivateKey);
        assert_eq!(app.key_path, "/tmp/id_ed25519");
        assert!(app.passphrase.is_empty());

        app.vault_password = "vault-master".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::UnlockVault);

        assert!(app.vault_unlocked);
        assert_eq!(app.passphrase, "key-passphrase");

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn selected_host_connect_restores_fields_before_validation() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let mut host = HostProfile::new("password host", "10.0.0.44");
        host.username = Some("ubuntu".to_string());
        host.auth = AuthRef::PasswordSecret(openterm_core::SecretId::new());
        app.hosts = vec![host];
        app.panel = Panel::Forward;
        app.workspace_tools_open = true;
        app.advanced_connection_open = true;
        app.host.clear();
        app.user.clear();
        app.port = "22".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::HostSelected(0));
        let _ = app.update(Message::ConnectShell);

        assert_eq!(app.selected, Some(0));
        assert_eq!(app.host_name, "password host");
        assert_eq!(app.host, "10.0.0.44");
        assert_eq!(app.user, "ubuntu");
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
        assert!(app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert!(app.status.contains("Password is required"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn filtered_host_indices_match_name_target_user_and_fuzzy_text() {
        let mut prod = HostProfile::new("prod-api", "10.0.0.10");
        prod.username = Some("ubuntu".to_string());
        let mut bastion = HostProfile::new("bastion", "jump.internal");
        bastion.username = Some("ops".to_string());
        let hosts = vec![HostProfile::new("local", "127.0.0.1"), prod, bastion];

        assert_eq!(filtered_host_indices(&hosts, ""), vec![0, 1, 2]);
        assert_eq!(filtered_host_indices(&hosts, "api"), vec![1]);
        assert_eq!(filtered_host_indices(&hosts, "ubuntu"), vec![1]);
        assert_eq!(filtered_host_indices(&hosts, "jump"), vec![2]);
        assert_eq!(filtered_host_indices(&hosts, "pdapi"), vec![1]);
        assert!(filtered_host_indices(&hosts, "missing").is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn filtered_host_indices_handles_one_thousand_hosts_quickly() {
        let mut hosts = Vec::with_capacity(1_000);
        for index in 0..1_000 {
            let mut host = HostProfile::new(
                format!("prod-api-{index:04}"),
                format!("10.42.{}.{}", index / 255, index % 255),
            );
            host.username = Some(if index % 2 == 0 { "ubuntu" } else { "deploy" }.to_string());
            hosts.push(host);
        }

        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
        let matches = filtered_host_indices(&hosts, "prod-api-0999");
        let elapsed = started.elapsed();

        assert_eq!(matches, vec![999]);
        assert!(
            elapsed < Duration::from_millis(50),
            "1000-host search took {elapsed:?}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_font_size_is_sanitized_to_supported_range() {
        assert_eq!(parse_terminal_font_size("14"), Some(14));
        assert_eq!(parse_terminal_font_size("9"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_terminal_font_size("25"), None);
        assert_eq!(sanitize_terminal_font_size(""), "");
        assert_eq!(sanitize_terminal_font_size("abc"), "14");
        assert_eq!(sanitize_terminal_font_size("8"), "10");
        assert_eq!(sanitize_terminal_font_size("30"), "24");
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_appearance_updates_theme_and_font_size() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-appearance");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();

        let _ = app.update(Message::ThemeModeChanged(AppThemeMode::Light));
        assert_eq!(app.theme_mode, AppThemeMode::Light);
        assert!(app.status.contains("Light"));

        let _ = app.update(Message::TerminalFontSizeChanged("18".to_string()));
        assert_eq!(app.terminal_font_size, "18");
        assert_eq!(app.terminal_font_size_value(), 18);

        let _ = app.update(Message::TerminalFontSizeChanged("99".to_string()));
        assert_eq!(app.terminal_font_size, "24");
        assert_eq!(app.terminal_font_size_value(), 24);

        let settings = WorkspaceStore::open(&path)
            .unwrap()
            .get_ui_settings()
            .unwrap()
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(settings.theme_mode, "light");
        assert_eq!(settings.terminal_font_size, 24);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn ui_settings_round_trip_through_workspace_store() {
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-app-settings");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        let store = WorkspaceStore::open(&path).unwrap();
        store
            .save_ui_settings(&UiSettings {
                theme_mode: AppThemeMode::Light.storage_value().to_string(),
                terminal_font_size: 19,
            })
            .unwrap();

        let settings = store.get_ui_settings().unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            AppThemeMode::from_storage(&settings.theme_mode),
            AppThemeMode::Light
        );
        assert_eq!(
            sanitize_terminal_font_size(&settings.terminal_font_size.to_string()),
            "19"
        );

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn auto_reconnect_toggle_is_saved_per_terminal_tab() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleAutoReconnect);
        assert!(app.auto_reconnect);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Auto reconnect enabled.");

        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);
        assert!(app.auto_reconnect);
        assert!(app.terminal_tabs[1].auto_reconnect);
    }

    #[test]
    fn follow_output_toggle_is_saved_per_terminal_tab() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        assert!(app.follow_output);

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleFollowOutput);
        assert!(!app.follow_output);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal follow output paused.");

        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);
        assert!(!app.follow_output);
        assert!(!app.terminal_tabs[1].follow_output);

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleFollowOutput);
        assert!(app.follow_output);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal will follow new output.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn capture_keys_toggle_is_off_by_default_and_saved_per_terminal_tab() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleCaptureKeys);
        assert!(app.capture_keys);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal key capture enabled.");

        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);
        assert!(app.capture_keys);
        assert!(app.terminal_tabs[1].capture_keys);

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleCaptureKeys);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal key capture disabled.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_surface_click_enables_capture_only_when_connected() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.hovered_terminal_row = Some(0);

        let _ = app.update(Message::StartTerminalSelection);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert_eq!(app.selected_terminal_rows, None);

        app.shell = ShellState::Connected {
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        };
        let _ = app.update(Message::StartTerminalSelection);

        assert!(app.capture_keys);
        assert!(app.terminal_tabs[0].capture_keys);
        assert_eq!(app.selected_terminal_rows, None);
        assert!(app.status.contains("Terminal focused"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn shell_ready_auto_focuses_terminal_for_typing() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.shell = ShellState::Connecting {
            route: test_connect_route(),
            size: EngineTerminalSize {
                cols: 100,
                rows: 30,
            },
        };
        app.panel = Panel::Sftp;
        app.advanced_connection_open = true;
        app.terminal_context_menu_open = true;
        app.capture_keys = false;

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Ready {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        }));

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(matches!(app.shell, ShellState::Connected { .. }));
        assert!(app.capture_keys);
        assert!(!app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert!(!app.terminal_context_menu_open);
        assert!(app.terminal_tabs[0].capture_keys);
    }

    #[test]
    fn shell_close_releases_terminal_keyboard_capture() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.shell = ShellState::Connected {
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        };
        app.capture_keys = true;
        app.terminal_context_menu_open = true;

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
        }));

        assert!(matches!(app.shell, ShellState::Disconnected));
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert!(!app.terminal_context_menu_open);
        assert!(!app.terminal_tabs[0].capture_keys);
    }

    #[test]
    fn channel_send_error_is_treated_as_closed_shell_not_issue_banner() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.shell = ShellState::Connected {
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        };
        app.capture_keys = true;
        app.issue = Some(classify_ssh_issue("Shell failed", "connection refused"));

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Failed {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
            error: SshUiError::Message("SSH protocol error: Channel send error".to_string()),
        }));

        assert!(matches!(app.shell, ShellState::Disconnected));
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert!(app.issue.is_none());
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Shell closed.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn text_entry_contexts_disable_terminal_key_capture() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.capture_keys = true;

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp));
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert!(!app.terminal_tabs[0].capture_keys);

        app.capture_keys = true;
        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleCommandPalette);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);
        assert!(app.command_palette_open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn shell_output_respects_follow_output_pause() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.follow_output = false;

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Output {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
            bytes: b"hello\n".to_vec(),
        }));

        assert!(app.shell_output.contains("hello"));
        assert!(!app.follow_output);
    }

    #[test]
    fn auto_reconnect_runs_after_unexpected_close_for_key_based_auth() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        app.user = "me".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::AgentOrDefault;
        app.auto_reconnect = true;
        app.shell = ShellState::Connected {
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        };

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
        }));

        assert!(app.status.contains("Reconnecting shell"));
        assert!(!app.manual_disconnect_requested);
    }

    #[test]
    fn auto_reconnect_does_not_run_after_manual_disconnect() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        app.user = "me".to_string();
        app.auto_reconnect = true;
        app.manual_disconnect_requested = true;

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
        }));
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Shell closed.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn auto_reconnect_runs_for_saved_password_auth() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        app.user = "me".to_string();
        app.auto_reconnect = true;
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "secret".to_string();
        app.shell = ShellState::Connected {
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        };

        let _ = app.update(Message::ShellEvent(ShellEvent::Closed {
            tab_id: usize::MAX,
        }));

        assert!(app.status.contains("Reconnecting shell"));
    }

    #[test]

        append_shell_log(&mut log, "四五六".as_bytes(), 4);

        assert_eq!(log.chars().count(), 4);
        assert_eq!(log, "三四五六");
    }

        app.shell_output = "visible".to_string();

        assert_eq!(app.terminal_copy_text(), "red\nplain\n");

            terminal_text_with_search(
                "one\ntwo error\nthree",
                "ERR",

            "one\n    2: two error\nthree"

            terminal_text_with_search(

                "error",

            ),
            "one\n>> two error\nthree"
