    #[test]
    fn terminal_screen_rows_group_adjacent_cells_with_same_style() {
        let snapshot = TerminalSnapshot {
            size: EngineTerminalSize { cols: 4, rows: 1 },
            cursor: openterm_terminal::TerminalCursor {
                row: 0,
                col: 3,
                visible: false,
            },
            cells: vec![vec![
                terminal_test_cell(0, 0, 'a', false, true, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 1, 'b', false, true, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 2, 'c', true, true, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
            ]],
        };

        let rows = terminal_screen_rows(&snapshot, "");
        let runs = &rows[0];

        assert_eq!(
            runs.as_slice(),
            [
                TerminalScreenRun {
                    text: "ab".to_string(),
                    style: TerminalCellStyle {
                        inverse: false,
                        bold: true,
                        underline: false,
                        search_match: false,
                        foreground: None,
                        background: None,
                    }
                },
                TerminalScreenRun {
                    text: "c".to_string(),
                    style: TerminalCellStyle {
                        inverse: true,
                        bold: true,
                        underline: false,
                        search_match: false,
                        foreground: None,
                        background: None,
                    }
                }
            ]
            .as_slice()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_screen_rows_marks_cursor_without_shifting_columns() {
        let snapshot = TerminalSnapshot {
            size: EngineTerminalSize { cols: 5, rows: 1 },
            cursor: openterm_terminal::TerminalCursor {
                row: 0,
                col: 3,
                visible: true,
            },
            cells: vec![vec![
                terminal_test_cell(0, 0, 'e', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 1, 'r', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 2, 'r', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 4, ' ', false, false, false),
            ]],
        };

        let rows = terminal_screen_rows(&snapshot, "ERR");
        let runs = &rows[0];

        assert_eq!(runs[0].text, "err");
        assert!(runs[0].style.search_match);
        assert_eq!(runs[1].text, "█");
        assert!(runs[1].style.inverse);
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_canvas_cursor_uses_shape_not_font_glyph_for_blank_cell() {
        assert_eq!(terminal_font_for_char('a', false), TERMINAL_FONT);
        assert_eq!(terminal_font_for_char('中', false), TERMINAL_FALLBACK_FONT);
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_font_for_char('中', true).weight,
            font::Weight::Bold
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_screen_search_does_not_insert_marker_columns() {
        let snapshot = TerminalSnapshot {
            size: EngineTerminalSize { cols: 5, rows: 1 },
            cursor: openterm_terminal::TerminalCursor {
                row: 0,
                col: 4,
                visible: false,
            },
            cells: vec![vec![
                terminal_test_cell(0, 0, 'e', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 1, 'r', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 2, 'r', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                terminal_test_cell(0, 4, ' ', false, false, false),
            ]],
        };

        let rows = terminal_screen_rows(&snapshot, "err");
        let rendered = rows[0]
            .iter()
            .map(|run| run.text.as_str())
            .collect::<String>();

        assert_eq!(rendered, "err");
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_row_for_pointer_respects_padding_and_bounds() {
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_row_for_pointer(Point::new(20.0, 4.0), 3, DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE),
            None
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_row_for_pointer(Point::new(20.0, 18.0), 3, DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE),
            Some(0)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_row_for_pointer(Point::new(20.0, 36.0), 3, DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE),
            Some(1)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_row_for_pointer(Point::new(20.0, 80.0), 3, DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE),
            None
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_cell_for_pointer_maps_to_grid_with_padding() {
        let metrics = terminal_metrics(DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE);
        let size = EngineTerminalSize { cols: 5, rows: 3 };

        assert_eq!(
            terminal_cell_for_pointer(
                Point::new(
                    TERMINAL_POINTER_LEFT_PADDING + metrics.cell_width * 2.0 + 1.0,
                    TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING + metrics.line_height + 1.0
                ),
                size,
                DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE
            ),
            Some(TerminalCellPoint { row: 1, col: 2 })
        );
        assert_eq!(
            terminal_cell_for_pointer(
                Point::new(
                    TERMINAL_POINTER_LEFT_PADDING - 1.0,
                    TERMINAL_POINTER_TOP_PADDING
                ),
                size,
                DEFAULT_TERMINAL_FONT_SIZE
            ),
            None
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_line_at_row_trims_and_ignores_blank_lines() {
        let snapshot = TerminalSnapshot {
            size: EngineTerminalSize { cols: 4, rows: 2 },
            cursor: openterm_terminal::TerminalCursor {
                row: 0,
                col: 0,
                visible: false,
            },
            cells: vec![
                vec![
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 0, 'o', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 1, 'k', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 2, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                ],
                vec![
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 0, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 1, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 2, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                ],
            ],
        };

        assert_eq!(terminal_line_at_row(&snapshot, 0), Some("ok".to_string()));
        assert_eq!(terminal_line_at_row(&snapshot, 1), None);
        assert_eq!(terminal_line_at_row(&snapshot, 2), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_text_for_row_range_joins_non_blank_lines() {
        let snapshot = TerminalSnapshot {
            size: EngineTerminalSize { cols: 5, rows: 3 },
            cursor: openterm_terminal::TerminalCursor {
                row: 0,
                col: 0,
                visible: false,
            },
            cells: vec![
                vec![
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 0, 'o', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 1, 'n', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 2, 'e', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(0, 4, ' ', false, false, false),
                ],
                vec![
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 0, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 1, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 2, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(1, 4, ' ', false, false, false),
                ],
                vec![
                    terminal_test_cell(2, 0, 't', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(2, 1, 'w', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(2, 2, 'o', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(2, 3, ' ', false, false, false),
                    terminal_test_cell(2, 4, ' ', false, false, false),
                ],
            ],
        };

        assert_eq!(
            terminal_text_for_row_range(&snapshot, (2, 0)),
            Some("one\ntwo".to_string())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_text_for_cell_selection_copies_partial_rows() {
        let snapshot = TerminalSnapshot {
            size: EngineTerminalSize { cols: 6, rows: 2 },
            cursor: openterm_terminal::TerminalCursor {
                row: 0,
                col: 0,
                visible: false,
            },
            cells: vec![
                "abcdef"
                    .chars()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(col, ch)| terminal_test_cell(0, col, ch, false, false, false))
                    .collect(),
                "ghijkl"
                    .chars()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(col, ch)| terminal_test_cell(1, col, ch, false, false, false))
                    .collect(),
            ],
        };

        assert_eq!(
            terminal_text_for_cell_selection(
                &snapshot,
                TerminalSelection {
                    start: TerminalCellPoint { row: 0, col: 2 },
                    end: TerminalCellPoint { row: 1, col: 3 },
                }
            ),
            Some("cdef\nghij".to_string())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_cell_selection_normalizes_reverse_drag() {
        let selection = TerminalSelection {
            start: TerminalCellPoint { row: 2, col: 4 },
            end: TerminalCellPoint { row: 1, col: 3 },
        };
        let (start, end) = normalize_cell_selection(selection);

        assert_eq!(start, TerminalCellPoint { row: 1, col: 3 });
        assert_eq!(end, TerminalCellPoint { row: 2, col: 4 });
        assert!(terminal_cell_is_selected(
            TerminalCellPoint { row: 1, col: 4 },
            Some(selection),
            None
        ));
        assert!(!terminal_cell_is_selected(
            TerminalCellPoint { row: 1, col: 2 },
            Some(selection),
            None
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn selected_terminal_rows_copy_selection_and_close_menu() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.terminal
            .write_remote_output(b"first line\r\nsecond line\r\nthird line\r\n")
            .expect("terminal output should parse");
        app.shell_output = app.terminal.snapshot().render_plain_text();
        app.terminal_context_menu_open = true;
        app.hovered_terminal_row = Some(0);

        let _ = app.update(Message::StartTerminalSelection);
        app.hovered_terminal_row = Some(1);
        let _ = app.update(Message::TerminalPointerMoved(Point::new(20.0, 36.0)));
        let _ = app.update(Message::FinishTerminalSelection);
        assert_eq!(app.selected_terminal_rows, Some((0, 1)));

        let _ = app.update(Message::CopySelectedTerminalLine);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Terminal selection copied.");
        assert!(!app.terminal_context_menu_open);
    }

    fn terminal_test_cell(
        row: usize,
        col: usize,
        ch: char,
        inverse: bool,
        bold: bool,
        underline: bool,
    ) -> TerminalCell {
        TerminalCell {
            row,
            col,
            ch,
            wide: false,
            wide_spacer: false,
            inverse,
            bold,
            underline,
            foreground: None,
            background: None,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn join_remote_path_handles_common_parent_forms() {
        assert_eq!(join_remote_path(".", "file.txt"), "file.txt");
        assert_eq!(join_remote_path("/tmp", "file.txt"), "/tmp/file.txt");
        assert_eq!(join_remote_path("/tmp/", "file.txt"), "/tmp/file.txt");
    }

    #[test]
    fn sorted_remote_files_places_directories_first_then_names() {
        let files = vec![
            remote_test_entry("z.txt", RemoteFileKind::File),
            remote_test_entry("bin", RemoteFileKind::Directory),
            remote_test_entry("a.txt", RemoteFileKind::File),
            remote_test_entry("link", RemoteFileKind::Symlink),
        ];

        let names: Vec<_> = sorted_remote_files(files)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|entry| entry.name)
            .collect();

        assert_eq!(names, vec!["bin", "a.txt", "z.txt", "link"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn sorted_local_files_places_directories_first_then_names() {
        let files = vec![
            local_test_entry("z.txt", LocalFileKind::File),
            local_test_entry("bin", LocalFileKind::Directory),
            local_test_entry("a.txt", LocalFileKind::File),
            local_test_entry("link", LocalFileKind::Symlink),
        ];

        let names: Vec<_> = sorted_local_files(files)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|entry| entry.name)
            .collect();

        assert_eq!(names, vec!["bin", "a.txt", "z.txt", "link"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn selected_local_file_path_uses_current_selection() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.local_files = vec![local_test_entry("upload.txt", LocalFileKind::File)];
        app.selected_local_file = Some(0);

        assert_eq!(
            app.selected_local_file_path(),
            Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp/upload.txt"))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn remote_file_labels_and_permissions_are_stable() {
        assert_eq!(
            remote_file_kind_label(&RemoteFileKind::Directory),
            "directory"
        );
        assert_eq!(remote_file_kind_label(&RemoteFileKind::Symlink), "symlink");
        assert_eq!(format_permissions(0o100755), "755");
        assert_eq!(format_permissions(0o40700), "700");
    }

    fn remote_test_entry(name: &str, kind: RemoteFileKind) -> RemoteFileEntry {
        RemoteFileEntry {
            name: name.to_string(),
            path: name.to_string(),
            kind,
            size: None,
            permissions: None,
            modified: None,
        }
    }

    fn local_test_entry(name: &str, kind: LocalFileKind) -> LocalFileEntry {
        LocalFileEntry {
            name: name.to_string(),
            path: PathBuf::from("/tmp").join(name),
            kind,
            size: (kind == LocalFileKind::File).then_some(10),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn parent_remote_path_handles_root_and_nested_paths() {
        assert_eq!(parent_remote_path("."), ".");
        assert_eq!(parent_remote_path("/"), ".");
        assert_eq!(parent_remote_path("/tmp/app"), "/tmp");
        assert_eq!(parent_remote_path("tmp/app"), "tmp");
        assert_eq!(parent_remote_path("tmp"), ".");
    }

    #[test]
    fn transfer_log_appends_status_lines() {
        let mut log = String::new();

        append_transfer_log_line(&mut log, "started", "Uploading a.txt");
        append_transfer_log_line(&mut log, "done", "Uploaded a.txt");

        assert_eq!(log, "[started] Uploading a.txt\n[done] Uploaded a.txt");
    }

    #[test]
    fn transfer_log_caps_without_splitting_unicode() {
        let mut log = "一".repeat(12_010);

        append_transfer_log_line(&mut log, "done", "完成");

        assert!(log.chars().count() <= 12_000);
        assert!(log.ends_with("[done] 完成"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn activity_log_appends_scoped_lines() {
        let mut log = String::new();

        append_activity_log_line(&mut log, "ssh", "Testing connection");
        append_activity_log_line(&mut log, "sftp", "Loaded files");

        assert_eq!(log, "[ssh] Testing connection\n[sftp] Loaded files");
    }

    #[test]
    fn capped_log_line_preserves_unicode_boundaries() {
        let mut log = "界".repeat(10);

        append_capped_log_line(&mut log, "完成", 6);

        assert!(log.chars().count() <= 6);
        assert!(log.ends_with("完成"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn parse_port_rejects_zero_and_non_numbers() {
        assert_eq!(parse_port("2222", "Port"), Ok(2222));
        assert!(parse_port("0", "Port").is_err());
        assert!(parse_port("abc", "Port").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn panel_and_runtime_state_labels_are_stable() {
        assert_eq!(ForwardMode::Dynamic.label(), "dynamic SOCKS");
        assert_eq!(
            ForwardState::Stopped.label(ForwardMode::Remote),
            "remote stopped"
        );
        assert_eq!(ShellState::Disconnected.label(), "disconnected");
    }

    #[test]
    fn forward_event_format_is_stable() {
        assert_eq!(
            format_forward_event(&ForwardEvent::Listening {
                bind_host: "127.0.0.1".to_string(),
                bind_port: 8080
            }),
            "listening 127.0.0.1:8080"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn forward_config_can_represent_dynamic_socks() {
        let forward = ForwardConfig::Dynamic(DynamicForwardOptions {
            bind_host: "127.0.0.1".to_string(),
            bind_port: 1080,
        });

        match forward {
            ForwardConfig::Dynamic(options) => {
                assert_eq!(options.bind_host, "127.0.0.1");
                assert_eq!(options.bind_port, 1080);
            }
            ForwardConfig::Local(_) | ForwardConfig::Remote(_) => {
                panic!("expected dynamic forward")
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn forward_config_can_represent_remote_forward() {
        let forward = ForwardConfig::Remote(RemoteForwardOptions {
            bind_host: "0.0.0.0".to_string(),
            bind_port: 9000,
            local_host: "127.0.0.1".to_string(),
            local_port: 3000,
        });

        assert_eq!(
            forward_start_status(&forward),
            "Starting remote forward 0.0.0.0:9000 -> local 127.0.0.1:3000"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn ssh_issue_classifier_guides_auth_failures() {
        let issue = classify_ssh_issue("Connection test failed", "authentication failed");

        assert!(issue.title.contains("Authentication failed"));
        assert!(issue.guidance.contains("username"));
        assert!(issue.guidance.contains("auth method"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn ssh_issue_classifier_guides_network_failures() {
        let issue = classify_ssh_issue("Shell failed", "connection refused");

        assert!(issue.title.contains("Network or address problem"));
        assert!(issue.guidance.contains("host/IP"));
        assert!(issue.guidance.contains("firewall"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn ssh_issue_classifier_guides_forward_failures() {
        let issue = classify_ssh_issue("Forward failed", "address already in use");

        assert!(issue.title.contains("Forwarding problem"));
        assert!(issue.guidance.contains("bind port"));
        assert!(issue.guidance.contains("service target"));
    }

    }

    #[test]
    fn compact_status_label_limits_long_chip_text() {
        let short = compact_status_label("sftp list /tmp".to_string());
        let long =
            compact_status_label("sftp list /very/long/path/that/would/stretch/ui".to_string());

        assert_eq!(short, "sftp list /tmp");
        assert!(long.chars().count() <= 32);
        assert!(long.ends_with('…'));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_server_shortcut_exposes_only_non_secret_fields() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.fill_test_server();

        assert_eq!(TEST_SERVER_HOST, "82.157.57.178");
        assert_eq!(TEST_SERVER_USER, "ubuntu");
        assert_eq!(TEST_SERVER_PORT, "22");
        assert!(app.status.contains("Test server filled"));
        assert!(app.status.contains("sidebar"));
        assert!(!app.status.contains("Gyb"));
        assert!(app.issue.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn fill_test_server_does_not_store_password() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        app.fill_test_server();

        assert_eq!(app.host, TEST_SERVER_HOST);
        assert_eq!(app.user, TEST_SERVER_USER);
        assert_eq!(app.port, TEST_SERVER_PORT);
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert!(app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert!(app.password.is_empty());
        assert!(app.issue.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn smoke_status_is_opt_in_and_records_shell_state() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("openterm-smoke-status-{}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.smoke_status_path = Some(path.clone());
        app.host = "example.test".to_string();
        app.status = "Ready\nwith tab".to_string();

        app.record_smoke_status("probe");

        let contents = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(contents.contains("state=probe"));
        assert!(contents.contains("shell=disconnected"));
        assert!(contents.contains("panel=Terminal"));
        assert!(contents.contains("capture_keys=false"));
        assert!(contents.contains("connection_editor=false"));
        assert!(contents.contains("tab=1"));
        assert!(contents.contains("tabs=1"));
        assert!(contents.contains("hosts=0"));
        assert!(contents.contains("selected_host=none"));
        assert!(contents.contains("theme=dark"));
        assert!(contents.contains("font_size=14"));
        assert!(contents.contains("example.test:22"));
        assert!(contents.contains("Ready with tab"));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn default_known_hosts_can_be_isolated_for_gui_smoke() {
        let path =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("openterm-known-hosts-{}", std::process::id()));
        unsafe {
            std::env::set_var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_KNOWN_HOSTS", &path);
        }

        assert_eq!(default_known_hosts_path(), path);

        unsafe {
            std::env::remove_var("OPENTERM_UI_SMOKE_KNOWN_HOSTS");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn import_export_controls_are_collapsed_until_palette_action() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        assert!(!app.sidebar_tools_open);

        let _ = app.update(Message::CommandPaletteRun(
            PaletteAction::ToggleImportExport,
        ));
        assert!(app.sidebar_tools_open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn connection_fields_are_hidden_until_connection_editing_is_open() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        assert!(!app.connection_fields_visible());

        let _ = app.update(Message::NewConnection);
        assert!(app.connection_fields_visible());

        app.advanced_connection_open = false;
        assert!(!app.connection_fields_visible());

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleAdvancedConnection);
        assert!(app.connection_fields_visible());

        app.shell = ShellState::Connecting {
            route: test_connect_route(),
            size: EngineTerminalSize {
                cols: 100,
                rows: 30,
            },
        };
        assert!(!app.connection_fields_visible());

        app.shell = ShellState::Connected {
            input: closed_shell_sender(),
        };
        assert!(!app.connection_fields_visible());
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_host_can_be_reopened_for_editing_without_toggle_confusion() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-edit-flow");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.hosts.clear();
        app.selected = None;
        app.host_name = "prod".to_string();
        app.host = "10.0.0.10".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.port = "22".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);
        assert!(app.selected.is_some());
        app.advanced_connection_open = false;

        let _ = app.update(Message::OpenConnectionEditor);
        assert!(app.connection_fields_visible());
        assert_eq!(app.host_name, "prod");
        assert_eq!(app.host, "10.0.0.10");
        assert_eq!(app.user, "ubuntu");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn advanced_connection_sections_are_collapsed_by_default() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        assert!(!app.vault_details_open);
        assert!(!app.routing_details_open);
        assert!(!app.pty_details_open);

        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleVaultDetails);
        let _ = app.update(Message::ToggleRoutingDetails);

        assert!(app.vault_details_open);
        assert!(app.routing_details_open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn recovery_controls_open_for_common_connection_errors() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        let _ = app.handle_local_validation_error("Terminal", "Password is required.".to_string());
        assert!(app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);

        app.advanced_connection_open = false;
        let _ = app.handle_local_validation_error(
            "Terminal",
            "Terminal rows must be between 10 and 100.".to_string(),
        );
        assert!(app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert!(app.pty_details_open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn authentication_failure_opens_password_recovery_editor() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.host = TEST_SERVER_HOST.to_string();
        app.user = TEST_SERVER_USER.to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::AgentOrDefault;
        app.advanced_connection_open = false;

        let _ = app.handle_ssh_error(
            SshUiError::Message("authentication failed".to_string()),
            "Shell failed",
        );

        assert!(app.connection_fields_visible());
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert!(app.status.contains("Enter the password"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn issue_action_opens_connection_recovery_controls() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.issue = Some(classify_ssh_issue("Shell failed", "authentication failed"));
        app.advanced_connection_open = false;
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::AgentOrDefault;

        let _ = app.update(Message::OpenIssueDetails);

        assert!(app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert_eq!(app.status, "Opened recovery details.");
    }

    #[test]
    fn recovery_controls_open_tool_panels_for_tool_errors() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        let _ = app.handle_local_validation_error(
            "SFTP upload",
            "Local file path is required for upload.".to_string(),
        );
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Sftp);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);

        let _ = app.handle_local_validation_error(
            "Forward",
            "Bind port must be greater than zero.".to_string(),
        );
        assert!(app.workspace_tools_open);
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Forward);
        assert!(app.forward_tools_open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn hidden_tool_panels_stay_available_without_a_visible_function_rail() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp));
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Sftp);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Settings));
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Settings);
        assert!(app.workspace_tools_open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminal_and_sftp_are_primary_workspace_tabs_not_tool_panels() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp));
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Sftp);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
        assert!(!app.capture_keys);

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Terminal));
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
    }

    fn sftp_workspace_keeps_transfer_actions_contextual() {

        assert!(!sftp_top_actions().contains(&"Upload"));
        assert!(!sftp_top_actions().contains(&"Download"));
        assert!(!sftp_top_actions().contains(&"Remove"));

        assert_eq!(
            sftp_selection_actions(),
            &["Upload", "Download", "Open", "Mkdir", "Rename", "Remove"]
        );
    }

        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.fill_test_server();

    #[test]
    fn workspace_tabs_use_low_chrome_tab_styling() {
        let inactive = tab_button_style(TabTone::Inactive, button::Status::Active);
        assert_eq!(inactive.border.width, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(inactive.border.color, Color::TRANSPARENT);
        assert_eq!(inactive.shadow, Shadow::default());

        let active = tab_button_style(TabTone::Active, button::Status::Active);
        assert_eq!(active.border.width, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(active.text_color, TEXT_HIGH);
        assert_eq!(active.shadow, Shadow::default());
    }

    #[test]
    fn primary_workspace_tabs_live_in_session_header() {
        let mut app = empty_test_app("openterm-primary-tabs");

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp));
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Sftp);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
        assert_eq!(app.target_label(), "No target");

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Terminal));
        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Terminal);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
    }

        let _ = app.update(Message::PanelSelected(Panel::Sftp));

    #[test]
    fn sftp_loaded_closes_connection_editor_for_file_workspace() {
        let mut app = empty_test_app("openterm-sftp-loaded-editor");
        app.host = "203.0.113.10".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.panel = Panel::Sftp;
        app.advanced_connection_open = true;

        let _ = app.update(Message::FilesLoaded(Ok(vec![remote_test_entry(
            "alpha.txt",
            RemoteFileKind::File,
        )])));

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Sftp);
        assert!(!app.advanced_connection_open);
        assert!(!app.connection_fields_visible());
        assert_eq!(app.remote_files.len(), 1);
    }

    fn empty_terminal_primary_action_opens_new_host_editor() {

        assert!(app.host.is_empty());

        let _ = app.update(Message::NewConnection);

        assert!(app.connection_fields_visible());

    #[test]
    fn session_connect_requires_current_target() {
        let mut app = empty_test_app("openterm-session-target");
        assert_eq!(app.target_label(), "No target");
        assert!(!app.has_target());

        app.host = "10.0.0.50".to_string();

        assert!(app.has_target());
        assert_eq!(app.target_label(), format!("{}@10.0.0.50:22", app.user));
    }

    #[test]
    fn command_palette_opens_hidden_tool_panels() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.command_palette_open = true;
        app.command_palette_query = "sftp".to_string();

        let _ = app.update(Message::CommandPaletteRun(PaletteAction::OpenSftp));

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Sftp);
        assert!(!app.workspace_tools_open);
        assert!(!app.command_palette_open);
        assert!(app.command_palette_query.is_empty());

        app.command_palette_open = true;
        let _ = app.update(Message::CommandPaletteRun(PaletteAction::OpenActivity));

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Activity);
        assert!(app.workspace_tools_open);
    }

    fn auto_forward_smoke_env_selects_forward_panel_and_port() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();

        assert_eq!(app.panel, Panel::Forward);

    #[test]
    fn duplicate_connect_smoke_path_uses_second_tab_target() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.fill_test_server();

        let _ = app.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);

        assert_eq!(app.terminal_tabs.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(app.active_terminal_tab, 1);

    #[test]
    fn new_tab_reuses_successful_password_session_for_same_target() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.host = "82.157.57.178".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.port = "22".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "session-password".to_string();
        app.mark_connection_ok("shell connected");
        app.password.clear();

        let _ = app.update(Message::NewTerminalTab);

        assert_eq!(app.host, "82.157.57.178");
        assert_eq!(app.user, "ubuntu");
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert!(app.password.is_empty());
        assert!(matches!(
            app.auth_method().unwrap(),
            AuthMethod::Password(password) if password == "session-password"
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_password_host_connects_without_visible_password_field() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-vault-auth-method");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "saved".to_string();
        app.host = "82.157.57.178".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.port = "22".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "saved-password".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);
        app.password.clear();

        assert!(matches!(
            app.auth_method().unwrap(),
            AuthMethod::Password(password) if password == "saved-password"
        ));

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn app_load_selects_single_saved_host_for_direct_connect() {
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-single-host-load");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        let mut app = OpenTerm::load().0;
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "saved".to_string();
        app.host = "82.157.57.178".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.port = "22".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "saved-password".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        let loaded = load_from_test_store(&path);

        assert_eq!(loaded.selected, Some(0));
        assert_eq!(loaded.host, "82.157.57.178");
        assert_eq!(loaded.user, "ubuntu");
        assert!(matches!(
            loaded.auth_method().unwrap(),
            AuthMethod::Password(password) if password == "saved-password"
        ));

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn app_load_single_saved_host_can_duplicate_session() {
        let path = temp_test_db("openterm-single-host-duplicate");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        let mut app = OpenTerm::load().0;
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.host_name = "saved".to_string();
        app.host = "82.157.57.178".to_string();
        app.user = "ubuntu".to_string();
        app.port = "22".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "saved-password".to_string();
        let _ = app.update(Message::SaveHost);

        let mut loaded = load_from_test_store(&path);

        let _ = loaded.update(Message::DuplicateTerminalTab);

        assert_eq!(loaded.terminal_tabs.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(loaded.active_terminal_tab, 1);
        assert_eq!(loaded.host, "82.157.57.178");
        assert_eq!(loaded.user, "ubuntu");
        assert_eq!(loaded.auth_mode, AuthMode::Password);
        assert!(matches!(
            loaded.auth_method().unwrap(),
            AuthMethod::Password(password) if password == "saved-password"
        ));

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn new_connection_clears_selected_host_and_secret_fields() {
        let (mut app, _) = OpenTerm::load();
        app.selected = Some(0);
        app.pending_delete_host = true;
        app.pending_delete_remote = Some(RemoteFileEntry {
            name: "old.txt".to_string(),
            path: "/tmp/old.txt".to_string(),
            kind: RemoteFileKind::File,
            size: Some(3),
            permissions: None,
            modified: None,
        });
        app.host_name = "old".to_string();
        app.host = "192.0.2.10".to_string();
        app.user = "root".to_string();
        app.port = "2200".to_string();
        app.auth_mode = AuthMode::Password;
        app.password = "secret".to_string();
        app.passphrase = "phrase".to_string();
        app.remote_files.push(RemoteFileEntry {
            name: "remote".to_string(),
            path: "/remote".to_string(),
            kind: RemoteFileKind::Directory,
            size: None,
            permissions: None,
            modified: None,
        });

        app.start_new_connection();

        assert_eq!(app.selected, None);
        assert!(!app.pending_delete_host);
        assert!(app.pending_delete_remote.is_none());
        assert!(app.remote_files.is_empty());
        assert!(app.host_name.is_empty());
        assert!(app.host.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(app.port, "22");
        assert_eq!(app.auth_mode, AuthMode::AgentOrDefault);
        assert!(app.password.is_empty());
        assert!(app.passphrase.is_empty());
        assert!(app.advanced_connection_open);
    }

    fn closed_shell_sender() -> iced_mpsc::Sender<ShellInput> {
        let (sender, _receiver) = iced_mpsc::channel(1);
        sender
    }

    fn temp_test_db(prefix: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let nanos = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap()
            .as_nanos();
        std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("{prefix}-{}-{nanos}.redb", std::process::id()))
    }

    fn load_with_db_path(path: &PathBuf) -> (OpenTerm, Task<Message>) {
        static DB_ENV_LOCK: std::sync::Mutex<()> = std::sync::Mutex::new(());
        let _guard = DB_ENV_LOCK.lock().expect("db env lock");
        let previous = std::env::var_os("OPENTERM_DB_PATH");
        std::env::set_var("OPENTERM_DB_PATH", path);
        let loaded = OpenTerm::load();
        if let Some(previous) = previous {
            std::env::set_var("OPENTERM_DB_PATH", previous);
        } else {
            std::env::remove_var("OPENTERM_DB_PATH");
        }
        loaded
    }

    fn load_from_test_store(path: &PathBuf) -> OpenTerm {
        let mut app = OpenTerm::load().0;
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.hosts = WorkspaceStore::open(path)
            .unwrap()
            .list_hosts()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        if app.hosts.len() == 1 {
            app.select_host(0);
        }
        app
    }

    fn empty_test_app(prefix: &str) -> OpenTerm {
        let path = temp_test_db(prefix);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        let mut app = load_with_db_path(&path).0;
        app.db_path = path.clone();
        app.hosts.clear();
        app.selected = None;
        app.host.clear();
        app.host_name.clear();
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
        app
    }

    fn test_connect_route() -> ConnectRoute {
        ConnectRoute {
            target: HostProfile::new("test", "example.test"),
            target_options: ConnectOptions {
                username: "tester".to_string(),
                auth: AuthMethod::AgentOrDefault,
                trust_unknown_host_keys: false,
                host_key_policy: HostKeyPolicy::TrustAll,
                timeout: Duration::from_secs(1),
            },
            jump: None,
        }
    }
}
